
if not (function()

    local KEYS = { "LedZKey" }
    local DISCORD_CODE = "T8R9K2vb9Y"
    local TITLE = "LedZ Hub"
    local KEY_FILE = "LedZHub/key.txt"
    local INTRO_ONCE = false

    if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
    local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    while not LocalPlayer do
        task.wait()
        LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    end
    while not workspace.CurrentCamera do task.wait() end


    local G = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G
    local waitStart = os.clock()
    while G.__LedZKeyOpen and os.clock() - waitStart < 90 do task.wait(0.1) end
    if INTRO_ONCE and G.__LedZKeyPassed then return true end

    local KEY_LINK = "https://discord.gg/" .. DISCORD_CODE
    local PAL = {
        bg     = Color3.fromRGB(11, 11, 12),
        panel  = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 26),
        line   = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 54),
        text   = Color3.fromRGB(226, 226, 229),
        sub    = Color3.fromRGB(130, 131, 137),
        blue   = Color3.fromRGB(78, 108, 140),
        blueHi = Color3.fromRGB(124, 148, 174),
        blueLo = Color3.fromRGB(48, 68, 92),
        red    = Color3.fromRGB(142, 62, 62),
        redHi  = Color3.fromRGB(178, 106, 106),
        redLo  = Color3.fromRGB(92, 38, 40),
        good   = Color3.fromRGB(98, 170, 126),
        bad    = Color3.fromRGB(190, 92, 92),
    }
    local LOGO_TEXT = ('<font color="#%s">L</font><font color="#%s">H</font>'):format(PAL.blue:ToHex(), PAL.red:ToHex())

    local function new(class, props, children)
        local o = Instance.new(class)
        for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do o[k] = v end
        for _, ch in ipairs(children or {}) do ch.Parent = o end
        return o
    end
    local function round(r) return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r) }) end
    local function tw(obj, t, props, style, dir)
        local tween = TweenService:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(t, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
        tween:Play()
        return tween
    end
    local function splitBar(props)
        local bar = new("Frame", props)
        bar.BackgroundColor3 = PAL.blue
        bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local redHalf = new("Frame", {
            Parent = bar, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 1),
            BackgroundColor3 = PAL.red, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = bar.ZIndex,
        })
        return bar, redHalf
    end
    local function guiParent(gui)
        local ok = pcall(function() gui.Parent = (type(gethui) == "function" and gethui()) or CoreGui end)
        if not ok or not gui.Parent then gui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
    end

    local function normKey(k) return (tostring(k or ""):gsub("%s+", "")):upper() end
    local function keyValid(k)
        local n = normKey(k)
        if n == "" then return false end
        for _, v in ipairs(KEYS) do
            if normKey(v) == n then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function readSavedKey()
        if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(readfile) ~= "function" then return nil end
        local ok, data = pcall(function() return isfile(KEY_FILE) and readfile(KEY_FILE) or nil end)
        return ok and data or nil
    end
    local function saveKey(k)
        if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
        pcall(function()
            local folder = KEY_FILE:match("^(.+)/[^/]+$")
            if folder and type(isfolder) == "function" and type(makefolder) == "function" and not isfolder(folder) then
                makefolder(folder)
            end
            writefile(KEY_FILE, k)
        end)
    end
    local function copyLink()
        local fn = setclipboard or toclipboard
        if type(fn) == "function" then return (pcall(fn, KEY_LINK)) end
        return false
    end
    local function openInvite()
        local req = http_request or request
        if type(req) ~= "function" then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(req, {
                Url = "http://127.0.0.1:6463/rpc?v=1",
                Method = "POST",
                Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json", Origin = "https://discord.com" },
                Body = HttpService:JSONEncode({ cmd = "INVITE_BROWSER", args = { code = DISCORD_CODE }, nonce = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false) }),
            })
        end)
    end

    local KS = {}
    local function run()
        local autoPass = keyValid(readSavedKey())
        local vp = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
        local SC = math.clamp(math.min(vp.X / 1280, vp.Y / 800), 0.6, 1.15)

        local gui = new("ScreenGui", {
            Name = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false):sub(1, 8), IgnoreGuiInset = true, ResetOnSpawn = false,
            DisplayOrder = 1000, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
        })
        guiParent(gui)
        local blur = new("BlurEffect", { Name = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false):sub(1, 6), Size = 0, Parent = Lighting })
        local conns = {}
        KS.gui, KS.blur, KS.conns = gui, blur, conns

        local dim = new("Frame", {
            Parent = gui, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5, 5, 6),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        })
        local stage = new("Frame", { Parent = gui, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
        local flash = new("Frame", {
            Parent = gui, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 20,
        })
        local LOGO_POS = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.45)
        local logo = new("Frame", { Parent = stage, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = LOGO_POS, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
        local logoScale = new("UIScale", { Parent = logo })

        local LS = 1.8 * SC
        local BW, BH = 92, 112
        local LH = BH * LS
        local function bigLetter(ch, base, hi, lo)
            local holder = new("Frame", { Parent = logo, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(BW, BH), BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
            new("UIScale", { Parent = holder, Scale = LS })
            local glow = new("TextLabel", {
                Parent = holder, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = ch, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack, TextSize = 100,
                TextColor3 = base, TextTransparency = 0.9, ZIndex = 1,
            }, { new("UIScale", { Scale = 1.12 }) })
            local main = new("TextLabel", {
                Parent = holder, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = ch,
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack, TextSize = 100, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), ZIndex = 2,
            })
            new("UIGradient", { Parent = main, Color = ColorSequence.new(hi, lo), Rotation = 90 })
            new("UIStroke", { Parent = main, Thickness = 2, Color = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 9), Transparency = 0.2 })
            return holder, main, glow
        end
        local L, Lmain, Lglow = bigLetter("L", PAL.blue, PAL.blueHi, PAL.blueLo)
        local Hh, Hmain, Hglow = bigLetter("H", PAL.red, PAL.redHi, PAL.redLo)
        L.Position, L.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(-230 * SC, -vp.Y * 0.8), -28
        Hh.Position, Hh.Rotation = UDim2.fromOffset(230 * SC, -vp.Y * 0.8), 28

        local function trail(holder, main, color)
            local on = true
            task.spawn(function()
                while on and gui.Parent do
                    local ghost = new("TextLabel", {
                        Parent = logo, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = holder.Size, Position = holder.Position,
                        Rotation = holder.Rotation, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = main.Text, Font = main.Font,
                        TextSize = 100, TextColor3 = color, TextTransparency = 0.55, ZIndex = 0,
                    })
                    new("UIScale", { Parent = ghost, Scale = LS })
                    tw(ghost, 0.28, { TextTransparency = 1 })
                    Debris:AddItem(ghost, 0.3)
                    task.wait(0.03)
                end
            end)
            return function() on = false end
        end
        local function shake(power, dur)
            task.spawn(function()
                local t0 = os.clock()
                while os.clock() - t0 < dur and gui.Parent do
                    local k = 1 - (os.clock() - t0) / dur
                    stage.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((math.random() * 2 - 1) * power * SC * k, (math.random() * 2 - 1) * power * SC * k)
                    task.wait(0.016)
                end
                stage.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
            end)
        end
        local function sparks(n, at, power)
            for i = 1, n do
                local ang = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                local dist = (90 + math.random() * 170) * SC * power
                local len = (6 + math.random() * 12) * SC
                local p = new("Frame", {
                    Parent = stage, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = at, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len, 3 * SC),
                    Rotation = math.deg(ang), BackgroundColor3 = ({ PAL.blueHi, PAL.redHi, PAL.sub })[i % 3 + 1], BorderSizePixel = 0,
                }, { round(2) })
                tw(p, 0.45 + math.random() * 0.3, {
                    Position = at + UDim2.fromOffset(math.cos(ang) * dist, math.sin(ang) * dist),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len * 0.3, 2 * SC),
                }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
                Debris:AddItem(p, 0.8)
            end
        end
        local function shockwave(at, color, delay)
            task.delay(delay or 0, function()
                if not gui.Parent then return end
                local ring = new("Frame", {
                    Parent = stage, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = at,
                    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20 * SC, 20 * SC), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                }, { new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
                local st = new("UIStroke", { Parent = ring, Color = color, Thickness = 4 * SC })
                tw(ring, 0.6, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(560 * SC, 560 * SC) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
                tw(st, 0.6, { Transparency = 1, Thickness = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
                Debris:AddItem(ring, 0.65)
            end)
        end
        local function land(holder)
            local us = holder:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
            if not us then return end
            us.Scale = LS * 1.15
            tw(us, 0.45, { Scale = LS }, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic)
        end


        tw(dim, 0.4, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.25 })
        tw(blur, 0.6, { Size = 22 })
        task.wait(0.25)
        local spread, gap = 190 * SC, 52 * SC
        local stopL = trail(L, Lmain, PAL.blue)
        tw(L, 0.5, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-spread, 0), Rotation = -6 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        task.wait(0.12)
        local stopH = trail(Hh, Hmain, PAL.red)
        tw(Hh, 0.5, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(spread, 0), Rotation = 6 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        task.wait(0.38)
        stopL()
        land(L)
        shake(6, 0.18)
        sparks(10, LOGO_POS + UDim2.fromOffset(-spread, LH * 0.32), 0.5)
        task.wait(0.12)
        stopH()
        land(Hh)
        shake(6, 0.18)
        sparks(10, LOGO_POS + UDim2.fromOffset(spread, LH * 0.32), 0.5)
        task.wait(0.2)
        tw(L, 0.3, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-gap, 0), Rotation = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        tw(Hh, 0.3, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(gap, 0), Rotation = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        task.wait(0.3)
        flash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.86
        tw(flash, 0.35, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
        shockwave(LOGO_POS, PAL.blueHi, 0)
        shockwave(LOGO_POS, PAL.sub, 0.08)
        sparks(30, LOGO_POS, 1)
        shake(14, 0.3)
        logoScale.Scale = 1.16
        tw(logoScale, 0.45, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
        for _, g in ipairs({ Lglow, Hglow }) do
            TweenService:Create(g, TweenInfo.new(1.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), { TextTransparency = 0.75 }):Play()
        end

        local title = new("TextLabel", {
            Parent = logo, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, LH * 0.38 + 24 * SC),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(320 * SC, 34 * SC), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = TITLE,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextScaled = true, TextColor3 = PAL.text, TextTransparency = 1,
        })
        local bar, barRed = splitBar({
            Parent = logo, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, LH * 0.38 + 64 * SC),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 3 * SC),
        })
        tw(title, 0.4, { TextTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, LH * 0.38 + 12 * SC) })
        tw(bar, 0.5, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170 * SC, 3 * SC) })
        task.wait(0.6)

        local function cleanup()
            for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
            pcall(function() blur:Destroy() end)
        end
        local function fadeLogo(t)
            for _, o in ipairs({ Lmain, Hmain, Lglow, Hglow, title }) do tw(o, t, { TextTransparency = 1 }) end
            for _, h in ipairs({ Lmain, Hmain }) do
                local st = h:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if st then tw(st, t, { Transparency = 1 }) end
            end
            tw(bar, t, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
            tw(barRed, t, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
        end

        if autoPass then
            title.Text = "Welcome back"
            task.wait(0.7)
            fadeLogo(0.35)
            tw(logoScale, 0.35, { Scale = 0.8 })
            tw(dim, 0.4, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
            tw(blur, 0.4, { Size = 0 })
            task.wait(0.42)
            cleanup()
            return true
        end


        tw(logo, 0.55, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.22) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
        tw(logoScale, 0.55, { Scale = 0.55 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)

        local fade = {}
        local function reg(obj, props)
            fade[#fade + 1] = { obj = obj, props = props }
            for k in pairs(props) do obj[k] = 1 end
            return obj
        end
        local card = reg(new("Frame", {
            Parent = gui, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.6),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(380 * SC, 252 * SC), BackgroundColor3 = PAL.bg, ZIndex = 5,
        }, { round(16 * SC) }), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.04 })
        reg(new("UIStroke", { Parent = card, Thickness = 1.2, Color = PAL.line }), { Transparency = 0 })
        local strip, stripRed = splitBar({
            Parent = card, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18 * SC, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -36 * SC, 0, 2 * SC), ZIndex = 6,
        })
        reg(strip, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
        reg(stripRed, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
        local cardScale = new("UIScale", { Parent = card, Scale = 0.88 })

        local function label(props)
            local base = {
                Parent = card, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextColor3 = PAL.text,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 6,
            }
            for k, v in pairs(props) do base[k] = v end
            return reg(new("TextLabel", base), { TextTransparency = 0 })
        end
        label({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20 * SC, 16 * SC), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44 * SC, 34 * SC), Text = LOGO_TEXT, RichText = true, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack, TextSize = 26 * SC })
        label({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(68 * SC, 15 * SC), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240 * SC, 20 * SC), Text = "Key System", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 18 * SC })
        label({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(68 * SC, 36 * SC), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(260 * SC, 16 * SC), Text = "Get a free key from our Discord", TextSize = 12 * SC, TextColor3 = PAL.sub })

        local close = reg(new("TextButton", {
            Parent = card, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14 * SC, 0, 14 * SC),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26 * SC, 26 * SC), BackgroundColor3 = PAL.panel, AutoButtonColor = false,
            Text = "X", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 12 * SC, TextColor3 = PAL.sub, ZIndex = 6,
        }, { round(8 * SC) }), { BackgroundTransparency = 0, TextTransparency = 0 })

        local boxFrame = reg(new("Frame", {
            Parent = card, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20 * SC, 72 * SC), Size = UDim2.new(1, -40 * SC, 0, 44 * SC),
            BackgroundColor3 = PAL.panel, ZIndex = 6,
        }, { round(10 * SC) }), { BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
        local boxStroke = reg(new("UIStroke", { Parent = boxFrame, Color = PAL.line, Thickness = 1.2 }), { Transparency = 0 })
        local box = reg(new("TextBox", {
            Parent = boxFrame, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14 * SC, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28 * SC, 1, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Paste your key here", PlaceholderColor3 = PAL.sub,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextSize = 15 * SC, TextColor3 = PAL.text, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 7,
        }), { TextTransparency = 0 })

        local status = label({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20 * SC, 122 * SC), Size = UDim2.new(1, -40 * SC, 0, 18 * SC), Text = "", TextSize = 12 * SC, TextColor3 = PAL.sub })

        local function kbutton(x, text, primary)
            local b = reg(new("TextButton", {
                Parent = card, Position = UDim2.new(x, x == 0 and 20 * SC or 6 * SC, 0, 148 * SC),
                Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -26 * SC, 0, 44 * SC), BackgroundColor3 = primary and PAL.blue or PAL.panel,
                AutoButtonColor = false, Text = "", ZIndex = 6,
            }, { round(10 * SC) }), { BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
            if not primary then
                reg(new("UIStroke", { Parent = b, Color = PAL.line, Thickness = 1.2 }), { Transparency = 0 })
            end
            local t = reg(new("TextLabel", {
                Parent = b, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text,
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 14 * SC, TextColor3 = PAL.text, ZIndex = 7,
            }), { TextTransparency = 0 })
            local sc = new("UIScale", { Parent = b })
            b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tw(sc, 0.12, { Scale = 1.03 }) end)
            b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tw(sc, 0.12, { Scale = 1 }) end)
            b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tw(sc, 0.08, { Scale = 0.95 }) end)
            b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tw(sc, 0.12, { Scale = 1 }) end)
            return b, t
        end
        local getBtn = kbutton(0, "Get Key", false)
        local verifyBtn, verifyText = kbutton(0.5, "Verify", true)

        local remember = true
        local check = reg(new("TextButton", {
            Parent = card, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20 * SC, 210 * SC), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18 * SC, 18 * SC),
            BackgroundColor3 = PAL.panel, AutoButtonColor = false, Text = "", ZIndex = 6,
        }, { round(5 * SC) }), { BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
        reg(new("UIStroke", { Parent = check, Color = PAL.line, Thickness = 1.2 }), { Transparency = 0 })
        local tick = reg(new("Frame", {
            Parent = check, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10 * SC, 10 * SC), BackgroundColor3 = PAL.blue, ZIndex = 7,
        }, { round(3 * SC) }), { BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
        label({ Position = UDim2.fromOffset(44 * SC, 210 * SC), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(120 * SC, 18 * SC), Text = "Remember key", TextSize = 12 * SC, TextColor3 = PAL.sub })
        local link = reg(new("TextButton", {
            Parent = card, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -20 * SC, 0, 210 * SC),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170 * SC, 18 * SC), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "discord.gg/" .. DISCORD_CODE,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextSize = 12 * SC, TextColor3 = PAL.blueHi, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, ZIndex = 6,
        }), { TextTransparency = 0 })

        task.wait(0.15)
        for _, f in ipairs(fade) do tw(f.obj, 0.35, f.props) end
        tw(cardScale, 0.45, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)

        local result = nil
        local busy = false
        local function setStatus(msg, color)
            status.Text = msg
            status.TextColor3 = color or PAL.sub
        end
        local function shakeCard()
            local base = card.Position
            for _, dx in ipairs({ -12, 10, -8, 6, -3, 0 }) do
                card.Position = base + UDim2.fromOffset(dx * SC, 0)
                task.wait(0.035)
            end
            card.Position = base
        end
        local function verify()
            if busy then return end
            if keyValid(box.Text) then
                busy = true
                setStatus("Key accepted", PAL.good)
                verifyText.Text = "Welcome!"
                tw(boxStroke, 0.2, { Color = PAL.good })
                if remember then saveKey(normKey(box.Text)) end
                result = true
            else
                setStatus(normKey(box.Text) == "" and "Type your key first" or "Wrong key, try again", PAL.bad)
                tw(boxStroke, 0.15, { Color = PAL.bad })
                task.spawn(shakeCard)
            end
        end
        box.Focused:Connect(function()
            if not busy then tw(boxStroke, 0.15, { Color = PAL.blue }) end
        end)
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
            if busy then return end
            tw(boxStroke, 0.15, { Color = PAL.line })
            if enter then verify() end
        end)
        verifyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(verify)
        local function getKey()
            local copied = copyLink()
            openInvite()
            setStatus(copied and "Discord link copied" or KEY_LINK, PAL.blueHi)
        end
        getBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(getKey)
        link.MouseButton1Click:Connect(getKey)
        check.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            remember = not remember
            tw(tick, 0.12, { BackgroundTransparency = remember and 0 or 1 })
        end)
        close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if not busy then result = false end
        end)

        while result == nil and gui.Parent do task.wait(0.05) end
        local ok = result == true
        if ok then task.wait(0.5) end

        for _, f in ipairs(fade) do
            local p = {}
            for k in pairs(f.props) do p[k] = 1 end
            tw(f.obj, 0.3, p)
        end
        tw(cardScale, 0.3, { Scale = 0.9 })
        fadeLogo(0.3)
        tw(dim, 0.4, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
        tw(blur, 0.4, { Size = 0 })
        task.wait(0.42)
        cleanup()
        return ok
    end

    G.__LedZKeyOpen = true
    local ok, passed = pcall(run)
    G.__LedZKeyOpen = false
    if not ok then
        warn("[LedZ] key screen error: " .. tostring(passed))
        for _, c in ipairs(KS.conns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        pcall(function() if KS.gui then KS.gui:Destroy() end end)
        pcall(function() if KS.blur then KS.blur:Destroy() end end)
        passed = keyValid(readSavedKey())
    end
    if passed then G.__LedZKeyPassed = true end
    return passed == true
end)() then return end

local Players           = game:GetService("Players")

local Drawing = (function()
    local native = Drawing
    if native and pcall(function() local t = native.new("Text") t:Remove() end) then return native end
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "LZ_Draw"; gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true; gui.ResetOnSpawn = false; gui.DisplayOrder = 40
    pcall(function() gui.Parent = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    if not gui.Parent then gui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
    local function new(kind)
        local o = { Visible = false, Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Transparency = 1, Thickness = 1, ZIndex = 1 }
        local inst, stroke
        if kind == "Text" then
            inst = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            inst.BackgroundTransparency = 1; inst.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY; inst.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
            inst.Font = Enum.Font.Code
            o.Text, o.Size, o.Center, o.Outline, o.Position, o.Font = "", 13, false, false, Vector2.zero, 2
        elseif kind == "Square" then
            inst = Instance.new("Frame"); inst.BorderSizePixel = 0
            stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke"); stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border; stroke.Parent = inst
            o.Size, o.Position, o.Filled = Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, false
        elseif kind == "Line" then
            inst = Instance.new("Frame"); inst.BorderSizePixel = 0; inst.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            o.From, o.To = Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero
        elseif kind == "Triangle" then
            inst = Instance.new("TextLabel"); inst.BackgroundTransparency = 1; inst.Text = "\226\150\178"; inst.TextScaled = true
            inst.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            o.PointA, o.PointB, o.PointC, o.Filled = Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, true
        else
            inst = Instance.new("Frame"); inst.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        end
        inst.Visible = false
        inst.Parent = gui
        local function render()
            local alpha = 1 - (o.Transparency or 1)
            inst.Visible = o.Visible and true or false
            inst.ZIndex = o.ZIndex or 1
            if kind == "Text" then
                inst.Text = tostring(o.Text); inst.TextSize = o.Size; inst.TextColor3 = o.Color; inst.TextTransparency = alpha
                inst.TextStrokeTransparency = o.Outline and alpha or 1
                inst.AnchorPoint = o.Center and Vector2.new(0.5, 0) or Vector2.zero
                inst.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(o.Position.X, o.Position.Y)
            elseif kind == "Square" then
                inst.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(o.Position.X, o.Position.Y); inst.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(o.Size.X, o.Size.Y)
                inst.BackgroundColor3 = o.Color; inst.BackgroundTransparency = o.Filled and alpha or 1
                stroke.Color = o.Color; stroke.Thickness = o.Thickness; stroke.Transparency = o.Filled and 1 or alpha
            elseif kind == "Line" then
                local a, b = o.From, o.To
                local d = b - a
                inst.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((a.X + b.X) / 2, (a.Y + b.Y) / 2); inst.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.Magnitude, o.Thickness)
                inst.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X)); inst.BackgroundColor3 = o.Color; inst.BackgroundTransparency = alpha
            elseif kind == "Triangle" then
                local c = (o.PointA + o.PointB + o.PointC) / 3
                local dir = o.PointA - c
                local s = math.max((o.PointB - o.PointC).Magnitude, 8)
                inst.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c.X, c.Y); inst.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s, s)
                inst.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dir.Y, dir.X)) + 90; inst.TextColor3 = o.Color; inst.TextTransparency = alpha
            end
        end
        local proxy = {}
        function proxy:Remove() inst:Destroy() end
        proxy.Destroy = proxy.Remove
        return setmetatable(proxy, {
            __index = function(_, k) return o[k] end,
            __newindex = function(_, k, v) o[k] = v if inst.Parent then render() end end,
        })
    end
    return { new = new, Fonts = { UI = 0, System = 1, Plex = 2, Monospace = 3 } }
end)()

local lastJumpReq = 0
local jumpReqConn = UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function() lastJumpReq = os.clock() end)
local function flyMove()
    local cf = Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame
    local move = Vector3.zero
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then move = move + cf.LookVector end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then move = move - cf.LookVector end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then move = move - cf.RightVector end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then move = move + cf.RightVector end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then move = move + Vector3.yAxis end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then move = move - Vector3.yAxis end
    if move.Magnitude < 0.01 then
        local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local md = hum and hum.MoveDirection or Vector3.zero
        if md.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            local fl = Vector3.new(cf.LookVector.X, 0, cf.LookVector.Z)
            local fr = Vector3.new(cf.RightVector.X, 0, cf.RightVector.Z)
            fl = fl.Magnitude > 0.01 and fl.Unit or Vector3.zero
            fr = fr.Magnitude > 0.01 and fr.Unit or Vector3.zero
            move = cf.LookVector * md:Dot(fl) + cf.RightVector * md:Dot(fr)
        end
        if os.clock() - lastJumpReq < 0.25 or (hum and hum.Jump) then move = move + Vector3.yAxis end
    end
    return move
end

local function findPath(root, ...)
    local node = root
    for _, n in ipairs({ ... }) do
        if not node then return nil end
        node = node:FindFirstChild(n)
    end
    return node
end

local Maid = {}
Maid.__index = Maid
Maid.sliceT = os.clock()
function Maid.Slice() if os.clock() - Maid.sliceT > 0.008 then task.wait() Maid.sliceT = os.clock() end end
function Maid.new() return setmetatable({ _t = {} }, Maid) end
function Maid:Give(t) self._t[#self._t + 1] = t return t end
function Maid:Clean()
    for i = #self._t, 1, -1 do
        local t, ty = self._t[i], typeof(self._t[i])
        if ty == "RBXScriptConnection" then t:Disconnect()
        elseif ty == "Instance" then t:Destroy()
        elseif ty == "function" then pcall(t) end
        self._t[i] = nil
    end
end

local Char = {}
function Char.Get() return LocalPlayer.Character end
function Char.HRP() local c = LocalPlayer.Character return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end
function Char.Hum() local c = LocalPlayer.Character return c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") end
function Char.Val(n) local c = LocalPlayer.Character return c and c:FindFirstChild(n) end
function Char.PvpVal(n) local c = LocalPlayer.Character local p = c and c:FindFirstChild("Pvp") return p and p:FindFirstChild(n) end

local AutoMove = { dir = nil }

local CharPartCache = { char=nil, list={}, dirty=true, conns={} }
local function charBaseParts()
    local c = LocalPlayer.Character
    if c ~= CharPartCache.char then
        for _, x in ipairs(CharPartCache.conns) do x:Disconnect() end
        CharPartCache.conns, CharPartCache.char, CharPartCache.dirty = {}, c, true
        if c then
            local function mark() CharPartCache.dirty = true end
            CharPartCache.conns = { c.DescendantAdded:Connect(mark), c.DescendantRemoving:Connect(mark) }
        end
    end
    if CharPartCache.dirty then
        CharPartCache.dirty = false
        local l = {}
        if c then for _, p in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do if p:IsA("BasePart") then l[#l+1] = p end end end
        CharPartCache.list = l
    end
    return CharPartCache.list
end

local function valNoise(x)
    local i = math.floor(x); local f = x - i
    local a = math.sin(i*12.9898+78.233)*43758.5453;     a = a - math.floor(a)
    local b = math.sin((i+1)*12.9898+78.233)*43758.5453; b = b - math.floor(b)
    local s = f*f*(3-2*f); return a + (b-a)*s
end
local function rotOnly(cf) return cf - cf.Position end

local Speed = { Enabled=false, Mult=1.5, Jitter=0.06, Time=0, Maid=Maid.new() }
local Gov = { targets = {}, extra = 0 }
Gov.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum, wsv = Gov.cHum, Gov.cWsv
    if Gov.cChar ~= char or not (hum and hum.Parent == char and wsv and wsv.Parent == char) then
        hum, wsv = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"), char:FindFirstChild("WalkSpeed")
        if wsv and not wsv:IsA("NumberValue") then wsv = nil end
        Gov.cChar, Gov.cHum, Gov.cWsv = char, hum, wsv
    end
    if not (hum and wsv) or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    if Gov.char ~= char then Gov.char, Gov.baseVal, Gov.lastVal, Gov.ratio = char, wsv.Value, nil, nil end
    if Gov.lastVal == nil or math.abs(wsv.Value - Gov.lastVal) > 1e-3 then
        Gov.baseVal = wsv.Value
        if Gov.lastVal == nil and wsv.Value > 0 and hum.WalkSpeed > 0 then Gov.ratio = hum.WalkSpeed / wsv.Value end
    end
    local ratio = Gov.ratio or 1
    local mult = 1
    if Speed.Enabled then
        Speed.Time = Speed.Time + dt
        mult = Speed.Mult * (1 + (valNoise(Speed.Time * 2.3) - 0.5) * Speed.Jitter)
    end
    local want = Gov.baseVal * mult * ratio
    for _, t in pairs(Gov.targets) do if t > want then want = t end end
    want = want + (Gov.extra or 0)
    local val = want / ratio
    if math.abs(val - Gov.baseVal) < 0.02 then
        if Gov.lastVal then wsv.Value = Gov.baseVal end
        Gov.lastVal = nil
    elseif not Gov.lastVal or math.abs(val - Gov.lastVal) > 0.02 then
        wsv.Value = val
        Gov.lastVal = val
    end
end)

function Speed.Set(on)
    Speed.Enabled=on; Speed.Time=0
end

local NoSlow = { Enabled=false, Maid=Maid.new() }
function NoSlow.Set(on)
    NoSlow.Enabled=on; NoSlow.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then return end
    NoSlow.Maid:Give(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not NoSlow.Enabled then return end
        local c=Char.Get(); if not c then return end
        local s=c:FindFirstChild("Stationary"); if s and s.Value then s.Value=false end
        local cr=c:FindFirstChild("Crouching"); if cr and cr.Value then cr.Value=false end
        for _,n in ipairs({"Knocked","Aiming"}) do local v=Char.PvpVal(n) if v and v.Value then v.Value=false end end
    end))
end

function InfJump.Set(on)
    InfJump.Enabled=on; InfJump.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then return end
    InfJump.Maid:Give(UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
        if not InfJump.Enabled then return end

        if h then pcall(function() h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping,true) end) h:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
    end))
end

local FLY_CAP = 60
local Fly = { Enabled=false, Speed=30, Jitter=0.15, Time=0, Maid=Maid.new() }
function Fly.Start()
    Fly.Maid:Clean()
    local hum=Char.Hum(); if not hum then return end
    hum.PlatformStand=true; Fly.Time=0
    Fly.Maid:Give(function()
        local h=Char.Hum() if h then h.PlatformStand=false pcall(function() h:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end) end
        local r=Char.HRP() if r then r.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero end
    end)
    Fly.Maid:Give(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        local r,h=Char.HRP(),Char.Hum(); if not r or not h then return end
        h.PlatformStand=true; Fly.Time=Fly.Time+dt
        local cf=Camera.CFrame; local move=flyMove()
        r.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
        if move.Magnitude>0 then
            local spd=math.min(Fly.Speed,FLY_CAP)
            local wob=1+(valNoise(Fly.Time*2.4)-0.5)*Fly.Jitter
            r.CFrame=r.CFrame+move.Unit*spd*wob*dt
        end
    end))
end
function Fly.Stop() Fly.Maid:Clean() end

local savedCollide = {}
local function snapshotCollision()
    if next(savedCollide) then return end
    local c=Char.Get(); if not c then return end
    for _,p in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do if p:IsA("BasePart") then savedCollide[p]=p.CanCollide end end
end
local function restoreCollision()
    for p,v in pairs(savedCollide) do if p and p.Parent then pcall(function() p.CanCollide=v end) end end
    savedCollide={}
end
local Noclip = { Enabled=false, Maid=Maid.new() }
function Noclip.Set(on)
    Noclip.Enabled=on; Noclip.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then restoreCollision() return end
    snapshotCollision()
    Noclip.Maid:Give(RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        local c=Char.Get(); if not c then return end
        for _,p in ipairs(charBaseParts()) do if p.CanCollide then p.CanCollide=false end end
    end))
end

local Ghost = { Enabled=false, Speed=30, Stealth=2, Vel=Vector3.zero, Time=0, Noise=math.random()*1000, Pause=0, Tired=0, Maid=Maid.new() }
local function variation(t) return 1+math.sin(t*7.8)*0.018+math.cos(t*3.9+1.2)*0.022+math.sin(t*1.1)*0.03+valNoise(t*2.3+Ghost.Noise)*0.015 end
local function damp(c,tg,s) s=math.clamp(s,0,1) s=s*s*(3-2*s) return c+(tg-c)*s end
local function ghostStep(root,hum,dt)
    Ghost.Time=Ghost.Time+dt
    local dir=hum.MoveDirection; if dir.Magnitude<0.01 and AutoMove.dir then dir=AutoMove.dir end
    if dir.Magnitude>0.01 then
        Ghost.Tired=Ghost.Tired+dt*0.008
        local tired=1-math.sin(Ghost.Tired)*0.035
        local mult=({[4]=0.78,[3]=0.87,[2]=0.94})[Ghost.Stealth] or 1
        local target=Ghost.Speed*variation(Ghost.Time)*tired*mult
        Ghost.Pause=Ghost.Pause+dt
        if Ghost.Pause>RNG:NextNumber(5,10) then Ghost.Pause=0 if RNG:NextNumber()<0.12 then target=target*0.25 end end
        local smooth=0.1+Ghost.Stealth*0.015
        Ghost.Cur=damp(Ghost.Cur or hum.WalkSpeed,target,smooth)
        Gov.targets.ghost=Ghost.Cur
    else
        Ghost.Cur=nil
        Gov.targets.ghost=nil
    end
end
function Ghost.Set(on)
    Ghost.Enabled=on; Ghost.Maid:Clean(); Gov.targets.ghost=nil; Ghost.Cur=nil
    if not on then return end
    Ghost.Time,Ghost.Pause,Ghost.Tired=0,0,0; Ghost.Noise=math.random()*1000
    Ghost.Maid:Give(function() Gov.targets.ghost=nil end)
    Ghost.Maid:Give(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        local r,h=Char.HRP(),Char.Hum(); if not r or not h or h.Health<=0 then Gov.targets.ghost=nil return end
        ghostStep(r,h,dt)
    end))
end

local CFSpeed = { Enabled=false, Speed=24, Jitter=0.18, Time=0, Noise=math.random()*1000, Pause=0, Maid=Maid.new() }
function CFSpeed.Set(on)
    CFSpeed.Enabled=on; CFSpeed.Maid:Clean(); Gov.targets.cf=nil
    if not on then return end
    CFSpeed.Time,CFSpeed.Pause=0,0; CFSpeed.Noise=math.random()*1000
    CFSpeed.Maid:Give(function() Gov.targets.cf=nil end)
    CFSpeed.Maid:Give(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        CFSpeed.Time=CFSpeed.Time+dt
        local wob=1+math.sin(CFSpeed.Time*8.3)*0.06+math.cos(CFSpeed.Time*3.1+1.7)*0.05+(valNoise(CFSpeed.Time*2.6+CFSpeed.Noise)-0.5)*CFSpeed.Jitter
        CFSpeed.Pause=CFSpeed.Pause+dt
        if CFSpeed.Pause>RNG:NextNumber(3,7) then CFSpeed.Pause=0 if RNG:NextNumber()<0.25 then wob=wob*0.2 end end
        Gov.targets.cf=CFSpeed.Speed*math.max(wob,0.2)
    end))
end

local CFFly = { Enabled=false, Speed=24, Jitter=0.15, Time=0, Noise=math.random()*1000, Maid=Maid.new() }
function CFFly.Start()
    CFFly.Maid:Clean()
    local root,hum=Char.HRP(),Char.Hum(); if not root or not hum then return end
    snapshotCollision()
    hum.PlatformStand=true; CFFly.Time=0; CFFly.Noise=math.random()*1000
    CFFly.Maid:Give(function()
        local h=Char.Hum()
        if h then
            h.PlatformStand=false; h.Sit=false
            pcall(function() h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp,true) end)
            pcall(function() h:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
        end
        local r=Char.HRP()
        if r then
            r.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero; r.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
            local _,yaw=r.CFrame:ToOrientation()
            r.CFrame=CFrame.new(r.Position)*CFrame.Angles(0,yaw,0)
        end
        restoreCollision()
        task.delay(0.1, function() local h2=Char.Hum() if h2 and not CFFly.Enabled then h2.PlatformStand=false pcall(function() h2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall) end) end end)
    end)
    CFFly.Maid:Give(RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        local c=Char.Get(); if not c then return end
        for _,p in ipairs(charBaseParts()) do if p.CanCollide then p.CanCollide=false end end
    end))
    CFFly.Maid:Give(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        local r,h=Char.HRP(),Char.Hum(); if not r or not h then return end
        h.PlatformStand=true; CFFly.Time=CFFly.Time+dt
        local cf=Camera.CFrame; local move=flyMove()
        local spd=math.min(CFFly.Speed,FLY_CAP)
        r.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero r.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
        if move.Magnitude>0 then
            local wob=1+(valNoise(CFFly.Time*2.4+CFFly.Noise)-0.5)*CFFly.Jitter
            local jit=Vector3.new(valNoise(CFFly.Time*7.1)-0.5,valNoise(CFFly.Time*6.3+50)-0.5,valNoise(CFFly.Time*5.7+90)-0.5)*0.08
            r.CFrame=r.CFrame+(move.Unit*spd*wob+jit)*dt
        end
    end))
end
function CFFly.Stop() CFFly.Maid:Clean() end

local Water = { Enabled=false, Interval=1.0, HoldTime=2.0, Maid=Maid.new() }
local function drinkOnce()
    if not WaterSource then return end
    pcall(function() WaterSource:FireServer("Drinking") end)
    task.wait(Water.HoldTime + math.random()*0.25)
    if not Water.Enabled then pcall(function() WaterSource:FireServer("Stopped") end) return end
    pcall(function() WaterSource:FireServer("Drank") end)
    task.wait(0.05)
    pcall(function() WaterSource:FireServer("Stopped") end)
end
function Water.Set(on)
    Water.Enabled=on; Water.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then return end
    Water.Maid:Give(task.spawn(function()
        while Water.Enabled do
            local t=Char.Val("Thirst")
            if not t or t.Value<95 then drinkOnce() end
            task.wait(Water.Interval)
        end
    end))
end

local FOOD_KEYS = {"berry","berries","food","meat","bread","apple","carrot","wheat","fish","cooked",
    "steak","mushroom","banana","corn","potato","egg","cheese","pie","soup","stew","fruit","jerky"}
local function isFoodTool(t)
    if not t or not t:IsA("Tool") then return false end
    if CollectionService:HasTag(t,"Food") or CollectionService:HasTag(t,"Good Food") then return true end
    local n=t.Name:lower(); for _,k in ipairs(FOOD_KEYS) do if n:find(k) then return true end end
    return false
end
local function findFood()
    local c=Char.Get(); if c then for _,t in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do if isFoodTool(t) then return t,true end end end
    local bp=LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack"); if bp then for _,t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do if isFoodTool(t) then return t,false end end end
    return nil
end
local Food = { Enabled=false, Interval=2, Threshold=90, Maid=Maid.new() }
function Food.Set(on)
    Food.Enabled=on; Food.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then return end
    Food.Maid:Give(task.spawn(function()
        while Food.Enabled do
            local h=Char.Val("Hunger")
            if not h or h.Value<Food.Threshold then
                local hum=Char.Hum(); local tool,eq=findFood()
                if hum and tool then
                    if not eq then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end); task.wait(0.15) end
                    pcall(function() tool:Activate() end); task.wait(0.12); pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
                end
            end
            task.wait(Food.Interval)
        end
    end))
end

local SafePrompt = { last = 0, shown = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }), conns = {} }
do
    local PPS = game:GetService("ProximityPromptService")
    SafePrompt.conns[#SafePrompt.conns + 1] = PPS.PromptShown:Connect(function(p) SafePrompt.shown[p] = true end)
    SafePrompt.conns[#SafePrompt.conns + 1] = PPS.PromptHidden:Connect(function(p) SafePrompt.shown[p] = nil end)
end
SafePrompt.hidden = {}
function SafePrompt.restoreAll()
    for q in pairs(SafePrompt.hidden) do pcall(function() if q.Parent then q.Enabled = true end end) end
    table.clear(SafePrompt.hidden)
    if SafePrompt.aim then pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(SafePrompt.aim) end) SafePrompt.aim = nil end
end
function SafePrompt.hold(p, extra)
    local ok, a, b = pcall(SafePrompt.holdInner, p, extra)
    SafePrompt.restoreAll()
    if not ok then return false, "prompt error: " .. tostring(a) end
    return a, b
end
function SafePrompt.destroy() SafePrompt.restoreAll() for _, c in ipairs(SafePrompt.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end table.clear(SafePrompt.conns) end
function SafePrompt.pos(p)
    local par = p and p.Parent
    if not par then return nil end
    if par:IsA("Attachment") then return par.WorldPosition end
    if par:IsA("BasePart") then return par.Position end
    local bp = p:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart")
    if bp then return bp.Position end
    if par:IsA("Model") then local ok, cf = pcall(par.GetPivot, par) if ok then return cf.Position end end
    return nil
end
function SafePrompt.inRange(p, margin)
    local root = Char.HRP()
    if not root then return false end
    local pos = SafePrompt.pos(p)
    if not pos then return true end
    local m = p.MaxActivationDistance or 10
    return (pos - root.Position).Magnitude <= m - (margin or 0.5)
end
function SafePrompt.holdInner(p, extra)
    if not (p and p.Parent and p.Enabled) then return false, "prompt gone" end
    if not SafePrompt.inRange(p, 0.5) then return false, "too far for the prompt" end
    local gap = SafePrompt.last + 0.35 - os.clock()
    if gap > 0 then task.wait(gap) end
    local hold = math.max(p.HoldDuration or 0, 0) + math.max(extra or 0, 0)
    local root = Char.HRP()
    local hidden = SafePrompt.hidden
    local function restore() for q in pairs(hidden) do pcall(function() if q.Parent then q.Enabled = true end end) end table.clear(hidden) end
    if not SafePrompt.shown[p] and root then
        local key = p.KeyboardKeyCode
        for q in pairs(SafePrompt.shown) do
            if q ~= p and q.Parent and q.Enabled and q.KeyboardKeyCode == key and q.Exclusivity == Enum.ProximityPromptExclusivity.OnePerButton then
                hidden[q] = true
                q.Enabled = false
            end
        end
        for _, part in ipairs(Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(root.Position, 25)) do
            for _, q in ipairs(part:GetDescendants()) do
                if q:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and q ~= p and q.Enabled and q.KeyboardKeyCode == key and q.Exclusivity == Enum.ProximityPromptExclusivity.OnePerButton and not hidden[q] then
                    hidden[q] = true
                    q.Enabled = false
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local aimName
    if not SafePrompt.shown[p] then
        local aimPos = SafePrompt.pos(p)
        if aimPos then
            aimName = "LZPromptAim" .. tostring(math.random(1, 1e6))
            SafePrompt.aim = aimName
            pcall(function()
                RunService:BindToRenderStep(aimName, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 2, function()
                    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                    if cam and p.Parent then cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(cam.CFrame.Position, aimPos) end
                end)
            end)
        end
    end
    local function unaim() if aimName then pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(aimName) end) aimName = nil end end
    local tShow = os.clock()
    while not SafePrompt.shown[p] and os.clock() - tShow < 1.2 do task.wait(0.05) end
    if not SafePrompt.shown[p] and root then
        local pos = SafePrompt.pos(p)
        local hum = Char.Hum()
        if pos and hum then
            local away = Vector3.new(root.Position.X - pos.X, 0, root.Position.Z - pos.Z)
            away = away.Magnitude > 0.1 and away.Unit or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
            local spots = { away }
            if p.RequiresLineOfSight then
                spots[#spots + 1] = Vector3.new(-away.Z, 0, away.X)
                spots[#spots + 1] = Vector3.new(away.Z, 0, -away.X)
                spots[#spots + 1] = -away
            end
            for _, dir in ipairs(spots) do
                if SafePrompt.shown[p] or not p.Parent then break end
                local goal = Vector3.new(pos.X, root.Position.Y, pos.Z) + dir * 3
                local tw = os.clock()
                while not SafePrompt.shown[p] and os.clock() - tw < 1.6 and (goal - root.Position).Magnitude > 1.2 do
                    hum:MoveTo(goal)
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
                hum:MoveTo(root.Position)
                local t2 = os.clock()
                while not SafePrompt.shown[p] and os.clock() - t2 < 0.5 do task.wait(0.05) end
            end
        end
    end
    if not SafePrompt.shown[p] then unaim() restore() return false, "prompt is not showing on screen" end
    if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then unaim() restore() return false, "you are typing" end
    local fired = false
    local conn = p.Triggered:Connect(function() fired = true end)
    local key = p.KeyboardKeyCode
    local vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
    pcall(function()
        vim:SendKeyEvent(true, key, false, game)
        task.wait(hold + 0.1 + math.random() * 0.15)
        vim:SendKeyEvent(false, key, false, game)
    end)
    local t0 = os.clock()
    while not fired and os.clock() - t0 < 0.6 do
        if not (p.Parent and p.Enabled) then fired = true break end
        task.wait(0.03)
    end
    conn:Disconnect()
    unaim()
    restore()
    SafePrompt.last = os.clock()
    return fired, fired and nil or "prompt did not trigger"
end

local Prompts = { Enabled=false, MinDelay=0.6, RespectHold=true, LastUse={}, Maid=Maid.new() }
local function promptInRange(p)
    local root=Char.HRP(); if not root then return false end
    local part=(p.Parent and p.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and p.Parent) or p:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart")
    if not part then return true end
    local m=p.MaxActivationDistance
    if m and m>0 and (part.Position-root.Position).Magnitude>m then return false end
    return true
end
local function hookPrompt(p)
    Prompts.Maid:Give(p.PromptButtonHoldBegan:Connect(function()
        if not Prompts.Enabled or not promptInRange(p) or SafePrompt.busy then return end
        local now,last=os.clock(),Prompts.LastUse[p] or 0
        if now-last<Prompts.MinDelay then return end
        Prompts.LastUse[p]=now
        if (p.HoldDuration or 0)<=0 then return end
        local done,released=false,false
        local c1=p.Triggered:Connect(function() done=true end)
        local c2=p.PromptButtonHoldEnded:Connect(function() released=true end)
        local t0=os.clock()
        while not done and not released and os.clock()-t0<p.HoldDuration+1 do task.wait(0.05) end
        c1:Disconnect() c2:Disconnect()
        if done or not Prompts.Enabled or not p.Enabled or not promptInRange(p) then return end
        SafePrompt.busy=true
        pcall(SafePrompt.hold,p)
        SafePrompt.busy=false
    end))
end
function Prompts.Set(on)
    Prompts.Enabled=on; Prompts.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then Prompts.LastUse={} return end
    Prompts.Maid:Give(Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(o)
        if Prompts.Enabled and o.ClassName=="ProximityPrompt" then hookPrompt(o) end
    end))
    local gen={}
    Prompts.Gen=gen
    task.spawn(function()
        local list=Workspace:GetDescendants()
        local slice=os.clock()
        for i=1,#list do
            if Prompts.Gen~=gen or not Prompts.Enabled then return end
            local o=list[i]
            if o.ClassName=="ProximityPrompt" and o.Parent then hookPrompt(o) end
            if i%250==0 and os.clock()-slice>0.004 then task.wait() slice=os.clock() end
        end
    end)
end

local KeepBackpack = { Enabled=false, Maid=Maid.new() }
local StarterGuiSvc = game:GetService("StarterGui")
function KeepBackpack.Set(on)
    KeepBackpack.Enabled=on; KeepBackpack.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then return end
    KeepBackpack.Maid:Give(task.spawn(function()
        while KeepBackpack.Enabled do
            pcall(function() StarterGuiSvc:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack,true) end)
            task.wait(0.15)
        end
    end))
end

local AntiGrab = { Enabled=false, Maid=Maid.new() }
local function foreignPart(part)
    if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
    local m=part:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model"); if not m then return false end
    local plr=Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m)
    return plr~=nil and plr~=LocalPlayer
end
local function breakGrabs(cont, myParts)
    for _,d in ipairs(cont:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("WeldConstraint") or d:IsA("Motor6D") or d:IsA("Snap") then
            local p0,p1=d.Part0,d.Part1
            if (p0 and myParts[p0] and foreignPart(p1)) or (p1 and myParts[p1] and foreignPart(p0)) then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
        end
    end
end
function AntiGrab.Set(on)
    AntiGrab.Enabled=on; AntiGrab.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then return end
    local acc=0
    AntiGrab.Maid:Give(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        local c=Char.Get(); local root=Char.HRP(); local hum=Char.Hum(); if not c then return end
        if hum and hum.PlatformStand and not Fly.Enabled then hum.PlatformStand=false end
        if root and root.Anchored then root.Anchored=false end
        acc=acc+dt; if acc<0.2 then return end; acc=0
        local myParts={}; for _,p in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do if p:IsA("BasePart") then myParts[p]=true end end
        breakGrabs(c,myParts)
        local near,bd=nil,math.huge
        if root then for _,plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr~=LocalPlayer and plr.Character then local h=plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if h then local d=(h.Position-root.Position).Magnitude if d<bd then bd=d;near=plr.Character end end end
        end end
        if near then breakGrabs(near,myParts) end
    end))
end

function Char.IsTeammate(plr)
    local myTeam=LocalPlayer.Team
    return myTeam~=nil and not LocalPlayer.Neutral and plr.Team==myTeam
end

local function nearestEnemy(range, teamCheck)
    local root=Char.HRP(); if not root then return nil end
    local best,bd=nil,range or 1000
    for _,plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr~=LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
            if not (teamCheck and Char.IsTeammate(plr)) then
                local h=plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum=plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if h and hum and hum.Health>0 then
                    local d=(h.Position-root.Position).Magnitude
                    if d<bd then bd=d;best=h end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

local Aim = { Enabled=false, Prediction=true, PredFactor=0.16, MaxDist=300, TeamCheck=false, AutoThrow=false, ThrowRate=0.8, Hooked=false, ThrowRem=nil, MousePosRem=nil, Maid=Maid.new() }
local function aimPos()
    local h=nearestEnemy(Aim.MaxDist, Aim.TeamCheck); if not h then return nil end
    local head=h.Parent and h.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
    local p=(head and head.Position) or h.Position
    if Aim.Prediction then p=p+h.AssemblyLinearVelocity*Aim.PredFactor end
    return p
end
function Aim.Resolve()
    Aim.ThrowRem = findPath(ReplicatedStorage,"Remotes","Pvp","Throw") or ThrowRemote
    Aim.MousePosRem = findPath(ReplicatedStorage,"Remotes","Pvp","MousePos") or MousePos
end
local function applyMousePos()
    if Aim.MousePosRem then pcall(function()
        Aim.MousePosRem.OnClientInvoke=function()
            if Aim.Enabled then local t=aimPos() if t then return t end end
            return Mouse.Hit.Position
        end
    end) end
end
function Aim.Hook()
    if Aim.Hooked then return end
    Aim.Hooked=true
    Aim.Resolve(); applyMousePos()
    local G=getgenv()
    local shared=G.__LedZAimShared
    if not shared then shared={} G.__LedZAimShared=shared end
    shared.aim, shared.aimPos = Aim, aimPos
    if shared.installed then return end
    local ok,mt=pcall(getrawmetatable,game)
    if not (ok and mt) then return end
    shared.installed=true
    local old=mt.__namecall
    pcall(setreadonly,mt,false)
    mt.__namecall=newcclosure(function(self,...)
        local A=shared.aim
        if A and A.Enabled and self==A.ThrowRem and not checkcaller() and getnamecallmethod()=="FireServer" then
            local okT,t=pcall(shared.aimPos)
            if okT and t then
                local args=table.pack(...)
                local swapped=false
                for i=2,args.n do if typeof(args[i])=="Vector3" then args[i]=t swapped=true end end
                if swapped then return old(self,table.unpack(args,1,args.n)) end
            end
        end
        return old(self,...)
    end)
    pcall(setreadonly,mt,true)
end
function Aim.Set(on)
    Aim.Enabled=on; Aim.Maid:Clean(); Aim.Resolve(); if on then Aim.Hook() end; applyMousePos()
    if not on then return end
    Aim.Maid:Give(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.5) Aim.Resolve() applyMousePos() end))
    if Aim.AutoThrow then
        Aim.Maid:Give(task.spawn(function()
            while Aim.Enabled do
                local tr=Aim.ThrowRem
                if Aim.AutoThrow and tr then local t=aimPos() if t then pcall(function() tr:FireServer(true,t) end) end end
                task.wait(Aim.ThrowRate)
            end
        end))
    end
end

local Combat = {}
function Combat.Shield(s) if ShieldRemote then pcall(function() ShieldRemote:InvokeServer(s) end) end end
function Combat.Kick() if KickRemote then pcall(function() KickRemote:FireServer() end) end end
function Combat.ToggleFF() if FriendlyFire then pcall(function() FriendlyFire:FireServer() end) end end
function Combat.Throw() if ThrowRemote then local t=aimPos() or Mouse.Hit.Position pcall(function() ThrowRemote:FireServer(true,t) end) end end

local AutoCombat = {
    Kick=false, Shield=false, KickTeamCheck=false, ShieldTeamCheck=false,
    Range=14, ShieldRange=60, KickCooldown=0.35, ShieldHold=0.6, ManualGrace=0.75, RetryDelay=0.25, TickRate=1/30,
    OwnsShield=false, Busy=false, BusySince=0, LastRequest=0, LastKick=0, LastThreat=0, ManualUntil=0,
    UserHolding=false, Acc=0, Hooked=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"}), Maid=Maid.new(),
}

function AutoCombat.PvpFlag(pvp, name)
    local v=pvp:FindFirstChild(name)
    return v~=nil and v.Value==true
end

function AutoCombat.NoteManual()
    AutoCombat.OwnsShield=false
    AutoCombat.ManualUntil=os.clock()+AutoCombat.ManualGrace
end

function AutoCombat.RequestShield(state)
    if not ShieldRemote then return end
    local now=os.clock()
    if AutoCombat.Busy and now-AutoCombat.BusySince<2 then return end
    local sh=Char.PvpVal("Shielding")
    if sh and sh.Value==state then return end
    AutoCombat.Busy=true; AutoCombat.BusySince=now; AutoCombat.LastRequest=now
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function() ShieldRemote:InvokeServer(state) end)
        AutoCombat.Busy=false
    end)
end

function AutoCombat.ReleaseOwned()
    if not AutoCombat.OwnsShield then return end
    AutoCombat.OwnsShield=false
    if AutoCombat.UserHolding or UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) then return end
    AutoCombat.RequestShield(false)
end

function AutoCombat.HookGameButton()
    local pg=LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    local btn=pg and findPath(pg,"Mobile","Frame","ShieldButton")
    if not btn or AutoCombat.Hooked[btn] then return end
    AutoCombat.Hooked[btn]=true
    AutoCombat.Maid:Give(btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(AutoCombat.NoteManual))
end

function AutoCombat.Scan(root)
    local kickRange,shieldRange=AutoCombat.Range,AutoCombat.ShieldRange
    local wantKick,wantShield=AutoCombat.Kick,AutoCombat.Shield
    local shieldTarget,threat=false,false
    for _,plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local ch=plr~=LocalPlayer and plr.Character
        local hrp=ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum=ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local pvp=ch and ch:FindFirstChild("Pvp")
        if hrp and hum and pvp and hum.Health>0 and not AutoCombat.PvpFlag(pvp,"Knocked") then
            local dist=(hrp.Position-root.Position).Magnitude
            local mate=Char.IsTeammate(plr)
            if wantKick and not shieldTarget and dist<=kickRange
                and not (AutoCombat.KickTeamCheck and mate) and AutoCombat.PvpFlag(pvp,"Shielding") then
                shieldTarget=true
            end
            if wantShield and not threat and dist<=shieldRange
                and not (AutoCombat.ShieldTeamCheck and mate)
                and (AutoCombat.PvpFlag(pvp,"Throw") or AutoCombat.PvpFlag(pvp,"Aiming")) then
                threat=true
            end
            if shieldTarget==wantKick and threat==wantShield then break end
        end
    end
    return shieldTarget,threat
end

function AutoCombat.Set()
    AutoCombat.Maid:Clean()
    table.clear(AutoCombat.Hooked)
    if not AutoCombat.Shield then AutoCombat.ReleaseOwned() end
    if not (AutoCombat.Kick or AutoCombat.Shield) then return end
    AutoCombat.Acc=0
    AutoCombat.HookGameButton()
    AutoCombat.Maid:Give(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 or i.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL2 then AutoCombat.NoteManual() end
    end))
    AutoCombat.Maid:Give(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        AutoCombat.OwnsShield=false; AutoCombat.Busy=false; AutoCombat.LastThreat=0; AutoCombat.UserHolding=false
        task.delay(1, AutoCombat.HookGameButton)
    end))
    AutoCombat.Maid:Give(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        AutoCombat.Acc = AutoCombat.Acc + dt
        if AutoCombat.Acc<AutoCombat.TickRate then return end
        AutoCombat.Acc=0

        local root=Char.HRP(); local hum=Char.Hum()
        local cm=Char.PvpVal("CombatMode"); local myShield=Char.PvpVal("Shielding")
        local knocked=Char.PvpVal("Knocked")
        if not (root and hum and hum.Health>0 and cm and cm.Value and myShield) or (knocked and knocked.Value) then
            AutoCombat.ReleaseOwned(); return
        end
        if AutoCombat.OwnsShield and not myShield.Value and not AutoCombat.Busy then
            AutoCombat.OwnsShield=false
        end

        local shieldTarget,threat=AutoCombat.Scan(root)
        local now=os.clock()

        if AutoCombat.Kick and shieldTarget and now-AutoCombat.LastKick>=AutoCombat.KickCooldown then
            AutoCombat.LastKick=now
            Combat.Kick()
        end

        if AutoCombat.Shield and now>=AutoCombat.ManualUntil then
            if threat then
                AutoCombat.LastThreat=now
                if not myShield.Value and not AutoCombat.Busy and now-AutoCombat.LastRequest>=AutoCombat.RetryDelay then
                    AutoCombat.OwnsShield=true
                    AutoCombat.RequestShield(true)
                end
            elseif AutoCombat.OwnsShield and now-AutoCombat.LastThreat>=AutoCombat.ShieldHold then
                AutoCombat.ReleaseOwned()
            end
        end
    end))
end

local MobileCombat = { Enabled=false, OnlyInCombat=false, Hold=false, Size=72, Gui=nil, Shield=nil, Kick=nil, LastTap=0, Maid=Maid.new() }
local function mcShielding() local v=Char.PvpVal("Shielding") return v~=nil and v.Value==true end
local function mcSendShield(on) task.spawn(Combat.Shield, on) end
local function mcMakeButton(parent, text, pos, color)
    local b=Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Name=text; b.Text=text; b.AutoButtonColor=false; b.Active=true
    b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; b.TextScaled=true; b.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1)
    b.BackgroundColor3=color; b.BackgroundTransparency=0.15
    b.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5); b.Position=pos; b.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(MobileCombat.Size,MobileCombat.Size)
    local corner=Instance.new("UICorner"); corner.CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0); corner.Parent=b
    local stroke=Instance.new("UIStroke"); stroke.ApplyStrokeMode=Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border; stroke.Thickness=3
    stroke.Color=Color3.new(1,1,1); stroke.Transparency=0.35; stroke.Parent=b
    local pad=Instance.new("UIPadding")
    for _,k in ipairs({"PaddingLeft","PaddingRight","PaddingTop","PaddingBottom"}) do pad[k]=UDim.new(0,12) end
    pad.Parent=b
    b.Parent=parent
    return b, stroke
end
local function mcWire(b, onTap, onPress, onRelease)
    local active, start, startPos, moved
    MobileCombat.Maid:Give(b.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.Touch and i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
        active, start, startPos, moved = i, i.Position, b.Position, false
        if onPress then onPress() end
    end))
    MobileCombat.Maid:Give(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if not active then return end
        if i~=active and i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then return end
        local delta=i.Position-start
        if moved or Vector2.new(delta.X,delta.Y).Magnitude>12 then
            moved=true
            b.Position=UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale,startPos.X.Offset+delta.X,startPos.Y.Scale,startPos.Y.Offset+delta.Y)
        end
    end))
    MobileCombat.Maid:Give(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if not active then return end
        if i~=active and i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
        active=nil
        if onRelease then onRelease() end
        if not moved and onTap then onTap() end
    end))
end
function MobileCombat.Build()
    MobileCombat.Maid:Clean()
    local gui=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name="LZ_MobileCombat"; gui.ResetOnSpawn=false; gui.IgnoreGuiInset=true; gui.DisplayOrder=50
    gui.ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling; gui.Enabled=false
    local ok=pcall(function() gui.Parent=(gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    if not ok or not gui.Parent then gui.Parent=LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
    MobileCombat.Maid:Give(gui)
    MobileCombat.Gui=gui
    local shield, shieldStroke=mcMakeButton(gui,"SHIELD",UDim2.new(1,-190,1,-150),Color3.fromRGB(48,68,92))
    local kick=mcMakeButton(gui,"KICK",UDim2.new(1,-190,1,-240),Color3.fromRGB(142,62,62))
    MobileCombat.Shield, MobileCombat.Kick = shield, kick
    mcWire(shield,
        function()
            if MobileCombat.Hold then return end
            if os.clock()-MobileCombat.LastTap<0.15 then return end
            MobileCombat.LastTap=os.clock()
            AutoCombat.NoteManual()
            mcSendShield(not mcShielding())
        end,
        function()
            if not MobileCombat.Hold then return end
            AutoCombat.NoteManual(); AutoCombat.UserHolding=true
            mcSendShield(true)
        end,
        function()
            if not AutoCombat.UserHolding then return end
            AutoCombat.UserHolding=false; AutoCombat.NoteManual()
            mcSendShield(false)
        end)
    mcWire(kick, function() Combat.Kick() end)
    MobileCombat.Maid:Give(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local cm=Char.PvpVal("CombatMode")
        gui.Enabled=MobileCombat.Enabled and (not MobileCombat.OnlyInCombat or (cm~=nil and cm.Value==true))
        if not gui.Enabled then return end
        local up=mcShielding()
        shield.Text=up and "SHIELD UP" or "SHIELD"
        shield.BackgroundColor3=up and Color3.fromRGB(62,140,92) or Color3.fromRGB(48,68,92)
        shieldStroke.Color=up and Color3.fromRGB(150,255,170) or Color3.new(1,1,1)
    end))
end
function MobileCombat.Set(on)
    MobileCombat.Enabled=on
    if on and not (MobileCombat.Gui and MobileCombat.Gui.Parent) then MobileCombat.Build() end
end
function MobileCombat.Resize(px)
    MobileCombat.Size=px
    for _,b in ipairs({MobileCombat.Shield,MobileCombat.Kick}) do if b and b.Parent then b.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(px,px) end end
end
function MobileCombat.ResetPositions()
    if MobileCombat.Shield then MobileCombat.Shield.Position=UDim2.new(1,-190,1,-150) end
    if MobileCombat.Kick then MobileCombat.Kick.Position=UDim2.new(1,-190,1,-240) end
end
function MobileCombat.Destroy() MobileCombat.Enabled=false MobileCombat.Maid:Clean() MobileCombat.Gui=nil MobileCombat.Shield=nil MobileCombat.Kick=nil AutoCombat.UserHolding=false end

local WEAPON_TAGS={"Melee","Spear","Bow"}
local WEAPON_KEYS={"sword","spear","axe","club","bow","dagger","knife","mace","hammer","blade","pike"}
local function isWeaponTool(t)
    if not t or not t:IsA("Tool") then return false end
    for _,tag in ipairs(WEAPON_TAGS) do if CollectionService:HasTag(t,tag) then return true end end
    local n=t.Name:lower(); for _,k in ipairs(WEAPON_KEYS) do if n:find(k) then return true end end
    return false
end
local function toolDamage(t)
    for _,nm in ipairs({"Damage","Dmg","Power","Attack","BaseDamage"}) do
        local a=t:GetAttribute(nm); if type(a)=="number" then return a end
    end
    for _,nm in ipairs({"Damage","Dmg","Power","Attack","BaseDamage"}) do
        local v=t:FindFirstChild(nm); if v and v:IsA("ValueBase") and type(v.Value)=="number" then return v.Value end
    end
    return 0
end
local function findWeapon()
    local c=Char.Get(); local bp=LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    local best,bd=nil,-1
    for _,cont in ipairs({c,bp}) do if cont then for _,t in ipairs(cont:GetChildren()) do
        if isWeaponTool(t) then local d=toolDamage(t) if d>bd then bd=d;best=t end end
    end end end
    return best
end
local AutoWeapon = { Enabled=false, LastHP=nil, Maid=Maid.new() }
local function equipWeapon()
    local c=Char.Get(); local h=Char.Hum(); if not c or not h then return end
    local w=findWeapon(); if not w then return end
    local held=c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if held==w then return end
    if held and isWeaponTool(held) and toolDamage(held)>=toolDamage(w) then return end
    pcall(function() h:EquipTool(w) end)
end
function AutoWeapon.Set(on)
    AutoWeapon.Enabled=on; AutoWeapon.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then return end
    local function hook()
        local c=Char.Get(); local h=Char.Hum(); if not c or not h then return end
        AutoWeapon.LastHP=h.Health
        AutoWeapon.Maid:Give(h.HealthChanged:Connect(function(nh)
            if AutoWeapon.Enabled and nh<(AutoWeapon.LastHP or nh) then equipWeapon() end
            AutoWeapon.LastHP=nh
        end))
        local ic=c:FindFirstChild("InCombat")
        if ic then AutoWeapon.Maid:Give(ic.Changed:Connect(function() if AutoWeapon.Enabled and ic.Value then equipWeapon() end end)) end
    end
    hook()
    AutoWeapon.Maid:Give(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.6) if AutoWeapon.Enabled then hook() end end))
end

local Panic = { Auto=false, StayUp=false, Threshold=25, Height=40, Duration=0.45, Hold=1.5, Busy=false, Maid=Maid.new(), LastHP=nil }
local function panicRise()
    if Panic.Busy then return end
    local hrp=Char.HRP(); if not hrp then return end
    Panic.Busy=true
    task.spawn(function()
        local startPos=(Char.HRP() and Char.HRP().Position) or hrp.Position
        local targetPos=startPos+Vector3.new(0,Panic.Height,0)
        local t=0
        while t<Panic.Duration do
            local dt=RunService.RenderStepped:Wait(); t=t+dt
            local a=math.clamp(t/Panic.Duration,0,1); local e=1-(1-a)*(1-a)
            local r=Char.HRP(); if not r then break end
            r.CFrame=CFrame.new(startPos:Lerp(targetPos,e))*rotOnly(r.CFrame); r.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
        end
        local ty=targetPos.Y
        if Panic.StayUp then
            while Panic.StayUp do
                local r=Char.HRP(); if not r then break end
                local p=r.Position; r.CFrame=CFrame.new(p.X,ty,p.Z)*rotOnly(r.CFrame)
                local v=r.AssemblyLinearVelocity; r.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(v.X,0,v.Z)
                RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            end
        else
            local hs=os.clock()
            while os.clock()-hs<Panic.Hold do
                local r=Char.HRP(); if not r then break end
                r.CFrame=CFrame.new(targetPos)*rotOnly(r.CFrame); r.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
                RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            end
        end
        Panic.Busy=false
    end)
end
function Panic.SetStay(on) Panic.StayUp=on if on and not Panic.Busy then panicRise() end end
function Panic.SetAuto(on)
    Panic.Auto=on; Panic.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then return end
    local function hook()
        local h=Char.Hum(); if not h then return end
        Panic.LastHP=h.Health
        Panic.Maid:Give(h.HealthChanged:Connect(function(nh)
            if Panic.Auto and nh<=Panic.Threshold and nh<(Panic.LastHP or nh) then panicRise() end
            Panic.LastHP=nh
        end))
    end
    hook()
    Panic.Maid:Give(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.6) if Panic.Auto then hook() end end))
end

local GhostKill = { Enabled=false, Above=true, Depth=5, Offset=6, HBSize=11, Visible=true, Target=nil, LastPos=nil, HBOrig=nil, Box=nil, Collide={}, Maid=Maid.new() }
local function ghostRestoreHB()
    local o=GhostKill.HBOrig
    if o and o.part and o.part.Parent then pcall(function() o.part.Size=o.size o.part.Transparency=o.tr o.part.CanCollide=o.cc o.part.Material=o.mat end) end
    GhostKill.HBOrig=nil
    if GhostKill.Box then GhostKill.Box:Destroy() GhostKill.Box=nil end
end
local function ghostSnapCollide()
    GhostKill.Collide={}
    local c=Char.Get(); if not c then return end
    for _,p in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do if p:IsA("BasePart") then GhostKill.Collide[p]=p.CanCollide end end
end
local function ghostRestoreCollide()
    for p,v in pairs(GhostKill.Collide) do if p and p.Parent then pcall(function() p.CanCollide=v end) end end
    GhostKill.Collide={}
end
local function ghostGroundY(pos)
    local rp=RaycastParams.new(); rp.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local ig={} if Char.Get() then ig[#ig+1]=Char.Get() end
    local tgt=GhostKill.Target
    if tgt then ig[#ig+1]=(tgt.Parent or tgt) end
    if GhostKill.Box then ig[#ig+1]=GhostKill.Box end
    rp.FilterDescendantsInstances=ig
    local hit=Workspace:Raycast(pos+Vector3.new(0,6,0),Vector3.new(0,-800,0),rp)
    return hit and hit.Position.Y or (pos.Y-4)
end
local function ghostDesired(p)
    if GhostKill.Above then return Vector3.new(p.X,p.Y+GhostKill.Offset,p.Z) end
    return Vector3.new(p.X, ghostGroundY(p)-GhostKill.Depth, p.Z)
end
function GhostKill.Set(on)
    GhostKill.Enabled=on; GhostKill.Maid:Clean()
    ghostRestoreHB(); ghostRestoreCollide()
    if not on then return end
    local root=Char.HRP(); if not root then GhostKill.Enabled=false return end
    local h=nearestEnemy(200, false); if not h then GhostKill.Enabled=false return end
    GhostKill.Target=h; GhostKill.LastPos=h.Position
    ghostSnapCollide()
    root.CFrame=CFrame.new(ghostDesired(h.Position))*rotOnly(root.CFrame)
    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
    GhostKill.Maid:Give(function() ghostRestoreHB() ghostRestoreCollide() local h2=Char.Hum() if h2 then h2.PlatformStand=false pcall(function() h2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end) end end)
    GhostKill.Maid:Give(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not GhostKill.Enabled then return end
        local r=Char.HRP(); local hum=Char.Hum(); local c=Char.Get(); if not r or not hum or hum.Health<=0 or not c then return end
        if GhostKill.Above then
            hum.PlatformStand=false
            for p,v in pairs(GhostKill.Collide) do if p and p.Parent then p.CanCollide=v end end
        else
            hum.PlatformStand=true
            for _,p in ipairs(charBaseParts()) do if p.CanCollide then p.CanCollide=false end end
        end
        local tgt=GhostKill.Target
        if not tgt or not tgt.Parent then local nh=nearestEnemy(200,false) if not nh then return end tgt=nh GhostKill.Target=nh end
        local cur=tgt.Position; GhostKill.LastPos=cur
        local desired=ghostDesired(cur)
        r.CFrame=CFrame.new(desired)*rotOnly(r.CFrame)
        r.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
        r.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
        if not (GhostKill.HBOrig and GhostKill.HBOrig.part==tgt) then
            ghostRestoreHB()
            GhostKill.HBOrig={ part=tgt, size=tgt.Size, tr=tgt.Transparency, cc=tgt.CanCollide, mat=tgt.Material }
        end
        local s=GhostKill.HBSize; local sz=Vector3.new(s,s,s)
        if tgt.Size~=sz then tgt.Size=sz end
        tgt.CanCollide=false
        if GhostKill.Visible then
            tgt.Transparency=0.55; tgt.Material=Enum.Material.ForceField
            local box=GhostKill.Box
            if not box or box.Adornee~=tgt then
                if box then box:Destroy() end
                box=Instance.new("SelectionBox")
                box.Adornee=tgt; box.Color3=Color3.fromRGB(255,60,60); box.SurfaceColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,60,60)
                box.LineThickness=0.06; box.SurfaceTransparency=0.75; box.Transparency=0.15; box.Parent=tgt
                GhostKill.Box=box
            end
        end
    end))
end

local Hitbox = { Enabled=false, Size=10, Alpha=0.55, Visible=true, Color=Color3.fromRGB(0,255,100), Orig={}, Maid=Maid.new() }
local function hbTargetPart(char)
    return char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char.PrimaryPart or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
end
local function hbRestore(part)
    local o=Hitbox.Orig[part]
    if o and part and part.Parent then pcall(function() part.Size=o.size part.Transparency=o.tr part.CanCollide=o.cc part.Material=o.mat part.Color=o.col end) end
    local box=part and part:FindFirstChild("LZ_Hitbox") if box then box:Destroy() end
    Hitbox.Orig[part]=nil
end
local function hbRestoreAll() for part in pairs(Hitbox.Orig) do hbRestore(part) end Hitbox.Orig={} end
function Hitbox.Set(on)
    Hitbox.Enabled=on; Hitbox.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then hbRestoreAll() return end
    Hitbox.Maid:Give(function() hbRestoreAll() end)
    Hitbox.Acc=0
    Hitbox.Maid:Give(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        Hitbox.Acc=Hitbox.Acc+dt; if Hitbox.Acc<0.06 then return end; Hitbox.Acc=0
        local size=Vector3.new(Hitbox.Size,Hitbox.Size,Hitbox.Size)
        for _,plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr~=LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
                local part=hbTargetPart(plr.Character)
                if part then
                    if Hitbox.Orig[part]==nil then Hitbox.Orig[part]={size=part.Size,tr=part.Transparency,cc=part.CanCollide,mat=part.Material,col=part.Color} end
                    if part.Size~=size then part.Size=size end
                    part.Transparency=Hitbox.Visible and Hitbox.Alpha or 1
                    part.Material=Enum.Material.ForceField
                    part.Color=Hitbox.Color
                    part.CanCollide=false; part.CanTouch=true; part.CanQuery=true
                    local box=part:FindFirstChild("LZ_Hitbox")
