                    if not box then box=Instance.new("SelectionBox") box.Name="LZ_Hitbox" box.Adornee=part box.LineThickness=0.05 box.SurfaceTransparency=0.8 box.Transparency=0.1 box.Parent=part end
                    box.Visible=Hitbox.Visible; box.Color3=Hitbox.Color; box.SurfaceColor3=Hitbox.Color
                end
            end
        end
    end))
end

local ESP = { Enabled=false, Names=true, Boxes=true, Health=true, Chams=false, MaxDist=2000, Pool={}, Maid=Maid.new() }
local function makeDraw()
    local box=Drawing.new("Square"); box.Thickness,box.Filled,box.Transparency,box.Visible=1.5,false,1,false
    local name=Drawing.new("Text"); name.Size,name.Center,name.Outline,name.Visible=13,true,true,false
    local hp=Drawing.new("Line"); hp.Thickness,hp.Visible=3,false
    return { box=box, name=name, hp=hp, cham=nil }
end
local function hideDraw(d) d.box.Visible,d.name.Visible,d.hp.Visible=false,false,false end
local function removeDraw(plr)
    local d=ESP.Pool[plr]; if not d then return end
    d.box:Remove(); d.name:Remove(); d.hp:Remove(); if d.cham then d.cham:Destroy() end
    ESP.Pool[plr]=nil
end
local function updateDraw(plr,d,myPos)
    local char=plr.Character; local hrp=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health<=0 then hideDraw(d) if d.cham then d.cham.Enabled=false end return end
    if ESP.Chams then
        if not d.cham or d.cham.Adornee~=char then if d.cham then d.cham:Destroy() end
            d.cham=Instance.new("Highlight"); d.cham.FillColor=Color3.fromRGB(255,70,70); d.cham.OutlineColor=Color3.new(1,1,1)
            d.cham.FillTransparency=0.55; d.cham.DepthMode=Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop; d.cham.Adornee=char; d.cham.Parent=char end
        d.cham.Enabled=true
    elseif d.cham then d.cham.Enabled=false end
    local pos,on=Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
    local dist=myPos and (myPos-hrp.Position).Magnitude or 0
    if not on or dist>ESP.MaxDist then hideDraw(d) return end
    local sz=Vector2.new(1800/pos.Z,2600/pos.Z); local tl=Vector2.new(pos.X-sz.X/2,pos.Y-sz.Y/2)
    local col=plr.TeamColor.Color; d.box.Color,d.name.Color=col,col
    d.box.Visible=ESP.Boxes; if ESP.Boxes then d.box.Size,d.box.Position=sz,tl end
    d.name.Visible=ESP.Names; if ESP.Names then d.name.Text=string.format("%s  [%d]",plr.Name,math.floor(dist)); d.name.Position=Vector2.new(pos.X,tl.Y-16) end
    d.hp.Visible=ESP.Health
    if ESP.Health then local r=hum.Health/math.max(hum.MaxHealth,1)
        d.hp.Color=Color3.fromRGB(255*(1-r),255*r,0); d.hp.From=Vector2.new(tl.X-5,tl.Y+sz.Y); d.hp.To=Vector2.new(tl.X-5,tl.Y+sz.Y-sz.Y*r) end
end
function ESP.Set(on)
    ESP.Enabled=on; ESP.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then for plr in pairs(ESP.Pool) do removeDraw(plr) end return end
    for _,plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if plr~=LocalPlayer then ESP.Pool[plr]=makeDraw() end end
    ESP.Maid:Give(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr) if plr~=LocalPlayer then ESP.Pool[plr]=makeDraw() end end))
    ESP.Maid:Give(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(removeDraw))
    ESP.Acc=0
    ESP.Maid:Give(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        ESP.Acc=ESP.Acc+dt; if ESP.Acc<0.03 then return end; ESP.Acc=0
        local mr=Char.HRP(); local mp=mr and mr.Position
        for plr,d in pairs(ESP.Pool) do if plr.Parent then updateDraw(plr,d,mp) else removeDraw(plr) end end
    end))
end

local TownESP = { Enabled=false, MaxSize=46, MinSize=10, Growth=0.004, RefreshRate=2, Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,205,70),
    Draw={}, Towns={}, NameCache={}, Acc=0, Maid=Maid.new() }
local GEN={townhall=true,town=true,hall=true,model=true,part=true,folder=true,spawn=true,base=true,
    section=true,title=true,titleshadow=true,label=true,textlabel=true,frame=true,button=true,
    text=true,content=true,tier=true,jobs=true,name=true,owner=true,warning=true,value=true,[""]=true}
local function looksGeneric(s) if type(s)~="string" then return true end s=s:gsub("^%s+",""):gsub("%s+$","") if #s==0 or #s>40 then return true end return GEN[(s:lower():gsub("%s+",""):gsub("%d+$",""))]==true end
local function resolveTownName(o)
    local root=(o:IsA("Model") and o) or o.Parent or o
    local teamVal=root:FindFirstChild("Team")
    if not teamVal then for _,d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do if d.Name=="Team" then teamVal=d break end end end
    if teamVal then
        local ok,v=pcall(function() return teamVal.Value end)
        if ok and v~=nil then
            if typeof(v)=="Instance" then return v.Name
            elseif type(v)=="string" and #v>0 then return v end
        end
    end
    return o.Name
end
local function townName(o) return TownESP.NameCache[o] or o.Name end
local function townPos(o)
    if o:IsA("BasePart") then return o.Position end
    if o:IsA("Model") then local ok,cf=pcall(function() return o:GetPivot() end) if ok then return cf.Position end if o.PrimaryPart then return o.PrimaryPart.Position end end
    local bp=o:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true); return bp and bp.Position or nil
end
local function collectTowns()
    local set={}
    local function add(o) if o and o.Parent then set[o]=true end end
    for _,nm in ipairs({"Towns","Civilizations","Cities","Civs","Settlements"}) do local c=Workspace:FindFirstChild(nm) if c then for _,m in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do add(m) end end end
    for _,tg in ipairs({"Town","Civ","Civilization","City","Settlement"}) do local ok,tagged=pcall(function() return CollectionService:GetTagged(tg) end) if ok then for _,o in ipairs(tagged) do add(o) end end end
    for _,o in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do local n=o.Name:lower() if n:find("town") or n:find("civ") or n:find("settlement") then add(o) end end
    local list={}; for o in pairs(set) do list[#list+1]=o end; return list
end
local function projectClamped(wp)
    local vpp=Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(wp); local vs=Camera.ViewportSize; local margin=44
    local x,y,z=vpp.X,vpp.Y,vpp.Z
    local on=z>0 and x>=0 and x<=vs.X and y>=0 and y<=vs.Y
    if z<=0 then x=vs.X-x; y=vs.Y-y end
    if not on then local cx,cy=vs.X/2,vs.Y/2 local dx,dy=x-cx,y-cy if dx==0 and dy==0 then dy=-1 end
        local sc=math.max(math.abs(dx)/math.max(cx-margin,1),math.abs(dy)/math.max(cy-margin,1)) if sc>0 then x=cx+dx/sc; y=cy+dy/sc end end
    return Vector2.new(x,y),on
end
local function makeTownDraw() local t=Drawing.new("Text") t.Size=14 t.Center=true t.Outline=true t.Font=2 t.Color=TownESP.Color t.Visible=false return t end
function TownESP.Set(on)
    TownESP.Enabled=on; TownESP.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then for o,d in pairs(TownESP.Draw) do pcall(function() d:Remove() end) end TownESP.Draw={} TownESP.Towns={} TownESP.NameCache={} return end
    TownESP.Towns=collectTowns()
    TownESP.Maid:Give(task.spawn(function()
        while TownESP.Enabled do
            TownESP.Towns=collectTowns()
            for _,o in ipairs(TownESP.Towns) do if not TownESP.Draw[o] then TownESP.Draw[o]=makeTownDraw() end local ok,nm=pcall(resolveTownName,o) TownESP.NameCache[o]=ok and nm or o.Name end
            for o in pairs(TownESP.Draw) do if not o.Parent then pcall(function() TownESP.Draw[o]:Remove() end) TownESP.Draw[o]=nil TownESP.NameCache[o]=nil end end
            task.wait(TownESP.RefreshRate)
        end
    end))
    TownESP.Acc=0
    TownESP.Maid:Give(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        TownESP.Acc=TownESP.Acc+dt; if TownESP.Acc<0.05 then return end; TownESP.Acc=0
        local mr=Char.HRP(); local mp=mr and mr.Position
        for _,o in ipairs(TownESP.Towns) do local d=TownESP.Draw[o]
            if d then local wp=o.Parent and townPos(o)
                if wp then local scr,on=projectClamped(wp) local dist=mp and (mp-wp).Magnitude or 0
                    d.Text=string.format("%s%s  [%dm]",on and "" or "-> ",townName(o),math.floor(dist))
                    d.Size=math.clamp(TownESP.MinSize+dist*TownESP.Growth,TownESP.MinSize,TownESP.MaxSize)
                    d.Position=scr; d.Visible=true
                else d.Visible=false end end
        end
    end))
end

local nearestTownHallPos
local Sapling = { Item="Sapling", Radius=20, Ring=false, RingRadius=14, RingCount=20, RingDone={}, Auto=false, Interval=2.0,
    Cache={}, MouseMaid=Maid.new(), AutoMaid=Maid.new(), RingMaid=Maid.new() }
local function getDeployable(name)
    local mat=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Materials"); local m=mat and mat:FindFirstChild(name) if m then return m end
    local dep=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Deployables"); return dep and dep:FindFirstChild(name)
end
local function templateInfo(name)
    local ci=Sapling.Cache[name]; if ci then return ci end
    local t=getDeployable(name); if not t then return nil end
    local okE,extY=pcall(function() return t:GetExtentsSize().Y/2 end)
    local base=t:FindFirstChild("Base"); local baseOffset
    if base then pcall(function() baseOffset=t:GetPivot():Inverse()*base.CFrame end) end
    ci={extY=(okE and extY) or 2, baseOffset=baseOffset}; Sapling.Cache[name]=ci; return ci
end
local function groundAt(pos)
    local rp=RaycastParams.new(); rp.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local ignore={} if Char.Get() then ignore[#ignore+1]=Char.Get() end
    local mi=Workspace:FindFirstChild("MouseIgnore") if mi then ignore[#ignore+1]=mi end
    local pb=Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlacingBuild") if pb then ignore[#ignore+1]=pb end
    rp.FilterDescendantsInstances=ignore
    local hit=Workspace:Raycast(pos+Vector3.new(0,200,0),Vector3.new(0,-1000,0),rp)
    return hit and hit.Position or nil
end
local function plantAt(pos,name)
    name=name or Sapling.Item
    if not Deploy then return false,"Deploy remote missing" end
    local hrp=Char.HRP(); if not hrp then return false,"no character" end
    if (hrp.Position-pos).Magnitude>49 then return false,"too far (server caps at 50)" end
    local info=templateInfo(name); if not info then return false,"unknown deployable: "..name end
    local rot=hrp.CFrame.Rotation
    local pivotCF=CFrame.new(pos)*CFrame.new(0,info.extY,0)*rot
    local baseCF=info.baseOffset and (pivotCF*info.baseOffset) or false
    local ok,res=pcall(function() return Deploy:InvokeServer(name,pivotCF,baseCF) end)
    return ((ok and res)==true),res
end
local function airFallback(pos)
    local hrp=Char.HRP()
    local base=(hrp and hrp.Position.Y) or pos.Y
    return Vector3.new(pos.X, base+2, pos.Z)
end
local function plantSmart(pos)
    local g=groundAt(pos) or airFallback(pos)
    local ok,res=plantAt(g,Sapling.Item)
    if not ok then ok,res=plantAt(airFallback(pos),Sapling.Item) end
    return ok,res
end
function Sapling.PlantHere()
    local hrp=Char.HRP(); if not hrp then window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="No character",Duration=2}) return end
    local ok,res=plantSmart(hrp.Position)
    window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description=ok and "Planted where you stand" or ("Plant failed: "..tostring(res)),Duration=2})
end
function Sapling.PlantAtTown()
    local hrp=Char.HRP(); if not hrp then window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="No character",Duration=2}) return end
    local center=nearestTownHallPos()
    if not center then window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="No town hall found",Duration=2}) return end
    if (center-hrp.Position).Magnitude>49 then window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="Move closer to town (server caps at 50)",Duration=2}) return end
    local ok,res=plantSmart(center)
    window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description=ok and "Planted at town root" or ("Plant failed: "..tostring(res)),Duration=2})
end
function nearestTownHallPos()
    local hrp=Char.HRP(); if not hrp then return nil end
    local best,bd=nil,math.huge
    local function consider(pos) if pos then local d=(pos-hrp.Position).Magnitude if d<bd then bd,best=d,pos end end end
    for _,o in ipairs(collectTowns()) do consider(townPos(o)) end
    local function scan(cont,depth)
        for _,o in ipairs(cont:GetChildren()) do
            local n=o.Name:lower()
            if n:find("townhall") or n:find("town hall") then consider(townPos(o)) end
            if depth>0 and (o:IsA("Folder") or o:IsA("Model")) then scan(o,depth-1) end
        end
    end
    scan(Workspace,1)
    return best
end
function Sapling.PlantNearMe()
    local hrp=Char.HRP(); if not hrp then window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="No character",Duration=2}) return end
    local ok,res=plantSmart(hrp.Position+hrp.CFrame.LookVector*6)
    window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description=ok and "Planted sapling" or ("Plant failed: "..tostring(res)),Duration=2})
end
function Sapling.SetMouse(on)
    Sapling.MouseMaid:Clean()
    if not on then return end
    Sapling.MouseMaid:Give(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp,gpe)
        if gpe then return end
        if inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            local pos=Mouse.Hit and Mouse.Hit.Position
            if pos then local ok,res=plantAt(pos,Sapling.Item) if not ok then window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="Plant failed: "..tostring(res),Duration=2}) end end
        end
    end))
    Sapling.MouseMaid:Give(UserInputService.TouchTapInWorld:Connect(function(pos, processed)
        if processed then return end
        local ray=Camera:ScreenPointToRay(pos.X,pos.Y)
        local rp=RaycastParams.new(); rp.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        rp.FilterDescendantsInstances={ Char.Get() or Workspace.CurrentCamera }
        local hit=Workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin,ray.Direction*1000,rp)
        if hit then local ok,res=plantAt(hit.Position,Sapling.Item) if not ok then window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="Plant failed: "..tostring(res),Duration=2}) end end
    end))
end
function Sapling.SetAuto(on)
    Sapling.Auto=on; Sapling.AutoMaid:Clean()
    if not on then return end
    Sapling.AutoMaid:Give(task.spawn(function()
        while Sapling.Auto do
            local hrp=Char.HRP()
            if hrp then
                local ang=RNG:NextNumber(0,math.pi*2); local rad=RNG:NextNumber(3,Sapling.Radius)
                local target=hrp.Position+Vector3.new(math.cos(ang)*rad,0,math.sin(ang)*rad)
                if (target-hrp.Position).Magnitude>40 then target=hrp.Position+(target-hrp.Position).Unit*40 end
                plantSmart(target)
            end
            task.wait(math.max(Sapling.Interval,0.5))
        end
    end))
end
function Sapling.SetRing(on)
    Sapling.Ring=on; Sapling.RingMaid:Clean()
    if not on then Sapling.RingDone={} return end
    Sapling.RingDone={}
    Sapling.RingMaid:Give(task.spawn(function()
        while Sapling.Ring do
            local hrp=Char.HRP(); local center=nearestTownHallPos() or (hrp and hrp.Position)
            if hrp and center then
                local n=math.max(math.floor(Sapling.RingCount),3)
                for i=0,n-1 do
                    if not Sapling.Ring then break end
                    local ang=(i/n)*math.pi*2
                    local p=center+Vector3.new(math.cos(ang)*Sapling.RingRadius,0,math.sin(ang)*Sapling.RingRadius)
                    local key=math.floor(p.X/2)..","..math.floor(p.Z/2)
                    if not Sapling.RingDone[key] and (p-hrp.Position).Magnitude<=44 then
                        local g=groundAt(p) or Vector3.new(p.X,hrp.Position.Y,p.Z)
                        local ok=plantAt(g,Sapling.Item) if ok then Sapling.RingDone[key]=true task.wait(0.3) end
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(math.max(Sapling.Interval,0.6))
        end
    end))
end

local Merchant = { Auto=false, Interval=1.5, Maid=Maid.new() }
local TradeCats={"Coins","Coins2","Pouch","Misc","Food","Good Food","Equipment","Dye"}
local function nearestTrader()
    local map=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map"); local merchants=map and map:FindFirstChild("Merchants"); local hrp=Char.HRP()
    if not merchants or not hrp then return nil end
    local best,bd=nil,math.huge
    for _,m in ipairs(merchants:GetChildren()) do local npc=m:FindFirstChild("NPC")
        if npc then local ok,d=pcall(function() return (hrp.Position-npc:GetPivot().Position).Magnitude end) if ok and d<bd then bd,best=d,m end end end
    if best and bd<=30 then return best end
    return nil
end
local function goodNode(tr,cat) local g=tr:FindFirstChild("Goods") return g and g:FindFirstChild(cat) end
local function tradeAffordable(good)
    local input=good:FindFirstChild("Input"); if not input then return false end
    local val=input:FindFirstChild("Value"); local nm=val and val.Name; local qty=input:GetAttribute("Quantity") or 0
    if not nm then return false end
    local bp=LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack"); if not bp then return false end
    local c=0 for _,it in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do if it.Name==nm then c=c+1 end end
    return c>=qty
end
local function tradeInStock(good) local o=good:FindFirstChild("Output"); local s=o and o:GetAttribute("Stock"); return (s or 0)>0 end
function Merchant.TradeAll()
    local tr=nearestTrader(); if not tr then window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="No merchant within 30 studs",Duration=2}) return end
    local n=0 for _,cat in ipairs(TradeCats) do local g=goodNode(tr,cat) if g and tradeInStock(g) and tradeAffordable(g) then pcall(function() MerchantTrade:FireServer(tr,g) end) n=n+1 task.wait(0.3) end end
    window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description=n>0 and ("Traded "..n.." affordable") or "Nothing affordable",Duration=2})
end
function Merchant.SetAuto(on)
    Merchant.Auto=on; Merchant.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then return end
    Merchant.Maid:Give(task.spawn(function()
        while Merchant.Auto do
            local tr=nearestTrader()
            if tr then for _,cat in ipairs(TradeCats) do if not Merchant.Auto then break end local g=goodNode(tr,cat)
                if g and tradeInStock(g) and tradeAffordable(g) then pcall(function() MerchantTrade:FireServer(tr,g) end) task.wait(0.3) end end end
            task.wait(math.max(Merchant.Interval,0.5))
        end
    end))
end

local function joinTeam(team)
    local lc=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("LoadCharacter")
    if not lc then window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="LoadCharacter missing",Duration=2}) return end
    if team==nil then team=false end
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok,res,msg=pcall(function() return lc:InvokeServer(team) end)
        window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description=ok and (res==false and ("Failed: "..tostring(msg or "denied")) or "Joined / respawned") or ("Error: "..tostring(res)),Duration=3})
    end)
end
local function nearestJobBoardPos()
    local hrp=Char.HRP(); if not hrp then return nil end
    local best,bd
    local function consider(o) local n=o.Name:lower()
        if n:find("jobboard") or (n:find("job") and n:find("board")) or n=="board" then
            local pos if o:IsA("BasePart") then pos=o.Position elseif o:IsA("Model") then local ok,cf=pcall(function() return o:GetPivot() end) pos=ok and cf.Position end
            if pos then local d=(pos-hrp.Position).Magnitude if not best or d<bd then best,bd=pos,d end end
        end
    end
    local roots={Workspace}; local map=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") if map then roots[#roots+1]=map end
    for _,root in ipairs(roots) do for _,o in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do consider(o) if o:IsA("Folder") or o:IsA("Model") then for _,c in ipairs(o:GetChildren()) do consider(c) end end end end
    return best
end
local function requestJob(job)
    local rem=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("ChangeJob") or ChangeJob
    if not rem then window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="ChangeJob missing",Duration=2}) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        local hrp=Char.HRP(); local back=hrp and hrp.CFrame; local board=nearestJobBoardPos()
        if hrp and board and (board-hrp.Position).Magnitude>12 then hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(board+Vector3.new(0,3,0)) task.wait(0.35) end
        pcall(function() rem:FireServer(job) end)
        task.wait(0.3)
        local r=Char.HRP() if back and r then r.CFrame=back end
        window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="Requested job: "..job.Name.." (needs XP)",Duration=3})
    end)
end

local RockESP = { Enabled=false, MaxDist=1400, MaxDrawn=80, RefreshRate=1.5, DirectWorkspace=false, Color=Color3.fromRGB(190,190,195), Rocks={}, Draw={}, Acc=0, Maid=Maid.new() }
local ROCK_KEYS={"stone node","gold node","marble node","copper node","stone","marble","rock","ore","node","boulder","mineral","gem","crystal","iron","coal","copper","gold","silver","tin","clay"}
local function rockPos(o)
    if o:IsA("BasePart") then return o.Position end
    if o:IsA("Model") then local ok,cf=pcall(function() return o:GetPivot() end) if ok then return cf.Position end if o.PrimaryPart then return o.PrimaryPart.Position end end
    local bp=o:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true) return bp and bp.Position or nil
end
local function isRockName(n) n=n:lower() for _,k in ipairs(ROCK_KEYS) do if n:find(k) then return true end end return false end
local function collectRocks()
    local out={}
    if RockESP.DirectWorkspace then
        for _,o in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if (o:IsA("Model") or o:IsA("BasePart")) and isRockName(o.Name) then out[#out+1]=o end
        end
    else
        local rocks=findPath(Workspace,"Map","Resources","Rocks")
        if rocks then for _,o in ipairs(rocks:GetChildren()) do out[#out+1]=o end end
    end
    return out
end
function RockESP.Set(on)
    RockESP.Enabled=on; RockESP.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then for _,d in ipairs(RockESP.Draw) do pcall(function() d:Remove() end) end RockESP.Draw={} RockESP.Rocks={} return end
    for i=1,RockESP.MaxDrawn do local t=Drawing.new("Text") t.Size=13 t.Center=true t.Outline=true t.Font=2 t.Color=RockESP.Color t.Visible=false RockESP.Draw[i]=t end
    RockESP.Rocks=collectRocks()
    RockESP.Maid:Give(task.spawn(function()
        while RockESP.Enabled do RockESP.Rocks=collectRocks() task.wait(RockESP.RefreshRate) end
    end))
    RockESP.Acc=0
    RockESP.Maid:Give(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        RockESP.Acc=RockESP.Acc+dt if RockESP.Acc<0.05 then return end RockESP.Acc=0
        for _,d in ipairs(RockESP.Draw) do d.Visible=false end
        local mr=Char.HRP() local mp=mr and mr.Position if not mp then return end
        local cand={}
        for _,o in ipairs(RockESP.Rocks) do if o.Parent then local wp=rockPos(o) if wp then local dd=(wp-mp).Magnitude if dd<=RockESP.MaxDist then cand[#cand+1]={o=o,wp=wp,d=dd} end end end end
        table.sort(cand,function(a,b) return a.d<b.d end)
        local n=math.min(#cand,RockESP.MaxDrawn)
        for i=1,n do local it=cand[i] local vp,on=Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(it.wp) local d=RockESP.Draw[i]
            if d and on and vp.Z>0 then d.Text=string.format("%s  [%dm]",it.o.Name,math.floor(it.d)) d.Position=Vector2.new(vp.X,vp.Y) d.Visible=true end
        end
    end))
end

local lootLabelToName = {}
local LootESP = { Enabled=false, Selected="*", ShowDistance=true, MaxDist=2000, MaxDrawn=100, RefreshRate=1.5, Color=Color3.fromRGB(120,225,255), Items={}, Draw={}, Acc=0, Maid=Maid.new() }
local function collectLoot()
    local out={}
    for _,c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
        local base=c.Name:match("^(.-)_Replica$")
        if base or c:IsA("Tool") then
            local part=c:FindFirstChild("Handle") or c:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
            if part then
                out[#out+1]={ prompt=part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt"), part=part, model=c, name=base or c.Name }
            end
        end
    end
    return out
end
local function buildLootNames()
    local counts, order={}, {}
    for _,it in ipairs(collectLoot()) do
        if counts[it.name]==nil then order[#order+1]=it.name end
        counts[it.name]=(counts[it.name] or 0)+1
    end
    table.sort(order)
    lootLabelToName={ ["All Items"]="*" }
    local names={"All Items"}
    for _,nm in ipairs(order) do
        local label=string.format("%s (%d)", nm, counts[nm])
        lootLabelToName[label]=nm
        names[#names+1]=label
    end
    return names
end
function LootESP.Set(on)
    LootESP.Enabled=on; LootESP.Maid:Clean()
    if not on then for _,d in ipairs(LootESP.Draw) do pcall(function() d:Remove() end) end LootESP.Draw={} LootESP.Items={} return end
    for i=1,LootESP.MaxDrawn do local t=Drawing.new("Text") t.Size=13 t.Center=true t.Outline=true t.Font=2 t.Color=LootESP.Color t.Visible=false LootESP.Draw[i]=t end
    LootESP.Items=collectLoot()
    LootESP.Maid:Give(task.spawn(function()
        while LootESP.Enabled do LootESP.Items=collectLoot() task.wait(LootESP.RefreshRate) end
    end))
    LootESP.Acc=0
    LootESP.Maid:Give(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        LootESP.Acc=LootESP.Acc+dt if LootESP.Acc<0.05 then return end LootESP.Acc=0
        for _,d in ipairs(LootESP.Draw) do d.Visible=false end
        local mr=Char.HRP() local mp=mr and mr.Position if not mp then return end
        local sel=LootESP.Selected
        local cand={}
        for _,it in ipairs(LootESP.Items) do
            if it.part and it.part.Parent and (sel=="*" or it.name==sel) then
                local wp=it.part.Position local dd=(wp-mp).Magnitude
                if dd<=LootESP.MaxDist then cand[#cand+1]={it=it, wp=wp, d=dd} end
            end
        end
        table.sort(cand,function(a,b) return a.d<b.d end)
        local n=math.min(#cand,LootESP.MaxDrawn)
        for i=1,n do local c=cand[i] local vp,vis=Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(c.wp) local d=LootESP.Draw[i]
            if d and vis and vp.Z>0 then
                if LootESP.ShowDistance then d.Text=string.format("%s  [%dm]",c.it.name,math.floor(c.d)) else d.Text=c.it.name end
                d.Position=Vector2.new(vp.X,vp.Y) d.Visible=true
            end
        end
    end))
end

local function withdrawAllStorage()
    local drop = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DropStorage")
    if not drop then window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="DropStorage remote missing",Duration=2}) return end
    local sg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local storage = sg and sg:FindFirstChild("Storage")
    local items = storage and findPath(storage,"Frame","Items")
    if not items then window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="Open a storage chest first",Duration=2}) return end
    local list={}
    for _,fr in ipairs(items:GetChildren()) do
        if fr:IsA("Frame") then local si=fr:FindFirstChild("StoredItem") if si then list[#list+1]=si end end
    end
    for _,si in ipairs(list) do pcall(function() drop:FireServer(si) end) end
    window:Notify({Title="LedZ",Description="Withdrew "..#list.." item(s)",Duration=2})
end

local Announce = { Log={}, Max=14, Para=nil, Conn=nil }
local function announceText()
    if #Announce.Log==0 then return "No announcements captured yet." end
    local lines={}
    for i=#Announce.Log,1,-1 do lines[#lines+1]=Announce.Log[i] end
    return table.concat(lines,"\n")
end
local function startAnnounceLog()
    if Announce.Conn then return end
    local ev=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Announce")
    if not ev then return end
    Announce.Conn=ev.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(msg,leader,avatar,plr)
        local line=string.format("[%s] %s", tostring(leader or "?"), tostring(msg or ""))
        Announce.Log[#Announce.Log+1]=line
        while #Announce.Log>Announce.Max do table.remove(Announce.Log,1) end
        if Announce.Para then pcall(function() Announce.Para:SetContent(announceText()) end) end
    end)
end

local CZ = (function()
	local RS = ReplicatedStorage
	local UIS = UserInputService
	local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
	local PathfindingService = game:GetService("PathfindingService")
	local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
	local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local VirtualUser = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
	local lp = LocalPlayer
	local mouse = Mouse

	local ResourceInfo = RS:WaitForChild("ResourceInfo", 15)
	local DeployRem = RS:WaitForChild("Deploy", 15)
	local CraftItem = RS:WaitForChild("CraftItem", 15)
	local ChangeJobRem = RS:WaitForChild("ChangeJob", 15)
	local Craftables = RS:FindFirstChild("Craftables")
	local JobsFolder = RS:FindFirstChild("Jobs")

	local API = {}
	local alive = true
	local conns = {}
	local function on(sig, fn)
		local c = sig:Connect(fn)
		table.insert(conns, c)
		return c
	end
	local function notify(title, content, dur)
		pcall(function() window:Notify({ Title = title, Description = content, Duration = dur or 4 }) end)
	end
	local function log(...) print("[LedZ farm]", ...) end
	local DEBUG = { on = false }
	local function dlog(...) if DEBUG.on then print("[LedZ walk]", ...) end end

	if not (ResourceInfo and DeployRem and CraftItem and ChangeJobRem) then
		warn("[LedZ farm] game remotes missing (ResourceInfo/Deploy/CraftItem/ChangeJob); farm engine disabled")
		API.ok = false
		API.destroy = function() end
		return API
	end
	API.ok = true

	local S = {
		farm = false, farmTarget = "Tree", farmRange = 600, replant = true, pick = {}, autoPickup = false, aura = false,
		homeMode = true, homeMaxDist = 1200, avoidSea = true, plantNearHall = true, hallPlantMin = 25, haul = true, haulEvery = 10, buildOnlyMine = true,
		autoLoot = false, lootRadius = 25, lootHold = 0.1, lootUpgradesOnly = true,
		goalOn = false, goalItem = "Spear", goalQty = 1,
		plantCooldown = 0.5, retryStep = 3, retryRadius = 15, maxTries = 12, spacing = 8,
		speed = true, speedExtra = 6, autoCalibrate = true, adaptive = true, calibrated = false,
		espOn = false, esp = {}, espSaplings = false, espRange = 600, espMax = 40,
		fullbright = false, noFog = false, fovOn = false, fov = 90,
		antiAfk = true, place = "Well",
		status = "idle", goalStatus = "idle",
	}
	local NEVER_PICK = { Stick = true, Log = true, Rock = true }
	local function setPhase(text)
		if S.goalOn then S.goalStatus = text else S.status = text end
	end
	for _, o in ipairs({ "Copper", "Tin", "Iron", "Gold", "Marble" }) do S.esp[o] = true end

	local function otherMovementOn()
		return Ghost.Enabled or CFSpeed.Enabled or Fly.Enabled or CFFly.Enabled
	end

	local RECIPES = {}
	if Craftables then
		for _, c in ipairs(Craftables:GetChildren()) do
			local req = {}
			local r = c:FindFirstChild("Requirements")
			if r then
				for _, v in ipairs(r:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("IntValue") or v:IsA("NumberValue") then req[v.Name] = v.Value end
				end
			end
			local st, job = c:FindFirstChild("Station"), c:FindFirstChild("Job")
			RECIPES[c.Name] = { inst = c, req = req, station = st and st.Value or 1, job = job and job.Value or "" }
		end
	end

	local SKIP_NODE = { Cow = true, Sheep = true, ["Iron Sword"] = true, ["Iron Chestpiece"] = true, ["Iron Helmet 1"] = true }
	local SOURCES = {}
	local LOOT = { Sapling = true, Apple = true }
	for _, ri in ipairs(ResourceInfo:GetChildren()) do
		if not SKIP_NODE[ri.Name] then
			local yields = {}
			local loot = ri:FindFirstChild("Loot")
			local sickled = ri:FindFirstChild("Sickled")
			if loot and not (sickled and sickled.Value) then
				for _, v in ipairs(loot:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("ObjectValue") and v.Value then yields[v.Value.Name] = (yields[v.Value.Name] or 0) + 1 end
				end
			end
			local obj = ri:FindFirstChild("Object")
			if obj and obj:IsA("ObjectValue") and obj.Value then yields[obj.Value.Name] = (yields[obj.Value.Name] or 0) + 1 end
			for item, n in pairs(yields) do
				LOOT[item] = true
				SOURCES[item] = SOURCES[item] or {}
				table.insert(SOURCES[item], { node = ri.Name, yield = n })
			end
			for _, k in ipairs({ "Seed", "ChanceFood" }) do
				local v = ri:FindFirstChild(k)
				if v and v:IsA("ObjectValue") and v.Value then LOOT[v.Value.Name] = true end
			end
		end
	end
	local LOOT_LIST = {}
	for k in pairs(LOOT) do table.insert(LOOT_LIST, k) end
	table.sort(LOOT_LIST)
	local DEFAULT_PICK = {}
	for _, n in ipairs(LOOT_LIST) do
		if not NEVER_PICK[n] then
			S.pick[n] = true
			table.insert(DEFAULT_PICK, n)
		end
	end

	local CRAFT_LIST, JOB_LIST = {}, {}
	for name in pairs(RECIPES) do table.insert(CRAFT_LIST, name) end
	if JobsFolder then for _, j in ipairs(JobsFolder:GetChildren()) do table.insert(JOB_LIST, j.Name) end end
	table.sort(CRAFT_LIST)
	table.sort(JOB_LIST)
	if #CRAFT_LIST == 0 then CRAFT_LIST = { "(none)" } end
	if #JOB_LIST == 0 then JOB_LIST = { "(none)" } end
	if not RECIPES[S.goalItem] then S.goalItem = CRAFT_LIST[1] end

	local FARMABLE = { "Tree", "Palm Tree", "Bush", "Stone", "Copper", "Tin", "Iron", "Gold", "Marble", "Cactus", "Fiber Plant", "Wheat Plant", "Corn Plant", "Carrot Plant", "Pumpkin Plant" }
	for i = #FARMABLE, 1, -1 do if not ResourceInfo:FindFirstChild(FARMABLE[i]) then table.remove(FARMABLE, i) end end
	local COLORS = {
		Tree = Color3.fromRGB(95, 200, 95), Stone = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 170), Copper = Color3.fromRGB(215, 120, 60),
		Tin = Color3.fromRGB(190, 205, 215), Iron = Color3.fromRGB(150, 115, 100), Gold = Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 50),
		Marble = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 255), Cactus = Color3.fromRGB(60, 160, 90), ["Fiber Plant"] = Color3.fromRGB(200, 230, 120),
		["Wheat Plant"] = Color3.fromRGB(230, 200, 110), ["Corn Plant"] = Color3.fromRGB(240, 220, 80),
		["Palm Tree"] = Color3.fromRGB(150, 210, 90), Bush = Color3.fromRGB(80, 170, 80), ["Carrot Plant"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 40),
		["Pumpkin Plant"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 117, 24),
		Sapling = Color3.fromRGB(140, 255, 140),
	}
	local PLACES = { "Well", "Campfire", "Crafting Table", "Crafting Table 2", "Crafting Table 3", "Tanning Rack", "Loom", "Blacksmith Shop", "Mortar", "Windmill", "Coin Press" }

	local nodes, nodePos, saplings = {}, {}, {}
	local function classify(m)
		local cn = m.ClassName
		if cn ~= "Model" and cn ~= "Actor" then return end
		local info = m:FindFirstChild("Info")
		if info and info:IsA("ObjectValue") and info.Value then
			local t = info.Value.Name
			if ResourceInfo:FindFirstChild(t) and not SKIP_NODE[t] then
				nodes[m] = t
				nodePos[m] = m:GetPivot().Position
			end
		elseif m.Name == "Sapling" and m:FindFirstChild("Mature") then
			saplings[m] = true
		end
	end
	local function unindex(m)
		nodes[m], nodePos[m], saplings[m] = nil, nil, nil
	end
	Maid.sliceT = os.clock()
	for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do classify(c) Maid.Slice() end
	on(Workspace.ChildAdded, function(c)
		task.delay(1, function() if alive and c.Parent == Workspace then classify(c) end end)
	end)
	on(Workspace.ChildRemoved, unindex)
	local mapRes = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("Resources")
	if mapRes then
		for _, d in ipairs(mapRes:GetDescendants()) do classify(d) Maid.Slice() end
		on(mapRes.DescendantAdded, function(d)
			local cn = d.ClassName
			if cn == "Model" or cn == "Actor" then
				task.delay(1, function() if alive and d:IsDescendantOf(mapRes) then classify(d) end end)
			end
		end)
		on(mapRes.DescendantRemoving, unindex)
	end

	local function nodeAlive(m)
		if not m.Parent then return false end
		local hp = m:FindFirstChild("Health")
		return not hp or hp.Value > 0
	end

	local cpCache = {}
	local function charParts()
		local char = lp.Character
		if not char then return end
		local hum, hrp = cpCache.hum, cpCache.hrp
		if cpCache.char ~= char or not (hum and hum.Parent == char and hrp and hrp.Parent == char) then
			hum, hrp = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"), char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			cpCache.char, cpCache.hum, cpCache.hrp = char, hum, hrp
		end
		if hum and hrp and hum.Health > 0 then return char, hum, hrp end
	end
	local function flat(a, b) return Vector3.new(a.X - b.X, 0, a.Z - b.Z).Magnitude end
	local function charVal(name)
		local c = lp.Character
		local v = c and c:FindFirstChild(name)
		return v and v.Value
	end
	local function valuesVal(name)
		local c = lp.Character
		local f = c and c:FindFirstChild("Values")
		local v = f and f:FindFirstChild(name)
		return v and v.Value, v
	end
	local function inCombat()
		return charVal("InCombat") == true
	end
	local function closeGameGuis()
		local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
		for _, n in ipairs({ "Crafting1", "Jobs1" }) do
			local g = pg and pg:FindFirstChild(n)
			if g and g:IsA("ScreenGui") then g.Enabled = false end
		end
	end

	local INV = { cap = 9, glitch = false, paused = 0, stash = {}, parked = {}, useChest = true, parkJunk = true,
		autoGlitch = false, survive = true, hungerMin = 40, thirstMin = 40, PROC = {} }
	do
		local function put(out, p) if out and not RECIPES[out] and not INV.PROC[out] then INV.PROC[out] = p end end
		local LD = RS:FindFirstChild("LootDrops")
		if LD then
			for _, x in ipairs(LD:GetChildren()) do
				local cr = x:FindFirstChild("Crushed")
				if cr and cr:IsA("ObjectValue") and cr.Value then put(cr.Value.Name, { kind = "station", station = "Mortar", input = x.Name }) end
				local sm = x:FindFirstChild("Smelted")
				if sm and sm:IsA("ObjectValue") and sm.Value then
					local fin = sm.Value:FindFirstChild("Finished", true)
					if fin and fin:IsA("ObjectValue") and fin.Value then
						put(fin.Value.Name, { kind = "smelt", input = x.Name, heated = sm.Value.Name, job = "Blacksmith" })
					end
				end
				local hl = x:FindFirstChild("HunterLoot")
				if hl then
					for _, v in ipairs(hl:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("ObjectValue") and v.Value then put(v.Value.Name, { kind = "hunt", carcass = x.Name, animal = (x.Name:gsub(" Carcass$", "")), job = "Hunter" }) end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		for _, ri in ipairs(ResourceInfo:GetChildren()) do
			local sh = ri:FindFirstChild("ShearedLoot")
			if sh and sh:IsA("ObjectValue") and sh.Value then put(sh.Value.Name, { kind = "shear", animal = ri.Name, job = "Farmer" }) end
		end
		put("Leather", { kind = "station", station = "Tanning Rack", input = "Animal Hide", job = "Hunter" })
		put("Cloth", { kind = "station", station = "Loom", input = "Plant Fiber", job = "Tailor" })
		put("Wool Cloth", { kind = "station", station = "Loom", input = "Wool", job = "Tailor" })
		put("Skull", { kind = "none", why = "skulls only drop when a player over 13 dies, so a goal can't farm them" })
	end
	local function allTools()
		local out = {}
		for _, cont in ipairs({ lp:FindFirstChild("Backpack"), lp.Character }) do
			if cont then
				for _, t in ipairs(cont:GetChildren()) do if t:IsA("Tool") then table.insert(out, t) end end
			end
		end
		return out
	end
	local function countItem(name)
		local n = 0
		for _, t in ipairs(allTools()) do if t.Name == name then n = n + 1 end end
		return n
	end
	local function findTool(name)
		for _, t in ipairs(allTools()) do if t.Name == name then return t end end
	end
	local function toolProvides(inst, kind)
		if kind == "Sickle" then
			local anim = inst:FindFirstChild("Animation")
			return inst.Name:find("Sickle") ~= nil or (anim and anim.Value == "SickleSwing") or false
		end
		local ty = inst:FindFirstChild("Type")
		return ty ~= nil and ty:FindFirstChild(kind) ~= nil
	end
	local function bestToolFor(kind)
		local best, bestH = nil, -1
		for _, t in ipairs(allTools()) do
			if toolProvides(t, kind) then
				local h = t:FindFirstChild("Harvest")
				local hv = h and h.Value or 0
				if hv > bestH then best, bestH = t, hv end
			end
		end
		return best
	end
	local function toolKindFor(nodeType)
		local ri = ResourceInfo:FindFirstChild(nodeType)
		if not ri then return nil end
		local sick = ri:FindFirstChild("Sickled")
		if sick and sick.Value then return nil end
		if ri:FindFirstChild("Axe") then return "Axe" end
		if ri:FindFirstChild("Pick") then return "Pick" end
		return nil
	end
	local function equip(tool)
		local char, hum = charParts()
		if not char or not tool or not tool.Parent then return false end
		if tool.Parent ~= char then
			if INV.glitch and INV.paused == 0 then tool.Parent = char else hum:EquipTool(tool) end
			local t0 = os.clock()
			while tool.Parent ~= char and os.clock() - t0 < 1.5 do task.wait(0.05) end
			task.wait(0.15)
		end
		return tool.Parent == char
	end

	function INV.backpackCount()
		local bp = lp:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		local n = 0
		if bp then for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do if t:IsA("Tool") then n = n + 1 end end end
		return n
	end
	function INV.full() return INV.backpackCount() >= INV.cap end
	function INV.pullAll()
		local char, bp = lp.Character, lp:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		if not (char and bp) then return end
		for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do if t:IsA("Tool") and not t:FindFirstChild("Attacks") then t.Parent = char end end
	end
	function INV.pushAll()
		local char, bp = lp.Character, lp:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		if not (char and bp) then return end
		for _, t in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do if t:IsA("Tool") then t.Parent = bp end end
	end
	function INV.setGlitch(on)
		INV.glitch = on and true or false
		if INV.paused > 0 then return end
		if INV.glitch then INV.pullAll() else INV.pushAll() end
	end
	function INV.withBackpack(fn, ...)
		INV.paused = INV.paused + 1
		if INV.glitch then INV.pushAll() task.wait(0.2) end
		local res = table.pack(pcall(fn, ...))
		INV.paused = INV.paused - 1
		if INV.glitch and INV.paused == 0 then INV.pullAll() end
		if not res[1] then error(res[2], 0) end
		return table.unpack(res, 2, res.n)
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		while alive do
			if INV.glitch and INV.paused == 0 then pcall(INV.pullAll) end
			task.wait(0.25)
		end
	end)

	local blacklist = {}
	local function nearestNode(nodeType, from, range)
		local best, bestD = nil, range or math.huge
		local now = os.clock()
		for m, t in pairs(nodes) do
			if t == nodeType and (blacklist[m] or 0) < now then
				local p = nodePos[m]
				local d = p and flat(p, from)
				if d and d < bestD and nodeAlive(m) then best, bestD = m, d end
			end
		end
		return best, bestD
	end
	local function anyNodeOf(nodeType)
		for m, t in pairs(nodes) do
			if t == nodeType and nodeAlive(m) then return true end
		end
		return false
	end

	local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
	rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	local function refreshRay()
		local list = { Workspace.CurrentCamera }
		for _, n in ipairs({ "Characters", "MouseIgnore", "NonCollidableObjects" }) do
			local f = Workspace:FindFirstChild(n)
			if f then table.insert(list, f) end
		end
		if lp.Character then table.insert(list, lp.Character) end
		rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = list
	end
	refreshRay()
	on(lp.CharacterAdded, function() task.wait(1); refreshRay() end)
	local function castSolid(origin, dir)
		local params, skip = rayParams, nil
		for _ = 1, 6 do
			local r = Workspace:Raycast(origin, dir, params)
			if not r then return nil end
			local inst = r.Instance
			if inst:IsA("Terrain") or inst.CanCollide then return r end
			skip = skip or rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances
			table.insert(skip, inst)
			params = RaycastParams.new()
			params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			params.FilterDescendantsInstances = skip
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function groundAt(pos)
		local r = castSolid(pos + Vector3.new(0, 40, 0), Vector3.new(0, -120, 0))
		return r and r.Position
	end
	local Sea = { list = {}, t = -100, rp = RaycastParams.new(), cache = {} }
	Sea.rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
	function Sea.parts()
		if os.clock() - Sea.t > 10 then
			Sea.t = os.clock()
			Sea.list = {}
			for _, p in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("Water")) do
				if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name == "Saltwater" then Sea.list[#Sea.list + 1] = p end
			end
			Sea.rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = Sea.list
		end
		return Sea.list
	end
	function Sea.at(p)
		if #Sea.parts() == 0 then return false end
		local r = Workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(p.X, 300, p.Z), Vector3.new(0, -600, 0), Sea.rp)
		if not r then return false end
		local g = castSolid(Vector3.new(p.X, r.Position.Y + 2, p.Z), Vector3.new(0, -80, 0))
		return not g or g.Position.Y < r.Position.Y - 1.5
	end
	function Sea.crosses(a, b)
		local d = flat(a, b)
		if d < 60 or #Sea.parts() == 0 then return false end
		local key = math.floor(a.X / 20) .. "," .. math.floor(a.Z / 20) .. ">" .. math.floor(b.X / 20) .. "," .. math.floor(b.Z / 20)
		local c = Sea.cache[key]
		if c and os.clock() - c.t < 60 then return c.v end
		local steps = math.clamp(math.floor(d / 20), 3, 250)
		local run, hit = 0, false
		for i = 1, steps - 1 do
			local p = a:Lerp(b, i / steps)
			if Sea.at(p) then
				run = run + d / steps
				if run >= 50 then hit = true break end
			else
				run = 0
			end
		end
		Sea.cache[key] = { v = hit, t = os.clock() }
		return hit
	end
	local function nearestReachable(nodeType, from, range, me)
		if not me or #Sea.parts() == 0 then return nearestNode(nodeType, from, range) end
		local now, list = os.clock(), {}
		for m, t in pairs(nodes) do
			if t == nodeType and (blacklist[m] or 0) < now then
				local p = nodePos[m]
				local d = p and flat(p, from)
				if d and d < (range or math.huge) then list[#list + 1] = { m = m, d = d, p = p } end
			end
		end
		table.sort(list, function(x, y) return x.d < y.d end)
		local checked = 0
		for _, x in ipairs(list) do
			if nodeAlive(x.m) then
				if not Sea.crosses(me, x.p) then return x.m, x.d end
				checked = checked + 1
				if checked >= 12 then break end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function wallHit(r) return r ~= nil and r.Normal.Y < 0.6 end
	local function blockedDir(origin, dir, dist)
		if wallHit(castSolid(origin, dir * dist)) then return true end
		local g = castSolid(origin, Vector3.new(0, -12, 0))
		local kneeY = g and (g.Position.Y + 1.1) or (origin.Y - 1.6)
		if kneeY >= origin.Y - 0.25 then return false end
		return wallHit(castSolid(Vector3.new(origin.X, kneeY, origin.Z), dir * dist))
	end

	local moveOverride
	local lastAutoMoveT = -100
	local BoostStats = {}
	API.BoostStats = BoostStats
	local expectedPos, expectedHrp, snapCooldown = nil, nil, 0
	local calibrating = false
	local function speedChanged()
		if API.onSpeed then pcall(API.onSpeed, S.speedExtra) end
	end

	local lastPos, lastHrp = nil, nil
	on(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
		local char, hum, hrp = charParts()
		if not char then lastPos = nil Gov.extra = 0 return end

		if lastPos and lastHrp == hrp and Gov.extra > 0 and S.adaptive and not calibrating then
			local j = hrp.Position - lastPos
			if Vector3.new(j.X, 0, j.Z).Magnitude > 6 and os.clock() > snapCooldown then
				snapCooldown = os.clock() + 3
				S.speedExtra = math.max(0, math.floor(S.speedExtra * 0.6))
				if getgenv().__crezCal then getgenv().__crezCal.extra = S.speedExtra end
				notify("Speed", ("Snapback seen, speed lowered to +%d"):format(S.speedExtra), 3)
				log("snapback -> speedExtra", S.speedExtra)
				speedChanged()
			end
		end
		lastPos, lastHrp = hrp.Position, hrp

		AutoMove.dir = moveOverride
		if moveOverride then lastAutoMoveT = os.clock() end

		local why
		if calibrating then why = "calibrating"
		elseif not S.speed then why = "off"
		elseif S.speedExtra <= 0 then why = "+0"
		elseif inCombat() then why = "combat"
		elseif otherMovementOn() then why = "other speed on"
		else why = "boosting" end
		Gov.extra = (why == "boosting") and S.speedExtra or 0
		S.boostWhy = why
		local dir = moveOverride or hum.MoveDirection
		if Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z).Magnitude > 0.05 then
			BoostStats[why] = (BoostStats[why] or 0) + 1
			BoostStats.total = (BoostStats.total or 0) + 1
		end
	end)

	local function calibrateSpeed()
		if calibrating then return end
		calibrating = true
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			local char, hum, hrp = charParts()
			if not char then return end
			local t0 = os.clock()
			while (hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall or inCombat()) and os.clock() - t0 < 5 do task.wait(0.2) end
			refreshRay()
			local g0 = groundAt(hrp.Position)
			local axis
			for i = 0, 7 do
				if not g0 then break end
				local a = i * math.pi / 8
				local d = Vector3.new(math.cos(a), 0, math.sin(a))
				local good = not blockedDir(hrp.Position, d, 45) and not blockedDir(hrp.Position, -d, 45)
				if good then
					for s = -45, 45, 5 do
						local g = groundAt(hrp.Position + d * s)
						if not g or math.abs(g.Y - g0.Y) > 2.5 then good = false; break end
					end
				end
				if good then axis = d; break end
			end
			if not axis then
				if not S.calWarned then
					S.calWarned = true
					notify("Speed", "Too cramped to calibrate, retrying in the open", 6)
				end
				log("calibrate: no open axis here")
				return
			end
			notify("Speed", "Calibrating speed, stand still ~15s", 4)
			local wasSpeed = S.speed
			S.speed = false
			local safe, sign, interfered = 0, 1, false
			local function busy(h) return moveOverride ~= nil or os.clock() - lastAutoMoveT < 1 or (h and h.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.05) end
			for _, extra in ipairs({ 4, 8, 12, 16, 20 }) do
				if not alive then break end
				_, hum, hrp = charParts()
				if not hrp then break end
				if busy(hum) then interfered = true break end
				local v = hum.WalkSpeed + extra
				local d = axis * sign
				sign = -sign
				local snapped, expected = false, nil
				local s0 = os.clock()
				while os.clock() - s0 < 0.7 do
					local dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
					if expected and (hrp.Position - expected).Magnitude > 5 then snapped = true; break end
					hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + (d * v * dt)
					expected = hrp.Position
				end
				local rest, s1 = hrp.Position, os.clock()
				while not snapped and os.clock() - s1 < 1.2 do
					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
					if busy(hum) then interfered = true break end
					if (hrp.Position - rest).Magnitude > 5 then snapped = true end
				end
				if interfered then break end
				log("calibrate extra", extra, snapped and "SNAPPED" or "ok")
				if snapped then break end
				safe = extra
			end
			S.speed = wasSpeed
			if interfered then
				log("calibrate: something else moved you, will retry when idle")
				return
			end
			if safe == 0 then
				S.speedExtra = 0
				S.calZero = (S.calZero or 0) + 1
				if S.calZero < 3 then log("calibrate: +6 snapped, retrying later") speedChanged() return end
			end
			S.speedExtra = math.min(math.floor(safe * 0.5), 10)
			S.calibrated = true
			if S.speedExtra > 0 then getgenv().__crezCal = { job = game.JobId, extra = S.speedExtra } end
			notify("Speed", ("Calibrated: +%d studs/s (last safe %d)"):format(S.speedExtra, safe), 5)
			speedChanged()
		end)
		calibrating = false
		if not ok then log("calibrate error", err) end
	end

	local walkGen = 0
	local walkInfo = { detour = false }
	local function cancelWalk()
		walkGen = walkGen + 1
		moveOverride = nil
		local _, hum, hrp = charParts()
		if hum then hum:MoveTo(hrp.Position) end
	end

	local function ring(center, radius, n)
		local pts = {}
		for i = 0, n - 1 do
			local a = i / n * math.pi * 2
			table.insert(pts, center + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * radius, 0, math.sin(a) * radius))
		end
		return pts
	end

	local function groundY(hrp)
		local r = castSolid(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0, -40, 0))
		return r and r.Position.Y or (hrp.Position.Y - 3)
	end
	local bodyCache = { char = nil, t = -100 }
	local function body()
		local char, hum, hrp = charParts()
		if not char then return nil end
		if bodyCache.char ~= char or os.clock() - bodyCache.t > 15 then
			local above = math.max(hrp.Position.Y - groundY(hrp), 1)
			local jumpH = hum.UseJumpPower and (hum.JumpPower ^ 2 / (2 * Workspace.Gravity)) or hum.JumpHeight
			bodyCache = {
				char = char, t = os.clock(),
				radius = math.clamp(hrp.Size.X, 0.8, 2.2),
				height = math.clamp(above + hrp.Size.Y * 1.6, 2.5, 7),
				jumpH = math.max(jumpH, 1.5),
			}
		end
		return bodyCache
	end

	local function computePath(from, to, tight)
		local b = body()
		local r, h = b and b.radius or 2, b and b.height or 5
		local params = tight
			and { AgentRadius = r, AgentHeight = h, AgentCanJump = true, AgentCanClimb = true, WaypointSpacing = 3, Costs = { Water = 30 } }
			or { AgentRadius = r + 0.8, AgentHeight = h + 0.5, AgentCanJump = true, AgentCanClimb = false, WaypointSpacing = 4, Costs = { Water = 30 } }
		local path = PathfindingService:CreatePath(params)
		local ok = pcall(path.ComputeAsync, path, from, to)
		if ok and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then return path end
	end
	local function findWalkPath(from, to)
		local p = computePath(from, to, false) or computePath(from, to, true)
		if p then return p end
		for _, pt in ipairs(ring(to, 5, 6)) do
			local g = groundAt(pt)
			if g then
				p = computePath(from, g, true)
				if p then return p end
			end
		end
	end

	local function sweepSolid(origin, radius, dir)
		local params, skip = rayParams, nil
		for _ = 1, 6 do
			local ok, r = pcall(Workspace.Spherecast, Workspace, origin, radius, dir, params)
			if not ok then return castSolid(origin, dir) end
			if not r then return nil end
			local inst = r.Instance
			if inst:IsA("Terrain") or inst.CanCollide then
				if r.Normal.Y > 0.6 then return nil end
				return r
			end
			skip = skip or rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances
			table.insert(skip, inst)
			params = RaycastParams.new()
			params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			params.FilterDescendantsInstances = skip
		end
	end
	local function freeAhead(hrp, dir, dist)
		local b = body()
		local gy = groundY(hrp)
		local rad = (b and b.radius or 1.5) * 0.7
		local p = hrp.Position
		local knee = Vector3.new(p.X, gy + math.max(rad + 0.2, 0.9), p.Z)
		local chest = Vector3.new(p.X, math.max(p.Y + 0.3, knee.Y + 0.5), p.Z)
		local rk = sweepSolid(knee, rad, dir * dist)
		local rc = sweepSolid(chest, rad, dir * dist)
		return math.min(rk and rk.Distance or dist, rc and rc.Distance or dist)
	end

	local function obstacleHeight(hrp, dir)
		local gy = groundY(hrp)
		local p = hrp.Position
		local function wall(y)
			local r = castSolid(Vector3.new(p.X, y, p.Z), dir * 3.5)
			return r ~= nil and r.Normal.Y < 0.6
		end
		if not wall(gy + 0.6) and not wall(gy + 1.4) then return nil end
		for h = 1.5, 14, 1 do
			if not wall(gy + h) then return h end
		end
		return math.huge
	end

	local function climbOver(hum, hrp, dir, budget)
		local start, t0, lastJump = hrp.Position, os.clock(), 0
		while os.clock() - t0 < (budget or 2.4) do
			moveOverride = dir
			hum:MoveTo(hrp.Position + dir * 6)
			if os.clock() - lastJump > 0.27 then
				hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
				lastJump = os.clock()
			end
			task.wait(0.05)
			if flat(hrp.Position, start) > 4.5 and not obstacleHeight(hrp, dir) then break end
		end
		moveOverride = nil
		return flat(hrp.Position, start) > 3
	end

	local NO_CLIMB = { Tree = true, ["Palm Tree"] = true, Bush = true, Cactus = true }
	local function resourceAhead(hrp, dir)
		local gy = groundY(hrp)
		for _, y in ipairs({ gy + 0.8, gy + 2 }) do
			local r = castSolid(Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, y, hrp.Position.Z), dir * 4)
			if r and r.Instance then
				local m = r.Instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
				for _ = 1, 3 do
					if not m then break end
					if NO_CLIMB[m.Name] or (nodes[m] and NO_CLIMB[nodes[m]]) then return true end
					m = m.Parent and m.Parent:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
				end
			end
		end
		return false
	end
	local function unwedge(hum, hrp)
		local char = hrp.Parent
		local op = OverlapParams.new()
		op.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		op.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }
		local sum, n = Vector3.zero, 0
		local box = CFrame.new(hrp.Position)
		for _, p in ipairs(Workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(box, Vector3.new(1.6, 2.2, 1.6), op)) do
			if p.CanCollide and not p:IsA("Terrain") and p.Name ~= "Grass" and p.Name ~= "Dirt" and p.Name ~= "Sand" then
				sum, n = sum + p.Position, n + 1
			end
		end
		if n == 0 then return false end
		local ctr = sum / n
		local away = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X - ctr.X, 0, hrp.Position.Z - ctr.Z)
		away = away.Magnitude > 0.05 and away.Unit or Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit * -1
		dlog("unwedge", ("pushing out of %d parts"):format(n))
		hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + away * 3.5 + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
		task.wait(0.3)
		return true
	end
	API.unwedge = unwedge
	local lastEscape
	local function escape(hum, hrp, goal)
		local tg = Vector3.new(goal.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, goal.Z - hrp.Position.Z)
		tg = tg.Magnitude > 0.1 and tg.Unit or Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit
		local b = body()
		if hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude < 0.3 and unwedge(hum, hrp) then return "unwedged" end
		local h = obstacleHeight(hrp, tg)
		if h and resourceAhead(hrp, tg) then h = nil end
		if h and h <= (b and b.jumpH or 4) * 0.85 then
			dlog("hop", ("%.1f studs"):format(h))
			hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
			local t0 = os.clock()
			while os.clock() - t0 < 0.5 do moveOverride = tg hum:MoveTo(hrp.Position + tg * 5) task.wait(0.05) end
			moveOverride = nil
			return "hopped"
		elseif h and h <= 10 then
			dlog("climb", ("%.1f studs"):format(h))
			if climbOver(hum, hrp, tg) then return "climbed" end
		end
		local best, bestScore
		for i = 0, 15 do
			local a = i / 16 * math.pi * 2
			local d = Vector3.new(math.cos(a), 0, math.sin(a))
			local free = freeAhead(hrp, d, 10)
			if free >= 3.5 then
				local score = free * 0.5 + d:Dot(tg) * 6
				if lastEscape and os.clock() - lastEscape.t < 4 and d:Dot(lastEscape.d) < -0.7 then score = score - 5 end
				if lastEscape and os.clock() - lastEscape.t < 5 and d:Dot(lastEscape.d) > 0.9 and (lastEscape.n or 1) >= 2 then score = score - 3 * ((lastEscape.n or 1) - 1) end
				if not bestScore or score > bestScore then best, bestScore = d, score end
			end
		end
		if best then
			dlog("sidestep", ("align %.2f"):format(best:Dot(tg)))
			local rep = (lastEscape and os.clock() - lastEscape.t < 5 and best:Dot(lastEscape.d) > 0.9) and (lastEscape.n or 1) + 1 or 1
			lastEscape = { d = best, t = os.clock(), n = rep, prev = lastEscape and lastEscape.d or nil }
			local t0 = os.clock()
			while os.clock() - t0 < 0.8 do
				moveOverride = best
				hum:MoveTo(hrp.Position + best * 6)
				task.wait(0.05)
			end
			moveOverride = nil
			return "sidestep"
		end
		climbOver(hum, hrp, tg, 1.5)
		return "forced climb"
	end

	local function walkTo(goal, stopDist, keepGoing, done, budget)
		walkGen = walkGen + 1
		local my = walkGen
		do
			local c, bp = lp.Character, lp:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
			local pvp = c and c:FindFirstChild("Pvp")
			local cm = pvp and pvp:FindFirstChild("CombatMode")
			if cm and cm.Value and bp then
				for _, t in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
					if t:IsA("Tool") and t:FindFirstChild("Attacks") then t.Parent = bp end
				end
				task.wait(0.3)
			end
		end
		local function live() return my == walkGen and alive and (not keepGoing or keepGoing()) end
		local function arrived(hrp)
			if done then return done(hrp) end
			return flat(goal, hrp.Position) <= stopDist
		end
		budget = budget or 60
		local startT = os.clock()
		local bestDist, bestT = math.huge, os.clock()
		local deadEnds, noPaths, noPathDist, detour = 0, 0, math.huge, false
		local earlyRepaths = 0
		while live() do
			local _, hum, hrp = charParts()
			if not hum then break end
			if arrived(hrp) then
				hum:MoveTo(hrp.Position)
				moveOverride = nil
				dlog("arrived", ("%.1fs"):format(os.clock() - startT))
				return true
			end
			local dist = flat(goal, hrp.Position)
			if dist < bestDist - 0.75 then bestDist, bestT = dist, os.clock() end
			if os.clock() - bestT > 7 then dlog("give up: no progress for 7s", ("dist %.1f"):format(dist)); break end
			if os.clock() - startT > budget then dlog("give up: time budget", ("dist %.1f"):format(dist)); break end

			local tg = Vector3.new(goal.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, goal.Z - hrp.Position.Z)
			if dist <= 8 and tg.Magnitude > 0.1 and freeAhead(hrp, tg.Unit, dist) >= dist - 0.3 then
				local t0, lastPos, lastProg = os.clock(), hrp.Position, os.clock()
				while live() and os.clock() - t0 < 1.5 do
					local _, h2, r2 = charParts()
					if not h2 or arrived(r2) then break end
					local dv = Vector3.new(goal.X - r2.Position.X, 0, goal.Z - r2.Position.Z)
					moveOverride = dv.Magnitude > 0.1 and dv.Unit or nil
					h2:MoveTo(goal)
					task.wait(0.06)
					if (r2.Position - lastPos).Magnitude > 0.4 then lastPos, lastProg = r2.Position, os.clock()
					elseif os.clock() - lastProg > 0.7 then escape(h2, r2, goal); break end
				end
				moveOverride = nil
			else

			local seg = goal
			if dist > 220 then
				seg = groundAt(hrp.Position + tg.Unit * 220) or (hrp.Position + tg.Unit * 220)
			end
			local path = findWalkPath(hrp.Position, seg)
			local wps = path and path:GetWaypoints() or { { Position = seg, Action = Enum.PathWaypointAction.Walk } }
			dlog(path and ("path %d wps"):format(#wps) or "no path, direct", ("dist %.1f"):format(dist))
			if path and #wps > 2 then
				local plen, prev = 0, hrp.Position
				for k = 1, #wps do plen = plen + flat(wps[k].Position, prev) prev = wps[k].Position end
				local straight = flat(seg, hrp.Position)
				if plen > straight * 2.6 + 60 then
					dlog("give up: path is a huge detour", ("%.0f studs of path for %.0f straight"):format(plen, straight))
					detour = true
					break
				end
			end
			if not path then
				noPaths = noPaths + 1
				if noPaths >= 3 and dist > (noPathDist or dist) - 3 then dlog("give up: no path and walking straight is blocked", ("dist %.1f"):format(dist)); break end
				if noPaths == 1 then noPathDist = dist end
			else
				noPaths = 0
			end
			if path and seg == goal and flat(wps[#wps].Position, hrp.Position) <= 2.5 then
				deadEnds = deadEnds + 1
				if deadEnds >= 3 then dlog("give up: path ends where I stand", ("dist %.1f"):format(dist)); break end
				local dv = Vector3.new(goal.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, goal.Z - hrp.Position.Z)
				if dv.Magnitude > 0.1 then
					local t1 = os.clock()
					while live() and os.clock() - t1 < 0.6 do
						moveOverride = dv.Unit
						hum:MoveTo(goal)
						task.wait(0.06)
					end
					moveOverride = nil
				end
				task.wait(0.1)
			else
				deadEnds = 0
			end
			local blocked, stuck = false, false
			local curWp, blockedAt = 1, nil
			local bc = path and path.Blocked:Connect(function(idx)
				if type(idx) ~= "number" then blocked = true return end
				if idx >= curWp then
					blockedAt = blockedAt and math.min(blockedAt, idx) or idx
					if idx <= curWp + 4 then blocked = true end
				end
			end)
			local jh = (body() or {}).jumpH or 4
			for i = (path and 2 or 1), #wps do
				if blockedAt and blockedAt <= i + 4 then blocked = true end
				if not live() or blocked or stuck then break end
				curWp = i
				local wp = wps[i]
				if wp.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
				local lastPos, lastProg, t0, hopped = hrp.Position, os.clock(), os.clock(), false
				local lookT = 0
				while live() and not blocked do
					local _, h2, r2 = charParts()
					if not h2 then stuck = true; break end
					hum, hrp = h2, r2
					if arrived(hrp) or flat(wp.Position, hrp.Position) <= 2.2 then break end
					local dv = Vector3.new(wp.Position.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, wp.Position.Z - hrp.Position.Z)
					moveOverride = dv.Magnitude > 0.1 and dv.Unit or nil
					hum:MoveTo(wp.Position)
					if dv.Magnitude > 1 and os.clock() - lookT > 0.2 then
						lookT = os.clock()
						local free = freeAhead(hrp, dv.Unit, 3.2)
						if free < 3.2 and free < dv.Magnitude - 0.5 then
							local oh = obstacleHeight(hrp, dv.Unit)
							local isRes = oh and resourceAhead(hrp, dv.Unit)
							if oh and oh <= jh * 0.85 and not isRes then
								if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then hum.Jump = true dlog("jumping early", ("%.1f studs"):format(oh)) end
							elseif oh and earlyRepaths < 3 then
								earlyRepaths = earlyRepaths + 1
								dlog("wall ahead, repathing early", ("%.1f studs high"):format(math.min(oh, 99)))
								blocked = true
								break
							end
						end
					end
					task.wait(0.07)
					if (hrp.Position - lastPos).Magnitude > 0.45 then
						lastPos, lastProg, hopped = hrp.Position, os.clock(), false
					else
						local still = os.clock() - lastProg
						if still > 0.4 and not hopped and dv.Magnitude > 0.1 then
							hopped = true
							local h = obstacleHeight(hrp, dv.Unit)
							if h and h <= jh * 0.85 and not resourceAhead(hrp, dv.Unit) then hum.Jump = true end
						end
						if still > 1.1 then stuck = true end
					end
					if os.clock() - t0 > 6 then stuck = true end
					if stuck then break end
				end
				if arrived(hrp) then break end
			end
			if bc then bc:Disconnect() end
			moveOverride = nil
			if stuck then
				local how = escape(hum, hrp, goal)
				dlog("stuck ->", how)
			elseif blocked then
				dlog("path blocked -> repath")
			end
			end
		end
		moveOverride = nil
		walkInfo.detour = detour
		local _, hum, hrp = charParts()
		if hum then hum:MoveTo(hrp.Position) end
		return hrp ~= nil and arrived(hrp)
	end

	local function walkToModel(center, reach, keepGoing)
		local function done(hrp) return flat(center, hrp.Position) <= reach end
		local _, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not hrp then return false end
		if done(hrp) then return true end
		local pts = ring(center, math.max(reach - 2, 1.5), 8)
		table.sort(pts, function(a, b) return flat(a, hrp.Position) < flat(b, hrp.Position) end)
		local bestD, fails = flat(center, hrp.Position), 0
		local startT = os.clock()
		for i = 1, #pts do
			local g = groundAt(pts[i]) or pts[i]
			dlog("approach side", i)
			if walkTo(g, 1, keepGoing, done, i == 1 and 60 or 15) then return true end
			if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then return false end
			if walkInfo.detour then dlog("approach: only a long detour reaches it, skipping") break end
			local _, _, h2 = charParts()
			if not h2 then return false end
			local d = flat(center, h2.Position)
			if d < (bestD or d + 10) - 3 then bestD, fails = d, 0 else fails = (fails or 0) + 1 end
			if fails >= 2 or (i > 1 and os.clock() - startT > 50) then dlog("approach: no progress from other sides, giving up") break end
		end
		return false
	end

	on(UIS.InputBegan, function(i, gp)
		if gp then return end
		local k = i.KeyCode
		if (k == Enum.KeyCode.W or k == Enum.KeyCode.A or k == Enum.KeyCode.S or k == Enum.KeyCode.D) and not S.farm and not S.goalOn and not S.buildOn then
			walkGen = walkGen + 1
		end
	end)

	local lastNote = { text = "", t = 0 }
	do
		local sn = RS:FindFirstChild("SmallNotification")
		if sn and sn:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
			on(sn.OnClientEvent, function(msg)
				lastNote.text, lastNote.t = tostring(msg), os.clock()
				if lastNote.text:find("Speeding detected", 1, true) and S.speedExtra > 0 and not calibrating then
					S.speedExtra = math.floor(S.speedExtra * 0.5)
					snapCooldown = os.clock() + 3
					if getgenv().__crezCal then getgenv().__crezCal.extra = S.speedExtra end
					notify("Speed", "Server speed check fired, now +" .. S.speedExtra, 4)
					log("speed check fired -> +" .. S.speedExtra)
					speedChanged()
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	local function holdPrompt(prompt, extra)
		if not (prompt and prompt.Parent and prompt.Enabled) then return false end
		local ok, why = SafePrompt.hold(prompt, extra)
		if not ok then dlog("prompt not used:", tostring(why)) end
		return ok
	end

	local function replicaInfo(m)
		local base = m.Name:match("^(.-)_Replica$")
		if not base then return end
		local h = m:FindFirstChild("Handle")
		local p = h and h:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
		if h and p and p.Enabled then return base, h, p end
	end
	local function firePrompt(prompt)
		local deb = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("PickUpDebounce")
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while deb and deb.Value and os.clock() - t0 < 1.5 do task.wait(0.05) end
		holdPrompt(prompt)
	end

	local pickupReadyT = 0
	local dropWatch = {}
	local HEAVY = {}
	do
		local ld = RS:FindFirstChild("LootDrops")
		if ld then for _, x in ipairs(ld:GetChildren()) do if x:IsA("BasePart") then HEAVY[x.Name] = true end end end
	end
	local myHeavy = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	on(Workspace.ChildAdded, function(c)
		if #dropWatch == 0 then return end
		if c.Name:find("_Replica$") then
			for _, w in ipairs(dropWatch) do table.insert(w.items, c) end
		elseif HEAVY[c.Name] and c:IsA("BasePart") then
			myHeavy[c] = true
			for _, w in ipairs(dropWatch) do
				if c.Name == "Log" then table.insert(w.logs, c) end
			end
		end
	end)
	local function openWatch(center)
		local w = { center = center, items = {}, logs = {}, opened = os.clock() }
		table.insert(dropWatch, w)
		return w
	end
	local function closeWatch(w)
		local i = table.find(dropWatch, w)
		if i then table.remove(dropWatch, i) end
	end

	local function collectDrops(w, set, keepGoing)
		local lastCount, stableSince, t0 = #w.items, os.clock(), os.clock()
		while os.clock() - t0 < 4 do
			if #w.items ~= lastCount then lastCount, stableSince = #w.items, os.clock() end
			if os.clock() - stableSince > 1.2 and (#w.items > 0 or os.clock() - t0 > 2) then break end
			task.wait(0.1)
		end
		local tries, skip = {}, {}
		local deadline = os.clock() + 30
		while os.clock() < deadline do
			if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then return end
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			if not hrp then return end
			local list = {}
			for _, m in ipairs(w.items) do
				if m.Parent and not skip[m] then
					local base, h, p = replicaInfo(m)
					if base and set[base] and flat(h.Position, w.center) <= 45 then
						table.insert(list, { m = m, base = base, h = h, p = p, d = (h.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude })
					end
				end
			end
			if #list == 0 then return end
			if not INV.glitch and INV.full() then
				setPhase("inventory full: leaving the rest on the ground")
				return
			end
			table.sort(list, function(a, b)
				local sa, sb = a.base == "Sapling", b.base == "Sapling"
				if sa ~= sb then return sa end
				return a.d < b.d
			end)
			local e = list[1]
			tries[e.m] = (tries[e.m] or 0) + 1
			if tries[e.m] > 4 then
				skip[e.m] = true
				dlog("pickup gave up on", e.base)
			else
				setPhase(("picking up %s (%d left)"):format(e.base, #list))
				local reach = tries[e.m] >= 2 and 3 or 5
				if e.d > reach then
					walkTo(e.h.Position, 1, keepGoing, function(r)
						return e.h.Parent == nil or (e.h.Position - r.Position).Magnitude <= reach
					end, 20)
				end
				if e.m.Parent and e.p.Parent then
					local gapLeft = pickupReadyT - os.clock()
					if gapLeft > 0 then task.wait(gapLeft) end
					local before = countItem(e.base)
					local fireT = os.clock()
					firePrompt(e.p)
					local t1 = os.clock()
					while e.m.Parent and countItem(e.base) <= before and os.clock() - t1 < 1.2 do task.wait(0.05) end
					local got = e.m.Parent == nil or countItem(e.base) > before
					local note = lastNote.t >= fireT and lastNote.text or ""
					if got then
						pickupReadyT = os.clock() + 1.5
					elseif note:lower():find("too fast", 1, true) then
						tries[e.m] = tries[e.m] - 1
						pickupReadyT = os.clock() + 0.8
					end
					dlog("pickup", e.base, got and "ok" or ("retry " .. note))
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while alive do
			if S.autoPickup and not S.farm and not S.goalOn then
				local _, _, hrp = charParts()
				if hrp then
					for _, m in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
						local base, h, p = replicaInfo(m)
						if base and S.pick[base] and os.clock() >= pickupReadyT and (h.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= p.MaxActivationDistance - 1 then
							firePrompt(p)
							pickupReadyT = os.clock() + 1.5
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
			task.wait(0.3)
		end
	end)

	local function arableOK(pos, arable)
		if not arable then return true end
		for _, v in ipairs(Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(pos + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), 5)) do
			if arable:FindFirstChild(v.Name) or (v.Parent and arable:FindFirstChild(v.Parent.Name)) then return true end
		end
		return false
	end
	local function spiral(center, step, radius)
		local pts = { center }
		local r = step
		while r <= radius do
			local n = math.max(6, math.floor(2 * math.pi * r / step))
			local off = math.random() * math.pi
			for i = 0, n - 1 do
				local a = off + i / n * 2 * math.pi
				table.insert(pts, center + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * r, 0, math.sin(a) * r))
			end
			r = r + step
		end
		return pts
	end

	local function deployTemplate(name)
		local dep, mat = RS:FindFirstChild("Deployables"), RS:FindFirstChild("Materials")
		return (dep and dep:FindFirstChild(name)) or (mat and mat:FindFirstChild(name))
	end

	local recentPlants = {}
	local badSpots = {}
	local lastDeployT = 0
	local serverPlaceReadyT = 0
	local function prune(list, ttl)
		for i = #list, 1, -1 do
			if os.clock() - list[i].t > ttl then table.remove(list, i) end
		end
	end

	local function treesNear(center, radius)
		local out = {}
		for m, t in pairs(nodes) do
			local p = nodePos[m]
			if t == "Tree" and p and flat(p, center) <= radius and nodeAlive(m) then table.insert(out, p) end
		end
		return out
	end

	local function spotFree(p, gap, trees)
		for m in pairs(saplings) do
			if m.Parent and flat(m:GetPivot().Position, p) < gap then return false end
		end
		for _, r in ipairs(recentPlants) do if flat(r.pos, p) < gap then return false end end
		for _, b in ipairs(badSpots) do if flat(b.pos, p) < 1.5 then return false end end
		for _, tp in ipairs(trees) do if flat(tp, p) < 4 then return false end end
		return true
	end

	local function pickPlantSpots(center, n, minR)
		minR = minR or 0
		prune(recentPlants, 300)
		prune(badSpots, 120)
		local gap = math.max(S.spacing, 5)
		local outer = minR + gap * 5
		local trees = treesNear(center, outer + gap)
		local tpl = deployTemplate("Sapling")
		local arable = tpl and tpl:FindFirstChild("Arable")
		local cands = spiral(center, minR > 0 and 3 or 2, outer)
		if minR > 0 then
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			local from = hrp and hrp.Position or center
			local kept = {}
			for _, c in ipairs(cands) do if flat(c, center) >= minR then table.insert(kept, c) end end
			table.sort(kept, function(a, b) return flat(a, from) < flat(b, from) end)
			cands = kept
		end
		local out = {}
		for _, c in ipairs(cands) do
			if #out >= n then break end
			local ok = spotFree(c, gap, trees)
			if ok then
				for _, o in ipairs(out) do if flat(o, c) < gap then ok = false; break end end
			end
			if ok then
				local g = groundAt(c)
				if g and arableOK(g, arable) then table.insert(out, g) end
			end
		end
		return out
	end

	local function placeWithRetries(name, pos)
		local template = deployTemplate(name)
		if not template then return false, "unknown deployable " .. name end
		local halfY = template:GetExtentsSize().Y / 2
		local base = template:FindFirstChild("Base")
		local rel = base and template:GetPivot():ToObjectSpace(base.CFrame)
		local arable = template:FindFirstChild("Arable")
		prune(recentPlants, 300)
		prune(badSpots, 120)
		local gap = math.max(S.spacing, 5) * 0.75
		local trees = treesNear(pos, S.retryRadius + gap + 6)
		local tries, lastReason = 0, nil
		refreshRay()
		for _, c in ipairs(spiral(pos, S.retryStep, S.retryRadius)) do
			if tries >= S.maxTries or not alive then break end
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			if not hrp then return false, "dead" end
			local g = groundAt(c)
			if g and (g - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= 46 and arableOK(g, arable) and spotFree(g, gap, trees) then
				for _ = 1, 3 do
					local cooldownLeft = math.max(S.plantCooldown - (os.clock() - lastDeployT), serverPlaceReadyT - os.clock())
					if cooldownLeft > 0 then task.wait(cooldownLeft) end
					tries = tries + 1
					local cf = CFrame.new(g) * CFrame.new(0, halfY, 0) * hrp.CFrame.Rotation
					local callT = os.clock()
					local ok, res = pcall(DeployRem.InvokeServer, DeployRem, name, cf, rel and (cf * rel) or false)
					lastDeployT = os.clock()
					if ok and res then
						table.insert(recentPlants, { pos = g, t = os.clock() })
						return true, g
					end
					task.wait(0.25)
					local reason = lastNote.t >= callT and lastNote.text or ""
					lastReason = reason ~= "" and reason or lastReason
					dlog("place rejected", reason ~= "" and reason or "(no message)")
					local r = reason:lower()
					local secs = tonumber(r:match("wait ([%d%.]+) sec") or "")
					if secs then serverPlaceReadyT = callT + secs + 0.1 end
					if not (secs or r:find("cooldown") or r:find("wait") or r:find("too fast") or r:find("slow down")) then break end
				end
				table.insert(badSpots, { pos = g, t = os.clock() })
			end
		end
		return false, ("no valid spot after %d tries%s"):format(tries, lastReason and (" (" .. lastReason .. ")") or "")
	end

	local plantBusy = false
	local function deployName(tool)
		local m = tool:FindFirstChild("Material")
		return (m and m:IsA("ObjectValue") and m.Value and m.Value.Name) or tool.Name
	end
	local function plant(pos, toolName, keepGoing)
		toolName = toolName or "Sapling"
		if plantBusy then return false, "busy" end
		plantBusy = true
		local r = table.pack(pcall(function()
			local tool = findTool(toolName)
			if not tool then return false, "no " .. toolName end
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			if not hrp then return false, "no character" end
			if flat(pos, hrp.Position) > 25 and not walkTo(pos, 10, keepGoing) then return false, "could not walk there" end
			if not equip(tool) then return false, "could not equip" end
			local before = countItem(toolName)
			local ok, info = placeWithRetries(deployName(tool), pos)
			log("plant", ok, info, "inv", before, "->", countItem(toolName))
			return ok, info
		end))
		plantBusy = false
		closeGameGuis()
		if not r[1] then log("plant error", r[2]); return false, tostring(r[2]) end
		return r[2], r[3]
	end

	local marks = {}
	local function addMark(pos)
		local p = Instance.new("Part")
		p.Anchored, p.CanCollide, p.CanQuery, p.CanTouch = true, false, false, false
		p.Transparency, p.Size = 1, Vector3.one
		p.Position = pos + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
		p.Parent = Workspace.CurrentCamera
		local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		bb.Size, bb.AlwaysOnTop = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 22), true
		bb.Parent = p
		local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		t.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		t.BackgroundColor3, t.BackgroundTransparency = Color3.fromRGB(30, 60, 30), 0.3
		t.TextColor3, t.Font, t.TextSize = COLORS.Sapling, Enum.Font.GothamBold, 12
		t.Text = "plant #" .. (#marks + 1)
		t.Parent = bb
		table.insert(marks, { pos = pos, part = p })
	end
	local function clearMarks()
		for _, m in ipairs(marks) do m.part:Destroy() end
		table.clear(marks)
	end
	local function mouseGround()
		refreshRay()
		local ray = Workspace.CurrentCamera:ScreenPointToRay(mouse.X, mouse.Y)
		local r = castSolid(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 600)
		return r and r.Position
	end
	local function screenGround(x, y)
		refreshRay()
		local ray = Workspace.CurrentCamera:ScreenPointToRay(x, y)
		local r = castSolid(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 600)
		return r and r.Position
	end
	API.screenGround = screenGround
	local function frontGround(dist)
		local _, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not hrp then return nil end
		local look = Vector3.new(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
		if look.Magnitude < 0.1 then look = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
		return groundAt(hrp.Position + look.Unit * (dist or 6))
	end

	local function plantAtMarks()
		local done, failed = 0, 0
		for _, m in ipairs(table.clone(marks)) do
			if not findTool("Sapling") then break end
			if plant(m.pos, "Sapling") then
				done = done + 1
				m.part:Destroy()
				local i = table.find(marks, m)
				if i then table.remove(marks, i) end
			else
				failed = failed + 1
			end
		end
		notify("Plant", ("Marks: %d planted, %d failed"):format(done, failed), 4)
	end
	local function plantGrid()
		local _, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not hrp then return end
		local n, ok = countItem("Sapling"), 0
		for _, p in ipairs(spiral(hrp.Position, S.spacing, S.spacing * 6)) do
			if ok >= n or not findTool("Sapling") then break end
			local tooClose = false
			for m in pairs(saplings) do
				if m.Parent and flat(m:GetPivot().Position, p) < S.spacing * 0.8 then tooClose = true; break end
