			end
			if not tooClose and plant(p, "Sapling") then ok = ok + 1 end
		end
		notify("Plant", ("Planted %d / %d"):format(ok, n), 4)
	end

	local hallCache = { t = -10 }
	local function myTownHall()
		local m = hallCache.model
		if m and m.Parent and os.clock() - hallCache.t < 5 then return m, hallCache.pos end
		hallCache.t, hallCache.model, hallCache.pos = os.clock(), nil, nil
		local team = lp.Team
		if not team then return nil end
		local link = team:FindFirstChild("TownHall")
		local linked = link and link:IsA("ObjectValue") and link.Value
		if linked and linked.Parent and linked:IsA("Model") then
			local okP, cf = pcall(function() return linked:GetPivot() end)
			if okP then
				hallCache.model, hallCache.pos = linked, cf.Position
				return linked, cf.Position
			end
		end
		for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("Model") and c.Name:match("^TownHall") then
				local tv = c:FindFirstChild("Team")
				if tv and tostring(tv.Value) == team.Name then
					hallCache.model, hallCache.pos = c, c:GetPivot().Position
					break
				end
			end
		end
		return hallCache.model, hallCache.pos
	end
	local function hallRadius(hall)
		local ok, size = pcall(function() return hall:GetExtentsSize() end)
		return ok and math.max(size.X, size.Z) / 2 or 15
	end
	local function saplingsNear(center, radius)
		local n = 0
		for m in pairs(saplings) do
			if m.Parent and flat(m:GetPivot().Position, center) <= radius then n = n + 1 end
		end
		return n
	end

	local dropCache = {}
	local function hallDropSpot(hall, hallPos, from)
		if dropCache.hall == hall and dropCache.pos then return dropCache.pos end
		local r = hallRadius(hall) + 7
		local dir = Vector3.new(from.X - hallPos.X, 0, from.Z - hallPos.Z)
		dir = dir.Magnitude > 0.1 and dir.Unit or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
		for _, off in ipairs({ 0, 0.5, -0.5, 1, -1, 1.6, -1.6, 2.4, -2.4, math.pi }) do
			local d = CFrame.Angles(0, off, 0):VectorToWorldSpace(dir)
			local g = groundAt(hallPos + d * r)
			if g and math.abs(g.Y - hallPos.Y) < 30 and not blockedDir(g + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), -d, 3) then
				dropCache.hall, dropCache.pos = hall, g
				return g
			end
		end
	end

	local pendingLogs, treesSinceHaul = {}, 0
	local DropTool = RS:FindFirstChild("DropTool")
	local function carried()
		local v = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("CarriedItem")
		return v and v.Value
	end
	local function dropCarried()
		local held = carried()
		if held and DropTool then pcall(function() DropTool:FireServer(lp, held) end) end
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while carried() and os.clock() - t0 < 2 do task.wait(0.1) end
		return carried() == nil
	end
	local function freeLog(l)
		local c = l.Parent and l:FindFirstChild("Carrier")
		return c ~= nil and c.Value == "" and l:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt") ~= nil
	end
	local function haulLogs(keepGoing, nearbyToo)
		local hall, hallPos = myTownHall()
		if not hallPos then return 0, "no town hall for your team" end
		if not DropTool then return 0, "DropTool remote missing" end
		local _, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not hrp then return 0, "no character" end
		local spot = hallDropSpot(hall, hallPos, hrp.Position)
		if not spot then return 0, "no open ground next to the town hall" end
		if nearbyToo then
			for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
				if c.Name == "Log" and c:IsA("BasePart") and freeLog(c) and flat(c.Position, hrp.Position) <= 80
					and not table.find(pendingLogs, c) then
					table.insert(pendingLogs, c)
				end
			end
		end
		local moved, tries = 0, {}
		while not keepGoing or keepGoing() do
			_, _, hrp = charParts()
			if not hrp then break end
			if carried() then
				if walkTo(spot, 3, keepGoing, nil, 90) then dropCarried() else break end
			end
			local best, bd
			for i = #pendingLogs, 1, -1 do
				local l = pendingLogs[i]
				if not freeLog(l) or (tries[l] or 0) >= 2 or flat(l.Position, spot) < 12 then
					table.remove(pendingLogs, i)
				else
					local d = flat(l.Position, hrp.Position)
					if not bd or d < bd then best, bd = l, d end
				end
			end
			if not best then break end
			tries[best] = (tries[best] or 0) + 1
			setPhase(("hauling logs \194\183 %d left \194\183 %d delivered"):format(#pendingLogs, moved))
			local prompt = best:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
			local reached = walkTo(best.Position, 1, keepGoing, function(r)
				return best.Parent == nil or (best.Position - r.Position).Magnitude <= 6
			end, 30)
			if reached and best.Parent and prompt then
				holdPrompt(prompt)
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while carried() ~= best and os.clock() - t0 < 2 do task.wait(0.1) end
				if carried() == best then
					setPhase(("carrying log to town hall \194\183 %d left"):format(#pendingLogs - 1))
					local drop = spot + Vector3.new(math.random(-3, 3), 0, math.random(-3, 3))
					local ok = walkTo(drop, 3, keepGoing, nil, 90)
					if dropCarried() and ok then moved = moved + 1 end
				end
			end
		end
		dlog("haul done", moved, "delivered,", #pendingLogs, "left")
		return moved
	end

	function INV.handPrompt(node)
		for _, d in ipairs(node:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and (d.ActionText == "Unroot" or d.ActionText == "Harvest") then return d end
		end
	end
	function INV.handHarvest(node, t, pickSet, keepGoing)
		local prompt = INV.handPrompt(node)
		if not prompt then
			local far = nodePos[node]
			if not far then return false, "no tool rule for " .. t end
			pcall(function() lp:RequestStreamAroundAsync(far, 5) end)
			prompt = INV.handPrompt(node)
			if not prompt then
				local _, _, hrp0 = charParts()
				setPhase(("walking to %s (%d studs)"):format(t, hrp0 and flat(far, hrp0.Position) or 0))
				if not walkToModel(far, 10, keepGoing) then
					blacklist[node] = os.clock() + 90
					return false, "unreachable"
				end
				local tw = os.clock()
				repeat
					prompt = INV.handPrompt(node)
					if not prompt then task.wait(0.25) end
				until prompt or os.clock() - tw > 5
			end
			if not prompt then
				blacklist[node] = os.clock() + 120
				return false, t .. " had no pick prompt"
			end
		end
		local center = nodePos[node] or node:GetPivot().Position
		local par = prompt.Parent
		local ppos = (par and par:IsA("Attachment") and par.WorldPosition) or (par and par:IsA("BasePart") and par.Position) or center
		local reach = math.max(prompt.MaxActivationDistance - 2.5, 3)
		do
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			setPhase(("walking to %s (%d studs)"):format(t, hrp and flat(ppos, hrp.Position) or 0))
		end
		if not walkToModel(ppos, reach, keepGoing) then
			blacklist[node] = os.clock() + 90
			return false, "unreachable"
		end
		local w = openWatch(ppos)
		local ok, res, why = pcall(function()
			local before = {}
			for item in pairs(pickSet) do before[item] = countItem(item) end
			local picked = false
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then return false, "stopped" end
				if not (prompt.Parent and prompt.Enabled and node.Parent) then picked = true break end
				local char, _, hrp = charParts()
				if not char or inCombat() then return false, "interrupted" end
				if (ppos - hrp.Position).Magnitude > prompt.MaxActivationDistance - 0.5 then
					if not walkToModel(ppos, reach, keepGoing) then return false, "lost reach" end
				end
				setPhase(("picking %s by hand"):format(t))
				holdPrompt(prompt)
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while os.clock() - t0 < 1.5 and prompt.Parent and prompt.Enabled and node.Parent do task.wait(0.1) end
				if not (prompt.Parent and prompt.Enabled and node.Parent) or #w.items > 0 then picked = true break end
				for item, n in pairs(before) do if countItem(item) > n then picked = true end end
				if picked then break end
			end
			if not picked then
				blacklist[node] = os.clock() + 60
				return false, "hand pick did not register"
			end
			blacklist[node] = os.clock() + 25
			setPhase("collecting drops")
			collectDrops(w, pickSet, keepGoing)
			return true
		end)
		closeWatch(w)
		if not ok then return false, "error: " .. tostring(res) end
		return res, why
	end

	local function harvestNode(node, pickSet, keepGoing)
		local t = nodes[node]
		if not t then return false, "not a node" end
		local kind = toolKindFor(t)
		if not kind then return INV.handHarvest(node, t, pickSet, keepGoing) end
		local tool = bestToolFor(kind)
		if not tool then return false, "need " .. kind end
		local center = nodePos[node] or node:GetPivot().Position
		local _, size = node:GetBoundingBox()
		local reach = math.clamp(math.max(size.X, size.Z) * 0.5 + 2.5, 4, 9)

		do
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			setPhase(("walking to %s (%d studs)"):format(t, hrp and flat(center, hrp.Position) or 0))
		end
		local wOk, wRes = pcall(walkToModel, center, reach, keepGoing)
		if not wOk then log("walk error", wRes) end
		if not (wOk and wRes) then
			blacklist[node] = os.clock() + 90
			return false, wOk and "unreachable" or "walk error"
		end
		if not equip(tool) then return false, "could not equip " .. tool.Name end

		local w = openWatch(center)
		local ok, res, why = pcall(function()
			local hp = node:FindFirstChild("Health")
			local lastHp = hp and hp.Value
			local cdv = tool:FindFirstChild("Cooldown")
			local cd = (cdv and cdv.Value or 0.6) + 0.08
			local before = {}
			for item in pairs(pickSet) do before[item] = countItem(item) end
			local function gotSomething()
				for item, n in pairs(before) do if countItem(item) > n then return true end end
				for _, m in ipairs(w.items) do
					local base = m.Parent and replicaInfo(m)
					if base and pickSet[base] then return true end
				end
				return false
			end
			local noProg = 0
			local harvestStart = os.clock()
			while (not keepGoing or keepGoing()) and nodeAlive(node) do
				if lastNote.t >= harvestStart and lastNote.text:lower():find("building", 1, true) then
					blacklist[node] = os.clock() + 3600
					return false, "too close to a building, skipped"
				end
				local char, _, hrp = charParts()
				if not char or inCombat() then return false, "interrupted" end
				if tool.Parent ~= char and not equip(tool) then return false, "lost tool" end
				if flat(center, hrp.Position) > reach + 1 then
					if not walkToModel(center, reach, keepGoing) then return false, "lost reach" end
				end
				hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(center.X, hrp.Position.Y, center.Z))
				tool:Activate()
				setPhase(("harvesting %s%s"):format(t, hp and (" \194\183 hp " .. math.floor(hp.Value)) or ""))
				task.wait(cd)
				if hp then
					if hp.Value < lastHp then lastHp, noProg = hp.Value, 0 else noProg = noProg + 1 end
				else
					if gotSomething() then break end
					noProg = noProg + 1
				end
				if noProg >= 7 then
					blacklist[node] = os.clock() + 60
					return false, "no damage registering"
				end
			end
			if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then return false, "stopped" end
			blacklist[node] = os.clock() + 25
			setPhase("collecting drops")
			collectDrops(w, pickSet, keepGoing)
			for _, l in ipairs(w.logs) do
				if l.Parent and not table.find(pendingLogs, l) then table.insert(pendingLogs, l) end
			end
			return true
		end)
		closeWatch(w)
		if not ok then return false, "error: " .. tostring(res) end
		return res, why
	end

	local function farmKeep() return alive and S.farm and not inCombat() end
	local pendingReplant = 0
	local function farmOnce()
		local char, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not char then S.status = "waiting for character"; task.wait(1); return end
		if inCombat() then S.status = "paused: in combat"; task.wait(1); return end
		INV.surviveCheck(farmKeep, function(t) S.status = t end)
		if INV.autoGlitch and not INV.glitch and INV.full() then INV.turnOnGlitch(function(t) S.status = t end) end
		char, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not char then return end
		local kind = toolKindFor(S.farmTarget)
		if kind and not bestToolFor(kind) then S.status = "need a " .. kind .. " tool (try the Goal tab)"; task.wait(1); return end
		local hall, hallPos = nil, nil
		if S.homeMode then hall, hallPos = myTownHall() end
		local farHall = hallPos and flat(hallPos, hrp.Position) > S.homeMaxDist
		if farHall then hall, hallPos = nil, nil end
		local me = S.avoidSea and hrp.Position or nil
		local node
		if hallPos then
			node = nearestReachable(S.farmTarget, hallPos, S.farmRange, me)
			if not node then S.status = ("no reachable %s within %d of your town hall"):format(S.farmTarget, S.farmRange); task.wait(1); return end
		else
			node = nearestReachable(S.farmTarget, hrp.Position, S.farmRange, me)
			if not node then S.status = ("no reachable %s within %d%s"):format(S.farmTarget, S.farmRange, farHall and " (town hall too far, farming around you)" or ""); task.wait(1); return end
		end

		local treeGround = groundAt(nodePos[node]) or nodePos[node]
		local sapBefore = countItem("Sapling")
		local ok, why = harvestNode(node, S.pick, farmKeep)
		if not ok then S.status = "skipped node: " .. tostring(why); return end
		if S.farmTarget == "Tree" then treesSinceHaul = treesSinceHaul + 1 end

		if S.farmTarget == "Tree" and S.replant then
			local gained = math.max(countItem("Sapling") - sapBefore, 0)
			local toPlant = math.min(gained + pendingReplant, countItem("Sapling"))
			if toPlant > 0 then
				local spots
				if hallPos and S.plantNearHall then
					local minR = math.max(S.hallPlantMin, hallRadius(hall) + 6)
					spots = pickPlantSpots(groundAt(hallPos) or hallPos, toPlant, minR)
				else
					spots = pickPlantSpots(treeGround, toPlant)
				end
				local planted = 0
				for i, spot in ipairs(spots) do
					if not farmKeep() then break end
					S.status = ("replanting sapling %d/%d"):format(i, toPlant)
					local pOk, info = plant(spot, "Sapling", farmKeep)
					if pOk then planted = planted + 1 else S.status = "replant failed: " .. tostring(info) end
				end
				pendingReplant = math.max(toPlant - planted, 0)
				log(("replant: gained %d, planted %d, pending %d"):format(gained, planted, pendingReplant))
			end
		end

		if S.haul and S.farmTarget == "Tree" and treesSinceHaul >= S.haulEvery and farmKeep() then
			local _, hp2 = myTownHall()
			local _, _, hrp2 = charParts()
			if hp2 and hrp2 and flat(hp2, hrp2.Position) <= S.homeMaxDist then
				local moved, hwhy = haulLogs(farmKeep)
				log(("haul: delivered %d logs%s"):format(moved, hwhy and (" (" .. hwhy .. ")") or ""))
				if farmKeep() then treesSinceHaul = 0 end
			end
		end
		S.status = S.farmTarget .. " done"
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		local wasFarming = false
		while alive do
			if S.farm then
				wasFarming = true
				local ok, err = pcall(farmOnce)
				if not ok then S.status = "error: " .. tostring(err); log("farm error", err); task.wait(1) end
			elseif wasFarming then
				wasFarming = false
				if INV.farmHome then
					task.wait(0.3)
					pcall(INV.goHome, function() return alive and not S.farm and not S.goalOn end, function(t) S.status = t end)
				end
			end
			task.wait(0.2)
		end
	end)

	local promptCache = { t = -100, lastStream = -100, all = {}, ready = false }

	do
		local all = promptCache.all
		local menu = Workspace:FindFirstChild("MenuScene")
		local function add(d)
			if d.ClassName == "ProximityPrompt" and not (menu and d:IsDescendantOf(menu)) then all[d] = true end
		end
		on(Workspace.DescendantAdded, add)
		on(Workspace.DescendantRemoving, function(d) if all[d] then all[d] = nil end end)
		task.spawn(function()
			local list = Workspace:GetDescendants()
			local slice = os.clock()
			for i = 1, #list do
				add(list[i])
				if i % 250 == 0 and os.clock() - slice > 0.004 then task.wait() slice = os.clock() end
			end
			promptCache.ready = true
		end)
	end
	local function findPrompts(text, byObject)
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while not promptCache.ready and os.clock() - t0 < 10 do task.wait(0.1) end
		local function collect()
			local out = {}
			for p in pairs(promptCache.all) do
				if (byObject and p.ObjectText or p.ActionText) == text and p.Enabled and p.Parent then out[#out + 1] = p end
			end
			return out
		end
		local out = collect()
		local hall, hallPos = myTownHall()
		if hall and hallPos and os.clock() - promptCache.lastStream > 15 then
			local inHall = false
			for _, p in ipairs(out) do if p:IsDescendantOf(hall) then inHall = true break end end
			if not inHall then
				promptCache.lastStream = os.clock()
				pcall(function() lp:RequestStreamAroundAsync(hallPos, 5) end)
				task.wait(0.3)
				out = collect()
			end
		end
		return out
	end
	local function promptPos(p)
		local par = p.Parent
		if not par then return nil end
		if par:IsA("Attachment") then return par.WorldPosition end
		if par:IsA("BasePart") then return par.Position end
		local m = p:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
		return m and m:GetPivot().Position
	end
	local function stationTier(prompt)
		local m = prompt:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
		while m do
			local st = m:FindFirstChild("Station")
			if st and st:IsA("IntValue") then return st.Value, m end
			m = m.Parent and m.Parent:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
		end
		return 0
	end
	local function townFirst(list, posOf)
		local _, _, hrp = charParts()
		local hall, hallPos = myTownHall()
		local from = hrp and hrp.Position or Vector3.zero
		if hallPos and flat(hallPos, from) > S.homeMaxDist then hall, hallPos = nil, nil end
		local cands = {}
		for _, x in ipairs(list) do
			local p = posOf(x)
			if p then
				local own = (hall ~= nil and x:IsDescendantOf(hall)) or (hallPos ~= nil and flat(p, hallPos) <= 300)
				local d = flat(p, from)
				local sea = S.avoidSea and d > 60 and Sea.crosses(from, p) or false
				table.insert(cands, { x = x, own = own, d = d, sea = sea })
			end
		end
		table.sort(cands, function(a, b)
			if a.sea ~= b.sea then return b.sea end
			if a.own ~= b.own then return a.own end
			return a.d < b.d
		end)
		return cands
	end

	local function usePrompt(prompt, keepGoing)
		local pos = promptPos(prompt)
		if not pos then return false end
		local reach = math.max(prompt.MaxActivationDistance - 2.5, 3)
		local function near(hrp) return (pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= reach end
		local _, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not hrp then return false end
		if not near(hrp) and not walkTo(pos, 2, keepGoing, near, 120) then return false end
		holdPrompt(prompt)
		return true
	end

	INV.badChest = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	function INV.ownChest(m)
		local hall = myTownHall()
		if hall and m:IsDescendantOf(hall) then return true end
		local b = m.Parent
		while b and b ~= Workspace and not (b:IsA("Model") and b:FindFirstChild("Door")) do b = b.Parent end
		if not b or b == Workspace then return false end
		local kp = b:FindFirstChild("KeyPrompt", true)
		if not (kp and kp:IsA("ProximityPrompt")) then return false end
		return (kp.ObjectText:gsub("%s+$", "")) == (lp.Name .. "'s Home")
	end
	function INV.findChest()
		local list = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(findPrompts("Store")) do
			local m = p.Name == "StorePrompt" and p:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			local inv = m and m:FindFirstChild("Inventory")
			local cap = m and m:FindFirstChild("Cap")
			if inv and p.Parent:FindFirstChild("OpenPrompt") and (not cap or #inv:GetChildren() < cap.Value)
				and (INV.badChest[m] or 0) < os.clock() then
				table.insert(list, p)
			end
		end
		local cands = townFirst(list, promptPos)
		for _, c in ipairs(cands) do
			local m = c.x:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			if m and INV.ownChest(m) then return m end
		end
		local c = cands[1]
		return c and c.x:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
	end
	function INV.chestRoom(m)
		local inv, cap = m:FindFirstChild("Inventory"), m:FindFirstChild("Cap")
		return inv ~= nil and (not cap or #inv:GetChildren() < cap.Value)
	end
	function INV.deposit(tools, bucket, keepGoing, report)
		if #tools == 0 then return 0 end
		local m = (INV.chestModel and INV.chestModel.Parent and INV.chestModel) or INV.findChest()
		if not m then return 0, "no chest with free space nearby" end
		if not INV.chestRoom(m) then return 0, "the chest is full (20/20)" end
		INV.chestModel = m
		local store = m:FindFirstChild("StorePrompt", true)
		return INV.withBackpack(function()
			local stored = 0
			for _, tool in ipairs(tools) do
				if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then break end
				if tool.Parent and INV.chestRoom(m) then
					local name = tool.Name
					report(("storing %s in the chest so you have room"):format(name))
					local before = countItem(name)
					if equip(tool) and usePrompt(store, keepGoing) then
						local t0 = os.clock()
						while countItem(name) >= before and os.clock() - t0 < 2 do task.wait(0.1) end
					end
					if countItem(name) < before then
						stored = stored + 1
						bucket[name] = (bucket[name] or 0) + 1
					end
				end
			end
			local _, hum = charParts()
			if hum then hum:UnequipTools() end
			if stored == 0 then
				log(("chest %s refused your items%s"):format(m:GetFullName(), lastNote.text ~= "" and (" (" .. lastNote.text .. ")") or ""))
				INV.badChest[m] = os.clock() + 600
				local any = false
				for _, b in ipairs({ INV.stash, INV.parked }) do for _, c in pairs(b) do if c > 0 then any = true end end end
				if not any then INV.chestModel = nil end
				return 0, "the chest did not accept items" .. (lastNote.text ~= "" and (" (" .. lastNote.text .. ")") or "")
			end
			return stored
		end)
	end
	function INV.retrieve(want, bucket, keepGoing, report, model)
		local m = model or INV.chestModel
		if not (m and m.Parent) then return 0, "the chest your items were stored in is gone" end
		local open = m:FindFirstChild("OpenPrompt", true)
		if not open then return 0, "chest has no Open prompt" end
		local set, left, before = {}, {}, {}
		for n, c in pairs(want) do if c > 0 then set[n], left[n], before[n] = true, c, countItem(n) end end
		if next(set) == nil then return 0 end
		report("taking your stored materials back out of the chest")
		if not usePrompt(open, keepGoing) then return 0, "could not reach the chest" end
		local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
		local sg, t0 = nil, os.clock()
		repeat
			sg = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("Storage")
			if sg and sg.Enabled then break end
			task.wait(0.1)
		until os.clock() - t0 > 3
		local items = sg and sg.Enabled and findPath(sg, "Frame", "Items")
		if not items then return 0, "chest did not open" end
		local function frameCount()
			local n = 0
			for _, fr in ipairs(items:GetChildren()) do if fr:IsA("Frame") and fr:FindFirstChild("StoredItem") then n = n + 1 end end
			return n
		end
		local invF = m:FindFirstChild("Inventory")
		local function inChest(name)
			local n = 0
			if invF then for _, c in ipairs(invF:GetChildren()) do if c.Name == name .. "_Replica" then n = n + 1 end end end
			return n
		end
		local expected = invF and #invF:GetChildren() or 1
		local tf = os.clock()
		while os.clock() - tf < 4 and frameCount() < expected do task.wait(0.1) end
		task.wait(0.25)
		local total = 0
		for _, c in pairs(left) do total = total + c end
		local tempGlitch = false
		if not INV.glitch and INV.cap - INV.backpackCount() < total then
			report("making room to carry your stored items back")
			INV.setGlitch(true)
			tempGlitch = true
		end
		local drop = RS:FindFirstChild("DropStorage")
		local w = openWatch(promptPos(open) or m:GetPivot().Position)
		local used = {}
		for _ = 1, 3 do
			for _, fr in ipairs(items:GetChildren()) do
				local si = fr:IsA("Frame") and fr:FindFirstChild("StoredItem")
				local obj = si and si.Value
				local base = obj and obj.Parent and obj.Name:match("^(.-)_Replica$")
				if drop and base and not used[obj] and (left[base] or 0) > 0 then
					used[obj] = true
					pcall(function() drop:FireServer(si) end)
					left[base] = left[base] - 1
					task.wait(0.3)
				end
			end
			local more = false
			for _, l in pairs(left) do if l > 0 then more = true end end
			if not more then break end
			task.wait(0.8)
		end
		sg.Enabled = false
		for n, l in pairs(left) do
			if l > 0 and inChest(n) == 0 then bucket[n] = math.max((bucket[n] or 0) - l, 0) end
		end
		collectDrops(w, set, keepGoing)
		closeWatch(w)
		if tempGlitch and not INV.autoGlitch then INV.setGlitch(false) end
		local got = 0
		for n in pairs(set) do
			local g = math.max(countItem(n) - before[n], 0)
			bucket[n] = math.max((bucket[n] or 0) - g, 0)
			got = got + g
		end
		return got
	end

	local function goToStation(tier, keepGoing)
		local list = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(findPrompts("Craft")) do
			if stationTier(p) >= tier then table.insert(list, p) end
		end
		if #list == 0 then return false, "no tier " .. tier .. " crafting table on the map" end
		local cands = townFirst(list, promptPos)
		for i = 1, math.min(4, #cands) do
			local p = cands[i].x
			local _, tierModel = stationTier(p)
			if usePrompt(p, keepGoing) then
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while os.clock() - t0 < 2.5 do
					local cur = valuesVal("CraftingTable")
					if cur ~= nil and (tierModel == nil or cur == tierModel or cur:IsDescendantOf(tierModel) or tierModel:IsDescendantOf(cur)) then
						return true
					end
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
				dlog("crafting table did not register, trying the next one")
			end
			if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then return false, "stopped" end
		end
		return false, "could not open a tier " .. tier .. " crafting table"
	end

	local function earnExp(need, keepGoing, report)
		local t0 = os.clock()
		local failsInRow = 0
		while (valuesVal("Exp") or 0) < need do
			if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then return false, "stopped" end
			if os.clock() - t0 > 900 then return false, "earning exp took over 15 minutes" end
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			if not hrp then
				task.wait(1)
			else
				local _, hallPos = myTownHall()
				if hallPos and flat(hallPos, hrp.Position) > S.homeMaxDist then hallPos = nil end
				local from = (S.homeMode and hallPos) or hrp.Position
				local best
				for _, t in ipairs({ "Tree", "Stone" }) do
					local kind = toolKindFor(t)
					if not kind or bestToolFor(kind) then
						local n, d = nearestReachable(t, from, 1500, S.avoidSea and hrp.Position or nil)
						if n and (not best or d < best.d) then best = { n = n, d = d, t = t } end
					end
				end
				if not best then return false, "no Axe/Pick to earn exp with (a Small Rock works)" end
				if report then report(("earning exp %.1f/%d for the job switch (%s)"):format(valuesVal("Exp") or 0, need, best.t)) end
				local ok = harvestNode(best.n, S.pick, keepGoing)
				failsInRow = ok and 0 or failsInRow + 1
				if failsInRow >= 6 then return false, "could not harvest anything to earn exp" end
			end
		end
		return true
	end

	local function ensureJob(job, keepGoing, report)
		if job == "" or charVal("Job") == job then return true end
		local jobInst = JobsFolder and JobsFolder:FindFirstChild(job)
		if not jobInst then return false, "unknown job " .. tostring(job) end
		local tierNeed = jobInst:IsA("ValueBase") and tonumber(jobInst.Value) or 0
		local tierVal = lp.Team and lp.Team:FindFirstChild("Tier")
		local tierHave = tierVal and tonumber(tierVal.Value) or 0
		if tierNeed > tierHave then
			return false, ("%s needs a tier %d town and yours is tier %d (upgrade your town hall or join a bigger civ)"):format(job, tierNeed, tierHave)
		end
		local need = jobInst:FindFirstChild("Exp") and jobInst.Exp.Value or 0
		if (valuesVal("Exp") or 0) < need then
			local ok, why = earnExp(need, keepGoing, report)
			if not ok then return false, why end
		end
		local boards = findPrompts("Job Board", true)
		for _, p in ipairs(findPrompts("Open Job Board")) do table.insert(boards, p) end
		if #boards == 0 then return false, "no job board loaded nearby (walk closer to your town hall)" end
		if report then report("switching job to " .. job) end
		local cands = townFirst(boards, promptPos)
		for i = 1, math.min(4, #cands) do
			if usePrompt(cands[i].x, keepGoing) then
				task.wait(0.6)
				local noteT = os.clock()
				ChangeJobRem:FireServer(jobInst)
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while os.clock() - t0 < 4 do
					if charVal("Job") == job then closeGameGuis(); return true end
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
				dlog("job board refused:", lastNote.t >= noteT and lastNote.text or "(no message)")
			end
			if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then break end
		end
		closeGameGuis()
		return false, "job change to " .. job .. " was not accepted" .. (lastNote.text ~= "" and (" (" .. lastNote.text .. ")") or "")
	end

	local function craft(item, keepGoing, report)
		local rec = RECIPES[item]
		if not rec then return false, "not craftable" end
		local ok, why = ensureJob(rec.job, keepGoing, report)
		if not ok then return false, why end
		if report then report("crafting " .. item) end
		ok, why = goToStation(rec.station, keepGoing)
		if not ok and (not keepGoing or keepGoing()) then
			local tableName = rec.station <= 1 and "Crafting Table" or ("Crafting Table " .. rec.station)
			local built, bwhy = INV.buildMissing(tableName, keepGoing, report or function() end)
			if built then
				ok, why = goToStation(rec.station, keepGoing)
			elseif bwhy then
				why = why .. " (building a " .. tableName .. " failed: " .. bwhy .. ")"
			end
		end
		if not ok then return false, why end
		local before = countItem(item)
		local noteT = os.clock()
		local cOk, res = pcall(CraftItem.InvokeServer, CraftItem, rec.inst)
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while countItem(item) <= before and os.clock() - t0 < 2.5 do task.wait(0.1) end
		if countItem(item) <= before and INV.glitch then
			cOk, res = INV.withBackpack(function()
				local ok2, res2 = pcall(CraftItem.InvokeServer, CraftItem, rec.inst)
				local t1 = os.clock()
				while countItem(item) <= before and os.clock() - t1 < 2.5 do task.wait(0.1) end
				return ok2, res2
			end)
		end
		closeGameGuis()
		if countItem(item) > before then return true end
		local note = lastNote.t >= noteT and lastNote.text or ""
		return false, ("server refused %s%s"):format(item, note ~= "" and (": " .. note) or (cOk and (" (" .. tostring(res) .. ")") or " (error)"))
	end

	local function reserveFor(item)
		if item ~= "Small Rock" then return 0 end
		for _, kind in ipairs({ "Axe", "Pick" }) do
			local other = false
			for _, t in ipairs(allTools()) do
				if t.Name ~= "Small Rock" and toolProvides(t, kind) then other = true end
			end
			if not other then return 1 end
		end
		return 0
	end

	local function cheapestToolRecipe(kind)
		local best, bestCost
		for name, rec in pairs(RECIPES) do
			if toolProvides(rec.inst, kind) then
				local cost = rec.station * 10 + (rec.job ~= "" and rec.job ~= charVal("Job") and 15 or 0)
				for _, n in pairs(rec.req) do cost = cost + n end
				if not bestCost or cost < bestCost then best, bestCost = name, cost end
			end
		end
		return best
	end

	local function chooseSource(item, from)
		local list = SOURCES[item]
		if not list then return nil end
		if not from then
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			from = hrp and hrp.Position
		end
		local best, bestScore
		for _, s in ipairs(list) do
			if anyNodeOf(s.node) then
				local kind = toolKindFor(s.node)
				local penalty = (kind and not bestToolFor(kind)) and 1500 or 0
				local d = 3000
				if from then
					local _, nd = nearestNode(s.node, from, 6000)
					d = nd or 6000
				end
				local score = (d + penalty) / math.sqrt(math.max(s.yield, 1))
				if not bestScore or score < bestScore then best, bestScore = s, score end
			end
		end
		return best
	end

	local function actionCost(a)
		if a.fail then return 1000 end
		if a.kind == "gather" then return 0 end
		local rec = RECIPES[a.item]
		if rec and rec.job ~= "" and rec.job ~= charVal("Job") then return 50 end
		return 1
	end
	local function nextAction(item, qty, depth)
		depth = depth or 0
		if depth > 10 then return { fail = "recipe chain too deep at " .. item } end
		local have = countItem(item) + (INV.stash[item] or 0)
		if have >= qty then return nil end
		local rec = RECIPES[item]
		if rec then
			local missing = qty - have
			local best, bestCost
			for ing, per in pairs(rec.req) do
				local a = nextAction(ing, per * missing + reserveFor(ing), depth + 1)
				if a then
					if a.fail then return a end
					local c = actionCost(a)
					if not bestCost or c < bestCost then best, bestCost = a, c end
				end
			end
			return best or { kind = "craft", item = item }
		end
		local src = chooseSource(item)
		if src then
			local kind = toolKindFor(src.node)
			if kind and not bestToolFor(kind) then
				local toolName = cheapestToolRecipe(kind)
				if not toolName then return { fail = "no recipe gives a " .. kind .. " tool" } end
				return nextAction(toolName, 1, depth + 1) or { kind = "craft", item = toolName }
			end
			return { kind = "gather", item = item, node = src.node, want = qty }
		end
		local proc = INV.PROC[item]
		if proc then
			if proc.kind == "none" then return { fail = proc.why } end
			if proc.kind == "station" then
				local a = nextAction(proc.input, qty - have, depth + 1)
				if a then return a end
			end
			return { kind = "process", item = item, proc = proc, want = qty }
		end
		return { fail = "don't know where to get " .. item }
	end

	local function describePlan(item, qty, depth, lines)
		depth, lines = depth or 0, lines or {}
		if depth > 8 then return lines end
		local have = countItem(item)
		local pad = string.rep("  ", depth)
		local rec = RECIPES[item]
		if rec then
			local jobNote = ""
			if rec.job ~= "" then
				jobNote = " \194\183 job " .. rec.job .. (charVal("Job") == rec.job and " (you have it)" or "")
			end
			table.insert(lines, ("%s%s x%d (have %d) \194\183 craft at tier %d%s"):format(pad, item, qty, have, rec.station, jobNote))
			for ing, per in pairs(rec.req) do describePlan(ing, per * math.max(qty - have, 0), depth + 1, lines) end
		else
			local src = chooseSource(item)
			local kind = src and toolKindFor(src.node)
			local how = src and ("from " .. src.node .. (kind and (" (needs " .. kind .. ")") or "")) or "UNKNOWN SOURCE"
			local proc = not src and INV.PROC[item]
			if proc then how = INV.procText(proc) end
			table.insert(lines, ("%s%s x%d (have %d) \194\183 %s"):format(pad, item, qty, have, how))
			if proc and proc.kind == "station" then describePlan(proc.input, math.max(qty - have, 0), depth + 1, lines) end
			if kind and qty > have and not bestToolFor(kind) then
				local toolName = cheapestToolRecipe(kind)
				if toolName then
					table.insert(lines, ("%s  first makes a %s tool:"):format(pad, kind))
					describePlan(toolName, 1, depth + 2, lines)
				else
					table.insert(lines, ("%s  no recipe gives a %s tool"):format(pad, kind))
				end
			end
		end
		return lines
	end
	local function planText(item, qty)
		local lines = describePlan(item, qty)
		local a = nextAction(item, qty)
		local nextStep = not a and "already have it" or a.fail and ("BLOCKED: " .. a.fail) or (a.proc and ("make " .. a.item .. ": " .. INV.procText(a.proc))) or (a.kind .. " " .. a.item .. (a.node and (" from " .. a.node) or ""))
		local jobs = {}
		local function walk(it, d)
			if d > 8 then return end
			local rec = RECIPES[it]
			local proc = not rec and not chooseSource(it) and INV.PROC[it]
			if proc then
				if proc.job and proc.job ~= "" and not table.find(jobs, proc.job) then table.insert(jobs, proc.job) end
				if proc.input then walk(proc.input, d + 1) end
				return
			end
			if not rec then
				local src = chooseSource(it)
				local kind = src and toolKindFor(src.node)
				if kind and not bestToolFor(kind) then
					local toolName = cheapestToolRecipe(kind)
					if toolName then walk(toolName, d + 1) end
				end
				return
			end
			if rec.job ~= "" and not table.find(jobs, rec.job) then table.insert(jobs, rec.job) end
			for ing in pairs(rec.req) do walk(ing, d + 1) end
		end
		walk(item, 0)
		local exp = valuesVal("Exp") or 0
		table.insert(lines, ("\njobs on the way: %s \194\183 your job %s \194\183 exp %.1f (switching needs 5, resets to 0)"):format(
			#jobs > 0 and table.concat(jobs, ", ") or "none", tostring(charVal("Job") or "none"), exp))
		return "next: " .. nextStep, table.concat(lines, "\n")
	end

	INV.shorn = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	INV.badAnimal = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	function INV.procText(p)
		if p.kind == "station" then return ("made at a %s from %s"):format(p.station, p.input) end
		if p.kind == "smelt" then return ("smelt %s at the Smelter (Blacksmith, 2 fuel), quench the %s in the Water Barrel"):format(p.input, p.heated) end
		if p.kind == "hunt" then return ("harvest a %s as a Hunter (kill a Cow or Sheep)"):format(p.carcass) end
		if p.kind == "shear" then return ("shear a %s"):format(p.animal) end
		return p.why or "can't be made"
	end
	function INV.stationPickup(m, out)
		for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and d.Enabled and (d.ObjectText == out or d.ActionText:find("^Take") or d.ActionText:find("^Collect") or d.ActionText:find("^Pick")) then return d end
		end
	end
	function INV.animals(name)
		local res = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("Resources")
		local folder = res and res:FindFirstChild("Animals")
		local out = {}
		if folder then for _, an in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do if an.Name == name and an:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then table.insert(out, an) end end end
		return out
	end
	function INV.findCarcass(maxDist)
		local _, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not hrp then return nil end
		local best, bd
		for d in pairs(promptCache.all) do
			if d.ActionText == "Harvest" and d.Enabled and d.Parent and d.ObjectText:find("Carcass$") then
				local pos = promptPos(d)
				local dist = pos and (pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude
				if dist and dist <= maxDist and (not bd or dist < bd) then best, bd = d, dist end
			end
		end
		return best
	end
	INV.badStation = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	INV.autoBuild, INV.builtAt = true, {}
	function INV.buildMissing(name, keepGoing, report)
		if not INV.autoBuild then return false, nil end
		local Bd = API.Builder
		if not (Bd and Bd.list and table.find(Bd.list, name)) then return false, nil end
		if INV.builtAt[name] and os.clock() - INV.builtAt[name] < 600 then return false, "already tried building one in the last 10 minutes" end
		INV.builtAt[name] = os.clock()
		report(("no usable %s around: building one"):format(name))
		log(("goal: building a %s because none was usable"):format(name))
		local ok, why = Bd.buildAndWait(name, keepGoing, report)
		if ok then
			promptCache.t = -100
			report(("built a %s, back to the goal"):format(name))
			return true
		end
		return false, tostring(why)
	end
	function INV.lockedFor(m)
		local b = m and m.Parent
		while b and b ~= Workspace and not (b:IsA("Model") and b:FindFirstChild("Door")) do b = b.Parent end
		if not b or b == Workspace then return false end
		local kp = b:FindFirstChild("KeyPrompt", true)
		local owner = (kp and kp:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and kp.ObjectText or ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
		return owner:find("'s Home$") ~= nil and owner ~= (lp.Name .. "'s Home")
	end
	function INV.openDoors(m, keepGoing, report)
		local b = m and m.Parent
		while b and b ~= Workspace and not (b:IsA("Model") and b:FindFirstChild("Door")) do b = b.Parent end
		if not b or b == Workspace then return false end
		local opened = false
		for _, door in ipairs(b:GetChildren()) do
			if door.Name == "Door" and door:IsA("Model") then
				for _, pr in ipairs(door:GetDescendants()) do
					if pr:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and pr.Enabled and pr.Name ~= "KeyPrompt" and not pr.ActionText:find("Key") then
						report(("opening the door of the %s"):format(b.Name))
						if usePrompt(pr, keepGoing) then opened = true task.wait(0.8) end
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return opened
	end
	function INV.runStation(prompt, m, input, out, before, keepGoing, report)
		local placeText = prompt.ActionText
		local tool = findTool(input)
		if not tool then return "noinput" end
		local w = openWatch(promptPos(prompt) or m:GetPivot().Position)
		local inBefore = countItem(input)
		local function place()
			return INV.withBackpack(function()
				if not equip(tool) then return false end
				return usePrompt(prompt, keepGoing)
			end)
		end
		local placed = place()
		if not placed and INV.openDoors(m, keepGoing, report) then placed = place() end
		if not placed then closeWatch(w) return "unreachable" end
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while countItem(input) >= inBefore and os.clock() - t0 < 3 do task.wait(0.1) end
		if countItem(input) >= inBefore then closeWatch(w) return "refused" end
		local lastWork, steps = os.clock(), 0
		while os.clock() - t0 < 300 do
			if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then break end
			if countItem(out) > before then break end
			local dropped = false
			for _, it in ipairs(w.items) do
				if it.Parent and replicaInfo(it) == out then dropped = true break end
			end
			if dropped then break end
			local work
			for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and d.Enabled and d.ActionText ~= "" and not d.ActionText:find("^Place") then work = d break end
			end
			if work then
				steps = steps + 1
				report(("%s: %s (%s)"):format(m.Name, work.ActionText, work.ObjectText ~= "" and work.ObjectText or ("step " .. steps)))
				usePrompt(work, keepGoing)
				lastWork = os.clock()
				task.wait(0.25)
			elseif os.clock() - lastWork > 60 then
				break
			else
				task.wait(0.4)
			end
		end
		collectDrops(w, { [out] = true }, keepGoing)
		closeWatch(w)
		return countItem(out) > before and "done" or "nooutput"
	end
	function INV.process(a, keepGoing, report)
		local noteT = os.clock()
		if a.proc.job and a.proc.kind ~= "station" then
			local okJ, whyJ = ensureJob(a.proc.job, keepGoing, report)
			if not okJ then return false, whyJ end
		end
		local ok, why = INV.processStep(a, keepGoing, report)
		if ok then return ok, why end
		local msg = lastNote.t >= noteT and lastNote.text or ""
		local learned = msg:match("[Mm]ust be an? (%a+)")
		if learned and JobsFolder and JobsFolder:FindFirstChild(learned) and charVal("Job") ~= learned then
			a.proc.job = learned
			log(("learned: %s needs the %s job"):format(a.item, learned))
			report(("the game says this needs a %s: switching job"):format(learned))
			local okJ, whyJ = ensureJob(learned, keepGoing, report)
			if not okJ then return false, whyJ end
			return INV.processStep(a, keepGoing, report)
		end
		return ok, why
	end
	function INV.processStep(a, keepGoing, report)
		local p, out = a.proc, a.item
		local before = countItem(out)
		local function note() return lastNote.text ~= "" and (" (" .. lastNote.text .. ")") or "" end
		if p.kind == "smelt" then
			return false, ("%s needs smelting, which the goal can't do yet: as a Blacksmith, carry %s to the Smelter in the Blacksmith Shop, add 2 fuel, then quench the %s in the Water Barrel (5 water)"):format(out, p.input, p.heated)
		elseif p.kind == "station" then
			local list = findPrompts(p.station, true)
			if #list == 0 then
				local built, bwhy = INV.buildMissing(p.station, keepGoing, report)
				if built then return true end
				return false, "no free " .. p.station .. " loaded nearby" .. (bwhy and (" and building one failed: " .. bwhy) or "")
			end
			local tried = 0
			for _, c in ipairs(townFirst(list, promptPos)) do
				if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then return false, "stopped" end
				local prompt = c.x
				local m = prompt:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
				if m and prompt.Parent and promptPos(prompt) and not INV.lockedFor(m) and (INV.badStation[m] or 0) < os.clock() then
					tried = tried + 1
					if tried > 4 then break end
					local jv = m:FindFirstChild("Job")
					local ok, why = ensureJob(jv and jv:IsA("StringValue") and jv.Value or "", keepGoing, report)
					if not ok then return false, why end
					if not findTool(p.input) then return true end
					report(("taking %s to the %s"):format(p.input, p.station))
					local noteT = os.clock()
					local res = INV.runStation(prompt, m, p.input, out, before, keepGoing, report)
					if res == "done" or res == "noinput" then return true end
					local msg = lastNote.t >= noteT and lastNote.text or ""
					local low = msg:lower()
					if low:find("town hall", 1, true) or low:find("must", 1, true) or low:find("not allowed", 1, true) or low:find("only", 1, true) then
						return false, ("the game refused the %s: %s"):format(p.station, msg)
					end
					INV.badStation[m] = os.clock() + (res == "unreachable" and 600 or 120)
					log(("%s %s: %s%s"):format(p.station, m:GetFullName(), res, note()))
				end
			end
			local built, bwhy = INV.buildMissing(p.station, keepGoing, report)
			if built then return true end
			return false, ("no reachable %s would take your %s%s%s"):format(p.station, p.input, note(), bwhy and (" (building one failed: " .. bwhy .. ")") or "")
		elseif p.kind == "shear" then
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			if not hrp then return false, "no character" end
			local best, bd
			for _, an in ipairs(INV.animals(p.animal)) do
				local pr = an:FindFirstChild("Shear", true)
				if pr and pr:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and pr.Enabled and (INV.shorn[an] or 0) < os.clock() then
					local d = (an.HumanoidRootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
					if not bd or d < bd then best, bd = an, d end
				end
			end
			if not best then return false, "no " .. p.animal .. " ready to shear" end
			INV.shorn[best] = os.clock() + 180
			local pr = best:FindFirstChild("Shear", true)
			report(("shearing a %s (%d studs)"):format(p.animal, bd))
			local reach = math.max(pr.MaxActivationDistance - 2.5, 3)
			walkTo(best.HumanoidRootPart.Position, 2, keepGoing, function(r)
				return not best.Parent or (best.HumanoidRootPart.Position - r.Position).Magnitude <= reach
			end, 90)
			local w = openWatch(best.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
			holdPrompt(pr)
			task.wait(1)
			collectDrops(w, { [out] = true }, keepGoing)
			closeWatch(w)
			if countItem(out) > before then return true end
			return false, ("shearing gave no %s%s"):format(out, note())
		elseif p.kind == "hunt" then
			local ok, why = ensureJob(p.job or "Hunter", keepGoing, report)
			if not ok then return false, why end
			local carcass = INV.findCarcass(150)
			if not carcass then
				local weapon, dmg = nil, -1
				for _, t in ipairs(allTools()) do
					local dv = t:FindFirstChild("Damage")
					if dv and dv:IsA("ValueBase") and tonumber(dv.Value) and dv.Value > dmg then weapon, dmg = t, dv.Value end
				end
				if not weapon then return false, "need a weapon (club, spear, knife) to hunt" end
				local function penned(an)
					local r = an:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if not r then return true end
					local okB, parts = pcall(function() return Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(r.Position, 20) end)
					if okB then
						for _, pt in ipairs(parts) do
							local mdl = pt:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
							if mdl and mdl.Name:find("Fence") then return true end
						end
					end
					return false
				end
				local killed = false
				for _ = 1, 3 do
					local _, _, hrp = charParts()
					if not hrp then return false, "no character" end
					local target, td
					for _, nm in ipairs({ "Cow", "Sheep" }) do
						for _, an in ipairs(INV.animals(nm)) do
							local hm = an:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							local d = (an.HumanoidRootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
							if hm and hm.MaxHealth > 0 then d = d * (0.4 + 0.6 * hm.Health / hm.MaxHealth) end
							if (not hm or hm.Health > 0) and (INV.badAnimal[an] or 0) < os.clock() and (not td or d < td) and not penned(an) then
								target, td = an, d
							end
						end
					end
					if not target then return false, "no wild animals left to hunt (the others are in pens)" end
					report(("hunting a %s (%d studs)"):format(target.Name, td))
					local t0, bestD, bestT = os.clock(), math.huge, os.clock()
					local lastHp, lastDmg, swings, nextSwing = nil, nil, 0, 0
					local cdv = weapon:FindFirstChild("Cooldown")
					local swingCd = (cdv and tonumber(cdv.Value) or 0.8) + 0.05
					while target.Parent and os.clock() - t0 < 240 do
						if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then moveOverride = nil return false, "stopped" end
						local hm = target:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						if hm and hm.Health <= 0 then killed = true break end
						local root = target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if not root then killed = true break end
						local char, hum2, me = charParts()
						if not char then moveOverride = nil return false, "no character" end
						local off = root.Position - me.Position
						local flatD = Vector3.new(off.X, 0, off.Z).Magnitude
						if flatD < bestD - 2 then bestD, bestT = flatD, os.clock() end
						if flatD > 30 then
							moveOverride = nil
							if os.clock() - bestT > (lastDmg and 60 or 25) then break end
							if weapon.Parent == char then weapon.Parent = lp:FindFirstChild("Backpack") or weapon.Parent task.wait(0.1) end
							walkTo(root.Position, 1, keepGoing, function(r)
								return not root.Parent or Vector3.new(root.Position.X - r.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z - r.Position.Z).Magnitude <= 20
							end, 10)
							bestD = math.huge
						else
							local above = me.Position.Y - root.Position.Y
							local onTop = flatD < 2.6 and above > 1.8
							local inReach = not onTop and flatD >= 1.2 and flatD <= 3.4 and above <= 2 and above >= -4
							local away = Vector3.new(me.Position.X - root.Position.X, 0, me.Position.Z - root.Position.Z)
							if away.Magnitude < 0.1 then away = Vector3.new(me.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, me.CFrame.LookVector.Z) end
							away = away.Unit
							if inReach then
								moveOverride = nil
								if weapon.Parent ~= char then weapon.Parent = char task.wait(0.08) end
							else
								if weapon.Parent == char then weapon.Parent = lp:FindFirstChild("Backpack") or weapon.Parent end
								local spot
								if onTop then
									spot = me.Position + away * 5
								else
									local lead = root.Position + root.AssemblyLinearVelocity * 0.3
									spot = lead + away * 2.5
								end
								local dv = Vector3.new(spot.X - me.Position.X, 0, spot.Z - me.Position.Z)
								moveOverride = dv.Magnitude > 0.1 and dv.Unit or nil
								if hum2 then hum2:MoveTo(Vector3.new(spot.X, me.Position.Y, spot.Z)) end
							end
							if inReach and os.clock() >= nextSwing then
								local lead = root.Position + root.AssemblyLinearVelocity * 0.25
								me.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(me.Position, Vector3.new(lead.X, me.Position.Y, lead.Z))
								weapon:Activate()
								nextSwing = os.clock() + swingCd
								bestT = os.clock()
								swings = swings + 1
								if hm then
									if lastHp and hm.Health < lastHp then lastDmg = os.clock() end
									lastHp = hm.Health
									if (not lastDmg and swings >= 12) or (lastDmg and os.clock() - lastDmg > 30) then
										moveOverride = nil
										INV.badAnimal[target] = os.clock() + 300
										return false, ("your %s hits are not hurting the %s"):format(weapon.Name, target.Name)
									end
								end
								report(("hunting a %s (hp %s)"):format(target.Name, hm and math.floor(hm.Health) or "?"))
							end
							if os.clock() - bestT > (lastDmg and 45 or 20) then break end
							task.wait(0.05)
						end
					end
					moveOverride = nil
					if killed or not target.Parent then killed = true break end
					if not lastDmg then INV.badAnimal[target] = os.clock() + 300 end
				end
				if not killed then return false, "could not reach a wild animal" end
				task.wait(1.5)
				carcass = INV.findCarcass(60)
			end
			if not carcass then return false, "could not get a carcass" .. note() end
			report("harvesting the carcass")
			local w = openWatch(promptPos(carcass))
			for _ = 1, 10 do
				if not (carcass.Parent and carcass.Enabled) then break end
				usePrompt(carcass, keepGoing)
				task.wait(0.6)
			end
			collectDrops(w, { [out] = true, ["Animal Hide"] = true, Bone = true }, keepGoing)
			closeWatch(w)
			if countItem(out) > before then return true end
			return false, ("the carcass gave no %s%s"):format(out, note())
		end
		return false, p.why or ("can't make " .. out)
	end

	INV.FOOD = {}
	for _, folder in ipairs({ RS:FindFirstChild("LootDrops"), RS:FindFirstChild("Craftables") }) do
		if folder then
			for _, x in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
				if CollectionService:HasTag(x, "Food") and not CollectionService:HasTag(x, "Cookable") then
					local hv = x:FindFirstChild("Hunger") or x:FindFirstChild("Food")
					INV.FOOD[x.Name] = hv and hv:IsA("ValueBase") and tonumber(hv.Value) or 10
				end
			end
		end
	end
	function INV.waterParts(from, maxD)
		local best, bd, be
		for _, part in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("Water")) do
			if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "Saltwater" then
				local o = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(from)
				local h = part.Size * 0.5
				local edge = part.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(math.clamp(o.X, -h.X, h.X), math.clamp(o.Y, -h.Y, h.Y), math.clamp(o.Z, -h.Z, h.Z)))
				local d = (edge - from).Magnitude
				if d <= (maxD or math.huge) and (not bd or d < bd) then best, bd, be = part, d, edge end
			end
		end
		return best, bd, be
	end
	function INV.inWaterBox(part)
		return function(r)
			local o = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(r.Position)
			local e = part.Size * 0.5 + Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
			return math.abs(o.X) <= e.X and math.abs(o.Y) <= e.Y and math.abs(o.Z) <= e.Z
		end
	end
	function INV.drinkAt(ws, keepGoing, report)
		local start = charVal("Thirst") or 0
		for _ = 1, 10 do
			if (charVal("Thirst") or 100) >= 95 or (keepGoing and not keepGoing()) then break end
			report(("drinking (thirst %d)"):format(charVal("Thirst") or 0))
			pcall(function() ws:FireServer("Drinking") end)
			task.wait(2.1)
			pcall(function() ws:FireServer("Drank") end)
			task.wait(0.1)
			pcall(function() ws:FireServer("Stopped") end)
			task.wait(0.3)
		end
		return (charVal("Thirst") or 0) > start or (charVal("Thirst") or 0) >= 95
	end
	function INV.drinkTrip(keepGoing, report)
		local ws = RS:FindFirstChild("WaterSource")
		local _, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not (ws and hrp) then return false end
		local part, pd, pe = INV.waterParts(hrp.Position)
		local well, wd
		for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if d:IsA("Model") and d.Name == "Well" then
				local wv = d:FindFirstChild("Water")
				if not wv or (tonumber(wv.Value) or 0) >= 5 then
					local dist = flat(d:GetPivot().Position, hrp.Position)
					if dist <= 900 and (not wd or dist < wd) and not (S.avoidSea and Sea.crosses(hrp.Position, d:GetPivot().Position)) then well, wd = d, dist end
				end
			end
		end
		if part and (pd > 900 or (S.avoidSea and Sea.crosses(hrp.Position, pe))) then part, pd = nil, nil end
		if well and (not pd or wd + 40 < pd) then
			local wp = well:GetPivot().Position
			report(("thirsty: walking to a well (%d studs)"):format(wd))
			pcall(function() lp:RequestStreamAroundAsync(wp, 5) end)
			walkToModel(wp, 8, keepGoing)
			local wpart = INV.waterParts(wp, 25)
			local _, _, h2 = charParts()
			local near = h2 and (wpart and INV.inWaterBox(wpart)(h2) or flat(wp, h2.Position) <= 12)
			if near and INV.drinkAt(ws, keepGoing, report) then return true end
			_, _, hrp = charParts()
			if not hrp then return false end
			part, pd, pe = INV.waterParts(hrp.Position)
			if part and (pd > 900 or (S.avoidSea and Sea.crosses(hrp.Position, pe))) then part, pd = nil, nil end
		end
		if not part then return false, "no fresh water within 900 studs" end
		local atWater = INV.inWaterBox(part)
		if not atWater(hrp) then
			local o = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(hrp.Position)
			local h = part.Size * 0.5
			local edge = part.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(math.clamp(o.X, -h.X, h.X), h.Y, math.clamp(o.Z, -h.Z, h.Z)))
			report(("thirsty: walking to water (%d studs)"):format(flat(edge, hrp.Position)))
			walkTo(groundAt(edge) or edge, 2, keepGoing, atWater, 150)
		end
		_, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not (hrp and atWater(hrp)) then return false, "could not reach water" end
		return INV.drinkAt(ws, keepGoing, report)
	end
	function INV.eatFromBag(report)
		for _ = 1, 8 do
			if (charVal("Hunger") or 100) >= 90 then return true end
			local food, best = nil, -1
			for _, t in ipairs(allTools()) do
				local v = INV.FOOD[t.Name]
				if v and v > best then food, best = t, v end
			end
			if not food then return false end
			report(("eating %s (hunger %d)"):format(food.Name, charVal("Hunger") or 0))
			if equip(food) then food:Activate() end
			task.wait(0.8)
		end
		return true
	end
	function INV.foodSources(hrp)
		local list = {}
		for _, m in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
			local base, h, p = replicaInfo(m)
			if base and INV.FOOD[base] then
				local d = (h.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
				if d <= 250 then table.insert(list, { kind = "ground", name = base, d = d, prompt = p, pos = h.Position }) end
			end
		end
		for _, name in ipairs({ "Prickly Pear", "Apple", "Coconut", "Berry" }) do
			for _, pr in ipairs(findPrompts(name, true)) do
				local pos = pr.ActionText == "Take" and promptPos(pr)
				if pos then table.insert(list, { kind = "plant", name = name, d = (pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude, prompt = pr, pos = pos }) end
			end
		end
		for _, cp in ipairs({ { "Corn Plant", "Corn" }, { "Carrot Plant", "Carrot" }, { "Pumpkin Plant", "Pumpkin" } }) do
			if INV.FOOD[cp[2]] then
				local node, nd = nearestNode(cp[1], hrp.Position, 1500)
				if node then table.insert(list, { kind = "crop", name = cp[2], d = nd, node = node }) end
			end
		end
		local hall = myTownHall()
		if hall then
			local seen = {}
			for pr in pairs(promptCache.all) do
				local c, inv = nil, nil
				if pr.Name == "OpenPrompt" and pr:IsDescendantOf(hall) then
					local a = pr.Parent
					for _ = 1, 5 do
						if not a or a == hall then break end
						if a.ClassName == "Model" then
							local iv = a:FindFirstChild("Inventory")
							if iv then c, inv = a, iv break end
						end
						a = a.Parent
					end
				end
				if c and not seen[c] then
					seen[c] = true
					for _, it in ipairs(inv:GetChildren()) do
						local base = it.Name:match("^(.-)_Replica$")
						if base and INV.FOOD[base] then
							table.insert(list, { kind = "chest", name = base, d = flat(c:GetPivot().Position, hrp.Position), chest = c })
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		for i = #list, 1, -1 do
			local x = list[i]
			local cap = x.kind == "chest" and S.homeMaxDist or 700
			local p = x.pos or (x.node and nodePos[x.node]) or (x.chest and x.chest:GetPivot().Position)
			if x.d > cap or (S.avoidSea and p and Sea.crosses(hrp.Position, p)) then table.remove(list, i) end
		end
		table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.d < b.d end)
		return list
	end
	function INV.eatTrip(keepGoing, report)
		if INV.eatFromBag(report) then return true end
		local tempGlitch = false
		if not INV.glitch and INV.full() then
			report("hungry and inventory full: holding everything so food can be picked up")
			INV.setGlitch(true)
			tempGlitch = true
		end
		local ok, why = INV.eatTripInner(keepGoing, report)
		if tempGlitch and not INV.autoGlitch then INV.setGlitch(false) end
		return ok, why
	end
	function INV.eatTripInner(keepGoing, report)
		local where = { ground = "the ground", plant = "a plant", crop = "a crop", chest = "your town chest" }
		for _ = 1, 5 do
			if INV.eatFromBag(report) then return true end
			if (charVal("Hunger") or 100) >= math.min(90, (INV.hungerMin or 40) + 30) then return true end
			if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then return false end
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			if not hrp then return false end
			local src = INV.foodSources(hrp)[1]
			if not src then return false, "no food anywhere nearby" end
			report(("hungry: getting %s from %s (%d studs)"):format(src.name, where[src.kind], src.d))
			if src.kind == "chest" then
				INV.retrieve({ [src.name] = 2 }, {}, keepGoing, report, src.chest)
			elseif src.kind == "crop" then
				harvestNode(src.node, { [src.name] = true }, keepGoing)
			else
				local w = openWatch(src.pos)
				usePrompt(src.prompt, keepGoing)
				collectDrops(w, { [src.name] = true }, keepGoing)
				closeWatch(w)
			end
		end
		return INV.eatFromBag(report)
	end
	function INV.goHome(keepGoing, report)
		report = report or function() end
		local hall, hallPos = myTownHall()
		if not hall then return false, "you don't have a town hall (join or make a civ first)" end
		local _, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not hrp then return false, "no character" end
		local d = flat(hallPos, hrp.Position)
		local r = hallRadius(hall) + 6
		if d <= r + 4 then report("at your town hall") return true end
		report(("walking back to your town hall (%d studs)"):format(d))
		pcall(function() lp:RequestStreamAroundAsync(hallPos, 5) end)
		local ok = walkToModel(hallPos, r, keepGoing)
		report(ok and "back at your town hall" or "could not reach your town hall")
		return ok
	end
	function INV.surviveCheck(keepGoing, report)
		if not INV.survive then return end
		local now = os.clock()
		if (charVal("Thirst") or 100) < INV.thirstMin and now > (INV.drinkRetryT or 0) then
			local before = charVal("Thirst") or 0
			local ok, why = INV.drinkTrip(keepGoing, report)
			if not ok and (charVal("Thirst") or 0) <= before then
				INV.drinkRetryT = os.clock() + 90
				report("no drinkable fresh water reached" .. (why and (" (" .. tostring(why) .. ")") or "") .. ", retrying in 90s")
			end
		end
		now = os.clock()
		if (charVal("Hunger") or 100) < INV.hungerMin and now > (INV.eatRetryT or 0) then
			local before = charVal("Hunger") or 0
			local ok, why = INV.eatTrip(keepGoing, report)
			if not ok and (charVal("Hunger") or 0) <= before then
				INV.eatRetryT = os.clock() + 90
				report("no food reached" .. (why and (" (" .. tostring(why) .. ")") or "") .. ", retrying in 90s")
			end
		end
	end
	INV.hist = {}
	function INV.goalReport(t)
		if t == S.goalStatus then return end
		S.goalStatus = t
		local stamp = os.date("%H:%M:%S")
		local last = INV.hist[#INV.hist]
		if not (last and last:sub(10) == t) then
			table.insert(INV.hist, stamp .. " " .. t)
			while #INV.hist > 6 do table.remove(INV.hist, 1) end
		end
		dlog("goal:", t)
	end
	function INV.turnOnGlitch(report)
		if INV.glitch then return end
		log("inventory full: Inventory Glitch switched on by the goal")
		report("inventory full: turning on Inventory Glitch")
		INV.setGlitch(true)
		if API.onGlitch then pcall(API.onGlitch, true) end
	end

	function INV.goalSet(item, set, depth)
		set, depth = set or {}, depth or 0
		if depth > 10 or set[item] then return set end
		set[item] = true
		local rec = RECIPES[item]
		if rec then for ing in pairs(rec.req) do INV.goalSet(ing, set, depth + 1) end end
		return set
	end
	function INV.keepTools()
		local keep = {}
		for _, kind in ipairs({ "Axe", "Pick" }) do
			local b = bestToolFor(kind)
			if b then keep[b] = true end
		end
		return keep
	end
	function INV.earlyCraft(item, qty, depth)
		depth = depth or 0
		local rec = RECIPES[item]
		if depth > 10 or not rec then return nil end
		local missing = qty - (countItem(item) + (INV.stash[item] or 0))
		if missing <= 0 then return nil end
		for ing, per in pairs(rec.req) do
			local found = INV.earlyCraft(ing, per * missing, depth + 1)
			if found then return found end
		end
		local used = 0
		for ing, per in pairs(rec.req) do
			if countItem(ing) < per then return nil end
			used = used + per
		end
		if used <= 1 or (rec.job ~= "" and rec.job ~= charVal("Job")) then return nil end
		return item
	end
	function INV.junk(goalItem, limit)
		local set, keep, out = INV.goalSet(goalItem), INV.keepTools(), {}
		for _, t in ipairs(allTools()) do
			if #out >= limit then break end
			if not set[t.Name] and not keep[t] and not t:FindFirstChild("Damage") then table.insert(out, t) end
		end
		return out
	end
	function INV.makeRoom(goalItem, goalQty, gatherItem, keepGoing, report)
		local early = INV.earlyCraft(goalItem, goalQty)
		if early then
			report(("inventory full: crafting %s now to free space"):format(early))
			if craft(early, keepGoing, report) then return true end
		end
		local fullMsg = "inventory full (9/9): turn on Inventory Glitch or free a slot"
		if not INV.useChest then
			INV.turnOnGlitch(report)
			return true
		end
		local set, keep, cands = INV.goalSet(goalItem), INV.keepTools(), {}
		for _, t in ipairs(allTools()) do
			if set[t.Name] and t.Name ~= gatherItem and t.Name ~= goalItem and not keep[t] then table.insert(cands, t) end
		end
		local stored, why = 0, nil
		if #cands > 0 then stored, why = INV.deposit(cands, INV.stash, keepGoing, report) end
		if stored == 0 and INV.parkJunk then stored, why = INV.deposit(INV.junk(goalItem, 3), INV.parked, keepGoing, report) end
		if stored > 0 then return true end
		report("no chest would take your items: using Inventory Glitch instead" .. (why and (" (" .. why .. ")") or ""))
		task.wait(1)
		INV.turnOnGlitch(report)
		return true
	end
	function INV.retrieveFor(item, goalItem, keepGoing, report)
		local rec = RECIPES[item]
		if not rec then return true end
		local want, need = {}, 0
		for ing, per in pairs(rec.req) do
			local s, short = INV.stash[ing] or 0, per - countItem(ing)
			if s > 0 and short > 0 then want[ing] = math.min(s, short) need = need + want[ing] end
		end
		if need == 0 then return true end
		if not INV.glitch and INV.autoGlitch and INV.cap - INV.backpackCount() < need then INV.turnOnGlitch(report) end
		if not INV.glitch and INV.cap - INV.backpackCount() < need then
			local short = need - (INV.cap - INV.backpackCount())
			if INV.parkJunk then INV.deposit(INV.junk(goalItem, short), INV.parked, keepGoing, report) end
			if INV.cap - INV.backpackCount() < need then INV.turnOnGlitch(report) end
		end
		local got, why = INV.retrieve(want, INV.stash, keepGoing, report)
		if got == 0 and why then return false, why end
		return true
	end
	function INV.finish(report)
		local keep = function() return alive end
		for _, bucket in ipairs({ INV.parked, INV.stash }) do
			local want = {}
			for n, c in pairs(bucket) do if c > 0 then want[n] = c end end
			if next(want) ~= nil then pcall(INV.retrieve, want, bucket, keep, report) end
		end
		local left = 0
		for _, bucket in ipairs({ INV.parked, INV.stash }) do for _, c in pairs(bucket) do left = left + c end end
		if left == 0 then INV.chestModel = nil end
		return left
	end

	function INV.needs(item, qty, acc, depth)
		acc, depth = acc or {}, depth or 0
		if depth > 10 then return acc end
		local missing = qty - (countItem(item) + (INV.stash[item] or 0))
		if missing <= 0 then return acc end
		acc[item] = (acc[item] or 0) + missing
		local rec = RECIPES[item]
		local proc = not rec and INV.PROC[item]
		if rec then
			for ing, per in pairs(rec.req) do INV.needs(ing, per * missing, acc, depth + 1) end
		elseif proc and proc.input then
			INV.needs(proc.input, missing, acc, depth + 1)
		end
		return acc
	end
	function INV.grabGround(goalItem, goalQty, keepGoing, report)
		local _, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not hrp then return 0 end
		local need = INV.needs(goalItem, goalQty)
		need[goalItem] = nil
		if next(need) == nil then return 0 end
		local list = {}
		for _, m in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
			local base, h, p = replicaInfo(m)
			if base and (need[base] or 0) > 0 then
				local d = (h.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
				if d <= 150 then table.insert(list, { base = base, h = h, p = p, d = d }) end
			end
		end
		table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.d < b.d end)
		local got = 0
		for i = 1, math.min(#list, 6) do
			local e = list[i]
			if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then break end
			if (need[e.base] or 0) > 0 and e.p.Parent and e.p.Enabled and (INV.glitch or not INV.full()) then
				report(("grabbing %s off the ground (%d studs)"):format(e.base, e.d))
				local before = countItem(e.base)
				usePrompt(e.p, keepGoing)
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while countItem(e.base) <= before and os.clock() - t0 < 1.5 do task.wait(0.1) end
				if countItem(e.base) > before then
					got = got + 1
					need[e.base] = need[e.base] - 1
					pickupReadyT = os.clock() + 1.5
				end
			end
		end
		return got
	end

	local function obtain(item, qty, keepGoing, report)
		report = report or function() end
		local lastSig, same, craftFails = nil, 0, {}
		local lastGroundScan = 0
		while true do
			if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then return false, "stopped" end
			local char = charParts()
			if not char then
				report("waiting for your character")
				task.wait(1)
			elseif inCombat() then
				report("paused: in combat")
				task.wait(1)
			else
				INV.surviveCheck(keepGoing, report)
				if os.clock() - lastGroundScan > 8 then
					lastGroundScan = os.clock()
					pcall(INV.grabGround, item, qty, keepGoing, report)
				end
				local a = nextAction(item, qty)
				if not a then return true end
				if a.fail then return false, a.fail end
				local sig = a.kind .. ":" .. a.item .. ":" .. countItem(a.item) .. ":" .. tostring(charVal("Job") or "none")
				if sig == lastSig then same = same + 1 else lastSig, same = sig, 0 end
				if same >= 10 then return false, "no progress on " .. a.kind .. " " .. a.item end
				local addsItems = a.kind == "gather" or (a.kind == "process" and (a.proc.kind == "shear" or a.proc.kind == "hunt"))
				if addsItems and not INV.glitch and INV.full() then
					if INV.autoGlitch or (INV.useChest and not INV.chestModel and not INV.findChest()) then
						if not INV.autoGlitch then report("inventory full and you have no chest of your own: using Inventory Glitch") task.wait(1) end
						INV.turnOnGlitch(report)
					else
						local roomOk, roomWhy = INV.makeRoom(item, qty, a.item, keepGoing, report)
						if not roomOk then return false, roomWhy end
					end
				elseif a.kind == "process" then
					local ok, why = INV.process(a, keepGoing, report)
					if ok then
						craftFails[a.item] = 0
					else
						craftFails[a.item] = (craftFails[a.item] or 0) + 1
						report(("%s failed (%d/3): %s"):format(a.item, craftFails[a.item], tostring(why)))
						if craftFails[a.item] >= 3 or a.proc.kind == "smelt" or tostring(why):find("the game refused", 1, true) or tostring(why):find("needs a tier", 1, true) then return false, tostring(why) end
						task.wait(1.5)
					end
				elseif a.kind == "gather" then
					local _, _, hrp = charParts()
					local _, hallPos = myTownHall()
					if hallPos and flat(hallPos, hrp.Position) > S.homeMaxDist then hallPos = nil end
					local from = (S.homeMode and hallPos) or hrp.Position
					local me = S.avoidSea and hrp.Position or nil
					local node = nearestReachable(a.node, from, 3000, me) or nearestReachable(a.node, hrp.Position, 6000, me)
					if not node then
						report("waiting for a " .. a.node .. " to respawn")
						task.wait(3)
					else
						report(("gathering %s from %s (%d/%d)"):format(a.item, a.node, countItem(a.item) + (INV.stash[a.item] or 0), a.want))
						local set = { [a.item] = true, Sapling = S.pick.Sapling or nil }
						local ok, why = harvestNode(node, set, keepGoing)
						if not ok then report("node skipped: " .. tostring(why)) end
					end
				elseif a.kind == "craft" then
					local rOk, rWhy = INV.retrieveFor(a.item, item, keepGoing, report)
					if not rOk then return false, rWhy end
					local ok, why = craft(a.item, keepGoing, report)
					if ok then
						craftFails[a.item] = 0
					else
						craftFails[a.item] = (craftFails[a.item] or 0) + 1
						report("craft failed (" .. craftFails[a.item] .. "/4): " .. tostring(why))
						if craftFails[a.item] >= 4 then return false, tostring(why) end
						task.wait(1.5)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function goalKeep() return alive and S.goalOn end
	local function goalStopped(msg)
		S.goalOn = false
		S.goalStatus = msg
		notify("Goal", msg, 8)
		if API.onGoalStopped then pcall(API.onGoalStopped) end
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		while alive do
			if S.goalOn then
				INV.hist = {}
				local ok, res, why
				for attempt = 1, 5 do
					ok, res, why = pcall(obtain, S.goalItem, S.goalQty, goalKeep, INV.goalReport)
					if ok or not (alive and S.goalOn) then break end
					log("goal step error, retrying (" .. attempt .. "/5)", res)
					INV.goalReport("hit an error, retrying " .. attempt .. "/5")
					task.wait(1.5)
				end
				local leftInChest = INV.finish(INV.goalReport)
				if INV.goalHome and alive then INV.goHome(function() return alive end, INV.goalReport) end
				if leftInChest > 0 then
					notify("Goal", ("%d stored item(s) are still in the chest (no room to carry them)"):format(leftInChest), 8)
				end
				if not ok then
					log("goal error", res)
					goalStopped("error: " .. tostring(res))
				elseif S.goalOn then
					goalStopped(res and ("done: %dx %s"):format(countItem(S.goalItem), S.goalItem) or ("stopped: " .. tostring(why)))
				end
			end
			task.wait(0.3)
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while alive do
			local waited = false
			if S.aura and not S.farm and not S.goalOn then
				local char, _, hrp = charParts()
				local tool = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
				if tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Harvest") and not inCombat() then
					local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
					for m, t in pairs(nodes) do
						local p = nodePos[m]
						if p and flat(p, hrp.Position) < 14 then
							local kind = toolKindFor(t)
							if kind and toolProvides(tool, kind) and nodeAlive(m) then
								local d = flat(p, hrp.Position)
								if d < bestD then best, bestD = m, d end
							end
						end
					end
					if best and bestD <= 9 then
						local p = nodePos[best]
						hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(p.X, hrp.Position.Y, p.Z))
						tool:Activate()
						local cdv = tool:FindFirstChild("Cooldown")
						task.wait((cdv and cdv.Value or 0.6) + 0.08)
						waited = true
					end
				end
			end
			if not waited then task.wait(0.2) end
		end
	end)

	local Builder = (function()
		local B = { structure = "Wooden Shack", spot = nil, rot = 0, site = nil, running = false, status = "idle", ghost = nil, badItem = {} }
		local Placing = RS:FindFirstChild("Placing")
		local BuildGui = RS:FindFirstChild("BuildGui")
		local Deployables = RS:FindFirstChild("Deployables")
		local MaterialsF = RS:FindFirstChild("Materials")

		B.list = {}
		if Deployables then
			for _, d in ipairs(Deployables:GetChildren()) do
				local st, mat = d:FindFirstChild("Station"), d:FindFirstChild("Material")
				if d:IsA("Model") and st and st.Value >= 1 and mat and mat.Value and not d.Name:find("TownHall") then
					table.insert(B.list, d.Name)
				end
			end
			table.sort(B.list)
		end
		if not table.find(B.list, B.structure) then B.structure = B.list[1] or "" end

		local function report(t) B.status = t end
		function B.info(name)
			local d = Deployables and Deployables:FindFirstChild(name or B.structure)
			if not d then return "?" end
			local job = d:FindFirstChild("Job") and d.Job.Value or ""
			local cat = d:FindFirstChild("Category") and d.Category.Value or ""
			return ("%s \194\183 Building Plan tier %d%s%s"):format(d.Name, d.Station.Value, job ~= "" and (" \194\183 job " .. job) or "", cat ~= "" and (" \194\183 " .. cat) or "")
		end

		local function placementCF(tpl, ground)
			local halfY = tpl:GetExtentsSize().Y / 2
			return CFrame.new(ground) * CFrame.new(0, halfY, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, B.rot, 0)
		end
		local function spotBlocked(tpl, cf)
			local det = tpl:FindFirstChild("PlacementDetector")
			if not det then return false end
			local pos = (cf * tpl:GetPivot():ToObjectSpace(det.CFrame)).Position
			for _, v in ipairs(Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(pos, 5)) do
				if (v.Name == "Airblock" or v.Name == "PlacementDetector") and not (B.ghost and v:IsDescendantOf(B.ghost)) then return true end
			end
			return false
		end
		local function clearGhost() if B.ghost then B.ghost:Destroy() B.ghost = nil end end
		local function drawGhost()
			clearGhost()
			local d = Deployables and Deployables:FindFirstChild(B.structure)
			if not (d and B.spot) then return end
			local g = d:Clone()
			for _, x in ipairs(g:GetDescendants()) do
				if x:IsA("BasePart") then
					x.Anchored, x.CanCollide, x.CanQuery, x.CanTouch = true, false, false, false
					x.Transparency = math.max(x.Transparency, 0.6)
				elseif x:IsA("Script") or x:IsA("LocalScript") or x:IsA("ProximityPrompt") or x:IsA("Sound") then
					x:Destroy()
				end
			end
			g.Name = "LZ_BuildGhost"
			g:PivotTo(placementCF(d, B.spot))
			local blocked = spotBlocked(d, placementCF(d, B.spot))
			local h = Instance.new("Highlight")
			h.FillTransparency, h.OutlineTransparency = 0.8, 0
			h.OutlineColor = blocked and Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70) or Color3.fromRGB(85, 255, 127)
			h.FillColor = h.OutlineColor
			h.Parent = g
			g.Parent = Workspace.CurrentCamera
			B.ghost = g
			return not blocked
		end
		function B.setSpot(pos)
			if not pos then return false, "no ground there" end
			B.spot = groundAt(pos) or pos
			local ok = drawGhost()
			return true, ok and "spot looks clear" or "something is in the way here (red), pick another spot"
		end
		function B.rotate(deg)
			B.rot = (B.rot + math.rad(deg)) % (math.pi * 2)
			if B.spot then drawGhost() end
		end
		function B.setStructure(name)
			B.structure = name
			if B.spot then drawGhost() end
		end
		B.clearSpot = function() B.spot = nil clearGhost() end

		local function siteParts(site)
			local base = site:FindFirstChild("Base")
			local att = base and base:FindFirstChild("Attachment")
			return att and att:FindFirstChild("Requirements"), att and att:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt"), att
		end
		local function isMySite(m)
			if not (m.Name:find("Material$") and m.ClassName == "Model") then return false end
			local bl = m:FindFirstChild("BuildLog")
			return bl ~= nil and bl.Value ~= nil and bl.Value:IsDescendantOf(lp)
		end
		local function mySites()
			local out = {}
			for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do if isMySite(c) then table.insert(out, c) end end
			return out
		end
		local function isAnySite(m)
			if not (m.Name:find("Material$") and m.ClassName == "Model") then return false end
			local base = m:FindFirstChild("Base")
			local att = base and base:FindFirstChild("Attachment")
			return att ~= nil and att:FindFirstChild("Requirements") ~= nil
		end
		local function workableSites()
			if S.buildOnlyMine then return mySites() end
			local out = {}
			for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do if isAnySite(c) then table.insert(out, c) end end
			return out
		end
		B.sites = workableSites
		local function remaining(site)
			local req = siteParts(site)
			local t, total = {}, 0
			if req then
				for _, v in ipairs(req:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("IntValue") and v.Value > 0 then t[v.Name] = v.Value total = total + v.Value end
				end
			end
			return t, total, req ~= nil
		end
		local function needText(t)
			local l = {}
			for k, v in pairs(t) do table.insert(l, v .. " " .. k) end
			table.sort(l)
			return #l > 0 and table.concat(l, ", ") or "nothing"
		end
		B.mySites = mySites
		B.needText = function(site) return needText((remaining(site))) end

		local function planTier(prompt)
			local st = stationTier(prompt)
			if st and st > 0 then return st end
			local m = prompt:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			return m and tonumber(m.Name:match("(%d+)%s*$") or "") or 1
		end
		local function closeBuildGui()
			local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
			local g = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("Building1")
			if g and g:IsA("ScreenGui") then g.Enabled = false end
		end
		local function openBuildPlan(tier, keepGoing)
			local list = {}
			for _, p in ipairs(findPrompts("Open")) do
				if p.ObjectText:find("Building Plan") and planTier(p) >= tier then table.insert(list, p) end
			end
			if #list == 0 then return false, "no tier " .. tier .. " Building Plan on the map" end
			local cands = townFirst(list, promptPos)
			for i = 1, math.min(4, #cands) do
				local opened
				local conn = BuildGui and BuildGui.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(t) opened = t end)
				report(("walking to a tier %d Building Plan"):format(planTier(cands[i].x)))
				local used = usePrompt(cands[i].x, keepGoing)
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while used and not opened and os.clock() - t0 < 3 do task.wait(0.1) end
