				if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
				if opened and opened >= tier then closeBuildGui() return true end
				if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then return false, "stopped" end
			end
			closeBuildGui()
			return false, "no Building Plan opened for you (other towns' plans refuse)"
		end

		local function deployAtSpot(name, keepGoing)
			local tpl = (MaterialsF and MaterialsF:FindFirstChild(name)) or (Deployables and Deployables:FindFirstChild(name))
			if not tpl then return false, "unknown template " .. tostring(name) end
			report("walking to the build spot")
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			if hrp and flat(B.spot, hrp.Position) > 30 then walkTo(B.spot, 25, keepGoing, nil, 300) end
			_, _, hrp = charParts()
			if not hrp or flat(B.spot, hrp.Position) > 45 then return false, "could not get within placing range of the spot" end
			local base = tpl:FindFirstChild("Base")
			local rel = base and tpl:GetPivot():ToObjectSpace(base.CFrame)
			local tries, lastReason = 0, nil
			for _, c in ipairs(spiral(B.spot, 3, 12)) do
				if tries >= 10 or (keepGoing and not keepGoing()) then break end
				local g = (c == B.spot) and B.spot or groundAt(c)
				local cf = g and placementCF(tpl, g)
				if cf and not spotBlocked(tpl, cf) then
					local wait = math.max(S.plantCooldown - (os.clock() - lastDeployT), serverPlaceReadyT - os.clock())
					if wait > 0 then task.wait(wait) end
					tries = tries + 1
					local callT = os.clock()
					local ok, res = pcall(DeployRem.InvokeServer, DeployRem, name, cf, rel and (cf * rel) or false)
					lastDeployT = os.clock()
					if ok and res then B.placedAt = g return true end
					task.wait(0.3)
					local note = lastNote.t >= callT and lastNote.text or ""
					lastReason = note ~= "" and note or lastReason
					local secs = tonumber(note:lower():match("wait ([%d%.]+) sec") or "")
					if secs then serverPlaceReadyT = callT + secs + 0.1 end
					dlog("build deploy rejected", note)
				end
			end
			return false, "server rejected the spot" .. (lastReason and (" (" .. lastReason .. ")") or "")
		end

		local function placeSite(keepGoing)
			local dep = Deployables and Deployables:FindFirstChild(B.structure)
			if not dep then return nil, "unknown structure " .. tostring(B.structure) end
			if not B.spot then return nil, "mark a build spot first" end
			if not Placing then return nil, "Placing remote missing" end
			local job = dep:FindFirstChild("Job") and dep.Job.Value or ""
			if job ~= "" then
				local ok, why = ensureJob(job, keepGoing, report)
				if not ok then return nil, why end
			end
			local ok, why = openBuildPlan(dep.Station.Value, keepGoing)
			if not ok then return nil, why end
			local before = {}
			for _, s in ipairs(mySites()) do before[s] = true end
			local old = getcallbackvalue and getcallbackvalue(Placing, "OnClientInvoke")
			local placed, reason = false, nil
			Placing.OnClientInvoke = function(name)
				local okP, r1, r2 = pcall(deployAtSpot, name, keepGoing)
				placed, reason = okP and r1 == true, okP and r2 or tostring(r1)
				return placed
			end
			report("placing " .. B.structure)
			local noteT = os.clock()
			local okI, res = pcall(Placing.InvokeServer, Placing, dep)
			Placing.OnClientInvoke = old
			closeBuildGui()
			if not okI then return nil, "placing error: " .. tostring(res) end
			if not placed then
				local note = lastNote.t >= noteT and lastNote.text or ""
				return nil, reason or (note ~= "" and note) or "the server did not start placing (tier/job?)"
			end
			local t0 = os.clock()
			while os.clock() - t0 < 5 do
				for _, s in ipairs(mySites()) do
					if not before[s] then return s end
				end
				task.wait(0.2)
			end
			return nil, "placed, but the construction site did not show up"
		end

		local function freeHeavy(c)
			local car = c:FindFirstChild("Carrier")
			return car ~= nil and car.Value == "" and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt") ~= nil
		end
		local function candidateItem(name, sitePos)
			local _, hallPos = myTownHall()
			local best, bd
			for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
				if c.Name == name and c:IsA("BasePart") and (B.badItem[c] or 0) < os.clock() and freeHeavy(c) then
					local mine = myHeavy[c] or flat(c.Position, sitePos) <= 40 or (hallPos and flat(c.Position, hallPos) <= 60)
					if mine then
						local d = flat(c.Position, sitePos)
						if not bd or d < bd then best, bd = c, d end
					end
				end
			end
			return best
		end

		local function deliverHeld(site, keepGoing)
			local held = carried()
			if not held then return false, "not carrying anything" end
			local req, prompt, att = siteParts(site)
			if not (req and prompt and att) then return false, "site is not loaded yet" end
			local reqVal = req:FindFirstChild(held.Name)
			if not reqVal or reqVal.Value <= 0 then dropCarried() return false, held.Name .. " is not needed" end
			local reach = math.max(prompt.MaxActivationDistance - 2, 3)
			report(("carrying %s to the site"):format(held.Name))
			local okW = walkTo(att.WorldPosition, 1, keepGoing, function(r) return (att.WorldPosition - r.Position).Magnitude <= reach end, 180)
			if not okW then return false, "could not reach the site" end
			local before = reqVal.Value
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				local noteT = os.clock()
				holdPrompt(prompt)
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while reqVal.Parent and reqVal.Value >= before and carried() and os.clock() - t0 < 2.5 do task.wait(0.1) end
				if not reqVal.Parent or reqVal.Value < before or not carried() then return true end
				dlog("site prompt did not take it:", lastNote.t >= noteT and lastNote.text or "")
			end
			return false, "the site would not take the " .. held.Name .. (lastNote.text ~= "" and (" (" .. lastNote.text .. ")") or "")
		end

		local function pickUp(item, keepGoing)
			local prompt = item:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
			if not prompt then return false end
			report("picking up " .. item.Name)
			local ok = walkTo(item.Position, 1, keepGoing, function(r)
				return item.Parent == nil or (item.Position - r.Position).Magnitude <= 6
			end, 60)
			if not ok or not item.Parent then return false end
			for _ = 1, 2 do
				holdPrompt(prompt)
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while carried() ~= item and os.clock() - t0 < 2 do task.wait(0.1) end
				if carried() == item then return true end
			end
			return false
		end

		local function gatherOne(name, site, keepGoing)
			local sitePos = site:GetPivot().Position
			local item = candidateItem(name, sitePos)
			if item then
				if pickUp(item, keepGoing) then return deliverHeld(site, keepGoing) end
				B.badItem[item] = os.clock() + 45
				return false, "could not pick up the " .. name
			end
			if not HEAVY[name] then
				local ok, why = obtain(name, 1, keepGoing, report)
				if not ok then return false, why end
				local tool = findTool(name)
				if tool then equip(tool) end
				local _, prompt, att = siteParts(site)
				if prompt and att then
					usePrompt(prompt, keepGoing)
					task.wait(1)
					return true
				end
				return false, "site not loaded"
			end
			local src = chooseSource(name, sitePos)
			if not src then return false, "nothing on the map drops " .. name end
			local kind = toolKindFor(src.node)
			if kind and not bestToolFor(kind) then
				local toolName = cheapestToolRecipe(kind)
				if not toolName then return false, "no recipe gives a " .. kind end
				local ok, why = obtain(toolName, 1, keepGoing, report)
				if not ok then return false, "need a " .. kind .. ": " .. tostring(why) end
			end
			local node = nearestNode(src.node, sitePos, 2500)
			if not node then report("waiting for a " .. src.node .. " to respawn") task.wait(3) return false, "no " .. src.node .. " nearby" end
			report(("getting %s: harvesting %s"):format(name, src.node))
			local ok, why = harvestNode(node, { Sapling = S.pick.Sapling or nil }, keepGoing)
			if not ok then return false, why end
			return "harvested"
		end

		local function stop(msg)
			B.running = false
			B.status = msg
			notify("Build", msg, 8)
			if B.onStopped then pcall(B.onStopped) end
		end
		local function keep() return alive and B.running end
		local function runBuild()
			local site = B.site
			if not (site and site.Parent) then
				site = nil
				for _, s in ipairs(workableSites()) do
					local fo = select(3, siteParts(s))
					fo = fo and fo:FindFirstChild("FinishedObject")
					if fo and fo.Value and fo.Value.Name == B.structure and (not B.spot or flat(s:GetPivot().Position, B.spot) < 30) then site = s break end
				end
				if not site then
					local s, why = placeSite(keep)
					if not s then return stop("stopped: " .. tostring(why)) end
					site = s
					notify("Build", B.structure .. " site placed, gathering materials", 5)
				end
			end
			B.site = site
			clearGhost()
			local fails, lastTotal, lastWhy, rounds = 0, math.huge, nil, 0
			while keep() do
				if not site.Parent then return stop(("built %s!"):format(B.structure)) end
				local char = charParts()
				if not char then
					report("waiting for your character")
					task.wait(1)
				elseif inCombat() then
					report("paused: in combat")
					task.wait(1)
				else
					INV.surviveCheck(keep, report)
					local need, total, loaded = remaining(site)
					if not loaded then
						report("walking back to the site")
						walkTo(site:GetPivot().Position, 15, keep, nil, 240)
					elseif total == 0 then
						report("finishing...")
						task.wait(2)
						if site.Parent then
							local _, t2 = remaining(site)
							if t2 == 0 then return stop(("built %s!"):format(B.structure)) end
						end
					else
						if total < lastTotal then fails, lastTotal, rounds = 0, total, 0 end
						rounds = rounds + 1
						if rounds > 40 then return stop("stopped: no delivery in 40 rounds (" .. tostring(lastWhy) .. ")") end
						local held = carried()
						local ok, why
						if held and need[held.Name] then
							ok, why = deliverHeld(site, keep)
						else
							if held then dropCarried() end
							local pick
							local sitePos = site:GetPivot().Position
							for n in pairs(need) do if candidateItem(n, sitePos) then pick = n break end end
							pick = pick or next(need)
							report(("%s needs %s \194\183 getting %s"):format(B.structure, needText(need), pick))
							ok, why = gatherOne(pick, site, keep)
						end
						if not ok then
							fails = fails + 1
							lastWhy = why
							report(("retry %d/8: %s"):format(fails, tostring(why)))
							if fails >= 8 then return stop("stopped: " .. tostring(lastWhy)) end
							task.wait(1)
						end
					end
				end
			end
			if carried() then dropCarried() end
		end
		function B.start()
			if B.running then return end
			B.running = true
			S.buildOn = true
			task.spawn(function()
				local ok, err = pcall(runBuild)
				if not ok then log("build error", err) stop("error: " .. tostring(err)) end
				B.running = false
				S.buildOn = false
			end)
		end
		function B.autoSpot(name, around)
			local d = Deployables and Deployables:FindFirstChild(name)
			if not d then return false end
			for r = 14, 56, 6 do
				for i = 0, 7 do
					local a = i * math.pi / 4 + r * 0.1
					local g = groundAt(around + Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * r, 0, math.sin(a) * r))
					if g and not spotBlocked(d, placementCF(d, g)) then
						B.spot = g
						return true
					end
				end
			end
			return false
		end
		function B.buildAndWait(name, keepGoing, outerReport)
			if B.running then return false, "the builder is already busy" end
			if not (Deployables and Deployables:FindFirstChild(name)) then return false, "unknown structure " .. tostring(name) end
			local _, hallPos = myTownHall()
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			local around = hallPos or (hrp and hrp.Position)
			if not around then return false, "no character" end
			local prevStructure, prevSpot = B.structure, B.spot
			B.structure, B.site = name, nil
			local resumed = false
			for _, s in ipairs(mySites()) do
				local _, _, att = siteParts(s)
				local fo = att and att:FindFirstChild("FinishedObject")
				if fo and fo.Value and fo.Value.Name == name then
					B.spot, B.site, resumed = s:GetPivot().Position, s, true
					break
				end
			end
			if not resumed and not B.autoSpot(name, around) then
				B.structure, B.spot = prevStructure, prevSpot
				return false, "no clear spot to build a " .. name
			end
			B.running, S.buildOn = true, true
			task.spawn(function()
				while B.running do
					if keepGoing and not keepGoing() then B.running = false break end
					if outerReport then outerReport("building a " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(B.status)) end
					task.wait(1)
				end
			end)
			local ok, err = pcall(runBuild)
			B.running, S.buildOn = false, false
			clearGhost()
			if not ok then return false, "build error: " .. tostring(err) end
			return tostring(B.status):find("^built") ~= nil, B.status
		end
		function B.stopBuild()
			B.running = false
			S.buildOn = false
			B.status = "stopped"
			cancelWalk()
			if B.onStopped then pcall(B.onStopped) end
		end
		function B.statusText()
			local site = B.site
			local lines = { B.status }
			if site and site.Parent then
				table.insert(lines, "site needs: " .. needText((remaining(site))))
			end
			if not B.siteCountT or os.clock() - B.siteCountT > 4 then B.siteCountT, B.siteCount = os.clock(), #mySites() end
			local n = B.siteCount
			if n > 0 then table.insert(lines, ("your unfinished sites: %d"):format(n)) end
			return table.concat(lines, "\n")
		end
		B.destroy = clearGhost
		return B
	end)()

	local LootGrab = (function()
		local G = { status = "off", busy = false }
		local skip = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

		local function slotOf(name)
			local n = name:lower()
			if n:find("shield", 1, true) then return "shield" end
			if n:find("helmet", 1, true) then return "helmet" end
			if n:find("chest", 1, true) or n:find("mail", 1, true) or n:find("plate", 1, true) or n:find("armor", 1, true) then return "chest" end
			for _, k in ipairs({ "sword", "spear", "club", "knife", "dagger", "mace", "crossbow", "bow" }) do
				if n:find(k, 1, true) then return "weapon" end
			end
		end
		local function num(inst, key)
			local v = inst:FindFirstChild(key)
			return v and v:IsA("ValueBase") and tonumber(v.Value) or nil
		end
		local GEAR = {}
		for _, fname in ipairs({ "Craftables", "LootDrops", "EquipmentGear" }) do
			local f = RS:FindFirstChild(fname)
			if f then
				for _, x in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
					local slot = slotOf(x.Name)
					if slot then
						local score
						if slot == "weapon" then score = num(x, "Damage")
						elseif slot == "shield" then
							local sp = num(x, "ShieldProtection")
							score = sp and (sp * 100 + math.min(num(x, "Durability") or 0, 99) / 100) or nil
						else score = num(x, "Protection") end
						local key = x.Name:lower()
						if score and score > 0 and (not GEAR[key] or score > GEAR[key].score) then GEAR[key] = { slot = slot, score = score } end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		local MAT = { { "steel", 45 }, { "iron", 40 }, { "bronze", 30 }, { "stone", 16 }, { "bone", 12 }, { "wood", 8 } }
		local function gearOf(name)
			local key = name:lower()
			if GEAR[key] then return GEAR[key] end
			local slot = slotOf(name)
			if not slot then return nil end
			local score = 10
			for _, m in ipairs(MAT) do if key:find(m[1], 1, true) then score = m[2] break end end
			if key:find("great", 1, true) then score = score + 6 end
			if slot == "shield" then
				local tier = (key:find("steel", 1, true) and 4) or (key:find("iron", 1, true) and 3) or (key:find("bronze", 1, true) and 2) or 1
				score = tier * 100 - 1
			end
			return { slot = slot, score = score, guess = true }
		end
		G.gearOf = gearOf

		local function myBest()
			local best = {}
			local function consider(name)
				local g = gearOf(name)
				if g and g.score > (best[g.slot] or 0) then best[g.slot] = g.score end
			end
			for _, t in ipairs(allTools()) do consider(t.Name) end
			local char = lp.Character
			if char then for _, c in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do if not c:IsA("Tool") and GEAR[c.Name:lower()] then consider(c.Name) end end end
			return best
		end
		G.myBest = myBest

		local function candidates(hrp)
			local out = {}
			for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
				if (skip[c] or 0) < os.clock() then
					local base, h, p = replicaInfo(c)
					if base then
						local g = gearOf(base)
						if g then
							local d = (h.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
							if d <= S.lootRadius then table.insert(out, { m = c, base = base, h = h, p = p, g = g, d = d }) end
						end
					end
				end
			end
			table.sort(out, function(a, b)
				if a.g.score ~= b.g.score then return a.g.score > b.g.score end
				return a.d < b.d
			end)
			return out
		end

		local function grab(e)
			G.busy = true
			G.status = ("grabbing %s (%s, score %.0f)"):format(e.base, e.g.slot, e.g.score)
			local reach = 4.5
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			if hrp and (e.h.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude > reach then
				walkTo(e.h.Position, 1, function() return alive and S.autoLoot and e.m.Parent ~= nil end, function(r)
					return not e.m.Parent or (e.h.Position - r.Position).Magnitude <= reach
				end, 10)
			end
			if e.m.Parent and e.p.Parent then
				local gap = pickupReadyT - os.clock()
				if gap > 0 then task.wait(gap) end
				local before = countItem(e.base)
				holdPrompt(e.p, S.lootHold)
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while e.m.Parent and countItem(e.base) <= before and os.clock() - t0 < 1 do task.wait(0.05) end
				local got = countItem(e.base) > before
				pickupReadyT = os.clock() + (got and 1.5 or 0.6)
				if got then
					G.status = "got " .. e.base
					notify("Loot", "Grabbed " .. e.base, 3)
				else
					skip[e.m] = os.clock() + 3
					G.status = "missed " .. e.base .. (lastNote.text ~= "" and (" (" .. lastNote.text .. ")") or "")
				end
			end
			G.busy = false
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			while alive do
				if S.autoLoot then
					local char, _, hrp = charParts()
					if char and hrp then
						local best = myBest()
						for _, e in ipairs(candidates(hrp)) do
							if not S.lootUpgradesOnly or e.g.score > (best[e.g.slot] or 0) then
								local ok, err = pcall(grab, e)
								if not ok then G.busy = false log("loot error", err) end
								break
							end
						end
						if not G.busy and G.status:find("^grabbing") then G.status = "watching" end
					end
					task.wait(0.15)
				else
					G.status = "off"
					task.wait(0.5)
				end
			end
		end)
		return G
	end)()

	local origLight = {
		Brightness = Lighting.Brightness, ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime, FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
		GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows, Ambient = Lighting.Ambient, OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
	}
	local atmos = {}
	for _, d in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("Atmosphere") then atmos[d] = d.Density end end
	local function applyLighting()
		if S.fullbright then
			local amb = Color3.fromRGB(178, 178, 178)
			if Lighting.Brightness ~= 2 then Lighting.Brightness = 2 end
			if Lighting.ClockTime ~= 14 then Lighting.ClockTime = 14 end
			if Lighting.GlobalShadows then Lighting.GlobalShadows = false end
			if Lighting.Ambient ~= amb then Lighting.Ambient = amb end
			if Lighting.OutdoorAmbient ~= amb then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = amb end
		end
		if S.noFog then
			if Lighting.FogEnd < 1e6 then Lighting.FogEnd = 1e6 end
			for a in pairs(atmos) do if a.Parent and a.Density ~= 0 then a.Density = 0 end end
		end
	end
	local function restoreLighting(which)
		if which == "fullbright" then
			for _, k in ipairs({ "Brightness", "ClockTime", "GlobalShadows", "Ambient", "OutdoorAmbient" }) do Lighting[k] = origLight[k] end
		else
			Lighting.FogEnd = origLight.FogEnd
			for a, dens in pairs(atmos) do if a.Parent then a.Density = dens end end
		end
	end
	on(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
		if S.fovOn then Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = S.fov end
	end)
	local function setConfig(name, value)
		local cfg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerConfig")
		local v = cfg and cfg:FindFirstChild(name)
		if v then v.Value = value end
	end

	local hui = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
	local espGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	espGui.Name = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
	espGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	espGui.Parent = hui
	local pool = {}
	local function getTag(i)
		if pool[i] then return pool[i] end
		local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		bb.Size, bb.AlwaysOnTop, bb.LightInfluence = UDim2.fromOffset(170, 34), true, 0
		bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
		bb.Parent = espGui
		local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		t.Size, t.BackgroundTransparency = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), 1
		t.Font, t.TextSize, t.TextStrokeTransparency = Enum.Font.GothamBold, 12, 0.4
		t.Parent = bb
		pool[i] = { bb = bb, text = t }
		return pool[i]
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		while alive do
			local _, _, hrp = charParts()
			local list = {}
			if hrp and (S.espOn or S.espSaplings) then
				local function add(adornee, pos, label, color)
					local d = (pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude
					if d <= S.espRange then table.insert(list, { a = adornee, d = d, label = label, color = color }) end
				end
				for m, t in pairs(S.espOn and nodes or {}) do
					local p = nodePos[m]
					if S.esp[t] and p and (p - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= S.espRange and nodeAlive(m) then
						local hp = m:FindFirstChild("Health")
						add(m, p, ("%s\n%dm%s"):format(t, (p - hrp.Position).Magnitude, hp and (" \194\183 " .. math.floor(hp.Value) .. " hp") or ""), COLORS[t])
					end
				end
				if S.espSaplings then
					for m in pairs(saplings) do
						if m.Parent then
							local g = m:FindFirstChild("Growth")
							add(m, m:GetPivot().Position, ("Sapling\ngrowth %s"):format(g and math.floor(g.Value * 10) / 10 or "?"), COLORS.Sapling)
						end
					end
				end
				table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.d < b.d end)
			end
			for i = 1, math.max(#pool, math.min(#list, S.espMax)) do
				local e = list[i]
				if e and i <= S.espMax then
					local tag = getTag(i)
					tag.bb.Adornee, tag.bb.Enabled = e.a, true
					tag.text.Text, tag.text.TextColor3 = e.label, e.color or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
				elseif pool[i] then
					pool[i].bb.Enabled, pool[i].bb.Adornee = false, nil
				end
			end
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)

	on(lp.Idled, function()
		if S.antiAfk then
			VirtualUser:CaptureController()
			VirtualUser:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
		end
	end)
	local function serverHop()
		local ok, body = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, ("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"):format(game.PlaceId))
		if not ok then notify("Server hop", "Server list request failed"); return end
		local okJ, data = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, body)
		if not okJ or type(data) ~= "table" then notify("Server hop", "Bad server list"); return end
		local options = {}
		for _, s in ipairs(data.data or {}) do
			if s.id ~= game.JobId and s.playing < s.maxPlayers then table.insert(options, s.id) end
		end
		if #options == 0 then notify("Server hop", "No other open servers"); return end
		TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, options[math.random(#options)], lp)
	end
	local function rejoin()
		TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, lp)
	end
	local function walkToPlace(name)
		local _, _, hrp = charParts()
		if not hrp then return false, "no character" end
		local best, bd
		local all = Workspace:GetDescendants()
		local slice = os.clock()
		for i = 1, #all do
			local m = all[i]
			if m.Name == name and m:IsA("Model") then
				local d = flat(m:GetPivot().Position, hrp.Position)
				if not bd or d < bd then best, bd = m, d end
			end
			if i % 250 == 0 and os.clock() - slice > 0.004 then task.wait() slice = os.clock() end
		end
		if not best then return false, "no " .. name .. " found" end
		return walkToModel(best:GetPivot().Position, 8), "could not reach it"
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while alive do
			applyLighting()
			task.wait(1)
		end
	end)

	if getgenv().__crezCal and getgenv().__crezCal.job == game.JobId and (getgenv().__crezCal.extra or 0) > 0 then
		S.speedExtra, S.calibrated = math.min(getgenv().__crezCal.extra, 10), true
		log("speed: reusing this server's calibration +" .. S.speedExtra)
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(3)
		while alive and not S.calibrated do
			local _, hum = charParts()
			if S.autoCalibrate and S.speed and hum and not S.farm and not S.goalOn and not S.buildOn and not otherMovementOn()
				and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude < 0.05 and os.clock() - lastAutoMoveT > 3 then
				calibrateSpeed()
			end
			if not S.calibrated then task.wait(20) end
		end
	end)

	API.S, API.DEBUG = S, DEBUG
	API.LOOT_LIST, API.DEFAULT_PICK, API.CRAFT_LIST, API.JOB_LIST, API.FARMABLE, API.PLACES = LOOT_LIST, DEFAULT_PICK, CRAFT_LIST, JOB_LIST, FARMABLE, PLACES
	API.nodes, API.countItem, API.nearestNode = nodes, countItem, nearestNode
	API.Sea, API.nearestReachable = Sea, nearestReachable
	API.walkTo, API.walkToModel, API.cancelWalk, API.walkToPlace = walkTo, walkToModel, cancelWalk, walkToPlace
	API.harvestNode, API.plant, API.plantAtMarks, API.plantGrid = harvestNode, plant, plantAtMarks, plantGrid
	API.addMark, API.clearMarks, API.markCount = addMark, clearMarks, function() return #marks end
	API.mouseGround, API.frontGround = mouseGround, frontGround
	API.nextAction, API.planText, API.craft, API.ensureJob = nextAction, planText, craft, ensureJob
	API.calibrate, API.isCalibrating = calibrateSpeed, function() return calibrating end
	API.restoreLighting, API.setConfig, API.serverHop, API.rejoin = restoreLighting, setConfig, serverHop, rejoin
	API.notify = notify
	API.Builder, API.obtain, API.holdPrompt, API.LootGrab = Builder, obtain, holdPrompt, LootGrab
	API.myTownHall = myTownHall
	API.INV = INV
	API.haulNow = function()
		if S.farm or S.goalOn then notify("Logs", "Stop the farm/goal first", 3) return end
		local moved, why = haulLogs(function() return alive and not S.farm and not S.goalOn end, true)
		treesSinceHaul = 0
		S.status = ("hauled %d logs%s"):format(moved, why and (" \194\183 " .. why) or "")
		notify("Logs", S.status, 4)
	end
	API.hallStatus = function()
		local _, hallPos = myTownHall()
		local logs = 0
		for _, l in ipairs(pendingLogs) do if freeLog(l) then logs = logs + 1 end end
		if not hallPos then
			return ("No town hall for your team, farming around you\nlogs waiting: %d"):format(logs)
		end
		local _, _, hrp = charParts()
		return ("Town hall %d studs away \194\183 saplings growing near it: %d\ntrees since last haul: %d/%d \194\183 logs waiting: %d"):format(
			hrp and flat(hallPos, hrp.Position) or 0, saplingsNear(hallPos, 120), treesSinceHaul, S.haulEvery, logs)
	end
	API.alive = function() return alive end
	API.destroy = function()
		if not alive then return end
		alive = false
		S.farm, S.goalOn = false, false
		if INV.glitch then INV.glitch = false pcall(INV.pushAll) end
		Builder.running = false
		pcall(Builder.destroy)
		walkGen = walkGen + 1
		moveOverride = nil
		if S.fullbright then restoreLighting("fullbright") end
		if S.noFog then restoreLighting("fog") end
		if S.fovOn then Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = 70 end
		for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
		clearMarks()
		pcall(function() espGui:Destroy() end)
	end

	local count, perType = 0, {}
	for _, t in pairs(nodes) do count = count + 1; perType[t] = (perType[t] or 0) + 1 end
	log(("engine ready \194\183 %d nodes (Tree %d, Stone %d, Fiber %d)"):format(count, perType.Tree or 0, perType.Stone or 0, perType["Fiber Plant"] or 0))
	return API
end)()
getgenv().__ledzFarm = CZ

local ItemESP = (function()
    local M = { Enabled=false, Arrows=true, MaxDist=6000, MaxDrawn=40, Selected={}, Index={}, Pool={}, Nearest=nil, Acc=0, Maid=Maid.new() }
    local RI = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("ResourceInfo")
    local COL_DROP = Color3.fromRGB(110, 220, 255)
    local COL_SPAWN = Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 70)

    local names, PICKUP_NODE = {}, {}
    for _, f in ipairs({ "Craftables", "LootDrops" }) do
        local c = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(f)
        if c then for _, x in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do names[x.Name] = true end end
    end
    if RI then
        for _, x in ipairs(RI:GetChildren()) do
            if x:FindFirstChild("PickUp") then PICKUP_NODE[x.Name] = true names[x.Name] = true end
        end
    end
    M.Names = {}
    for n in pairs(names) do M.Names[#M.Names + 1] = n end
    table.sort(M.Names)
    local GEAR_KEYS = { "sword", "chest", "helmet", "shield", "spear", "bow", "axe", "pickaxe", "knife", "mail", "dagger", "mace" }
    M.DefaultSel = {}
    for _, n in ipairs(M.Names) do
        local l = n:lower()
        for _, k in ipairs(GEAR_KEYS) do
            if l:find(k, 1, true) then M.DefaultSel[#M.DefaultSel + 1] = n M.Selected[n] = true break end
        end
    end

    local function classify(c)
        local base = c.Name:match("^(.-)_Replica$")
        if base then return base, "drop" end
        if c:IsA("Tool") then return c.Name, "drop" end
        if c:IsA("Model") then
            local info = c:FindFirstChild("Info")
            if info and info:IsA("ObjectValue") and info.Value and PICKUP_NODE[info.Value.Name] then return info.Value.Name, "spawn" end
        end
    end
    local function indexChild(c)
        local name, kind = classify(c)
        if name then M.Index[c] = { name = name, kind = kind } end
    end
    local function itemPos(c)
        local h = c:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if h and h:IsA("BasePart") then return h.Position end
        local ok, cf = pcall(c.GetPivot, c)
        return ok and cf.Position or nil
    end
    M.ItemPos = itemPos

    local function compass(from, to)
        local dx, dz = to.X - from.X, to.Z - from.Z
        local deg = (math.deg(math.atan2(dx, -dz)) + 360) % 360
        return ({ "N", "NE", "E", "SE", "S", "SW", "W", "NW" })[math.floor((deg + 22.5) / 45) % 8 + 1]
    end

    local function newEntry()
        local t = Drawing.new("Text") t.Size = 13 t.Center = true t.Outline = true t.Font = 2 t.Visible = false
        local a = Drawing.new("Triangle") a.Filled = true a.Thickness = 1 a.Transparency = 1 a.Visible = false
        return { text = t, arrow = a }
    end
    local function hideAll() for _, e in ipairs(M.Pool) do e.text.Visible = false e.arrow.Visible = false end end
    local function freePool() for _, e in ipairs(M.Pool) do pcall(function() e.text:Remove() e.arrow:Remove() end) end M.Pool = {} end

    function M.List(maxN)
        local root = Char.HRP()
        if not root then return {} end
        local me = root.Position
        local out = {}
        for c, info in pairs(M.Index) do
            if c.Parent and M.Selected[info.name] then
                local p = itemPos(c)
                if p then
                    local d = (p - me).Magnitude
                    if d <= M.MaxDist then out[#out + 1] = { inst = c, name = info.name, kind = info.kind, pos = p, dist = d } end
                end
            elseif not c.Parent then
                M.Index[c] = nil
            end
        end
        table.sort(out, function(a, b) return a.dist < b.dist end)
        if maxN then for i = #out, maxN + 1, -1 do out[i] = nil end end
        return out
    end

    local function render()
        local root = Char.HRP()
        if not root then hideAll() return end
        local me = root.Position
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local vs = cam.ViewportSize
        local center = vs / 2
        local ringR = math.min(vs.X, vs.Y) * 0.36
        local list = M.List(M.MaxDrawn)
        M.Nearest = list[1] and { name = list[1].name, dist = list[1].dist, dir = compass(me, list[1].pos), kind = list[1].kind } or nil
        for i = 1, math.max(#list, #M.Pool) do
            local it = list[i]
            local e = M.Pool[i]
            if it and not e then e = newEntry() M.Pool[i] = e end
            if e then
                if not it then
                    e.text.Visible = false e.arrow.Visible = false
                else
                    local col = it.kind == "spawn" and COL_SPAWN or COL_DROP
                    local label = ("%s%s [%dm %s]"):format(it.name, it.kind == "spawn" and " (spawn)" or "", math.floor(it.dist), compass(me, it.pos))
                    local sp, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(it.pos)
                    if onScreen and sp.Z > 0 then
                        e.arrow.Visible = false
                        e.text.Text = label e.text.Color = col e.text.Position = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) e.text.Visible = true
                    elseif M.Arrows then
                        local rel = cam.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(it.pos)
                        local ang = math.atan2(rel.X, -rel.Z)
                        local d = Vector2.new(math.sin(ang), -math.cos(ang))
                        local perp = Vector2.new(-d.Y, d.X)
                        local at = center + d * ringR
                        e.arrow.PointA = at + d * 14
                        e.arrow.PointB = at - d * 6 + perp * 9
                        e.arrow.PointC = at - d * 6 - perp * 9
                        e.arrow.Color = col e.arrow.Visible = true
                        e.text.Text = label e.text.Color = col e.text.Position = at - d * 22 e.text.Visible = true
                    else
                        e.text.Visible = false e.arrow.Visible = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function M.Set(on)
        M.Enabled = on
        M.Maid:Clean()
        if not on then freePool() M.Index = {} M.Nearest = nil return end
        for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do indexChild(c) end
        M.Maid:Give(Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
            indexChild(c)
            if not M.Index[c] then task.delay(1, function() if M.Enabled and c.Parent == Workspace then indexChild(c) end end) end
        end))
        M.Maid:Give(Workspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(c) M.Index[c] = nil end))
        M.Maid:Give(function() freePool() end)
        M.Acc = 0
        M.Maid:Give(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            M.Acc = M.Acc + dt
            if M.Acc < 0.05 then return end
            M.Acc = 0
            render()
        end))
    end

    function M.GoNearest()
        if not (CZ and CZ.ok) then return end
        local it = M.List(1)[1]
        if not it then CZ.notify("Item ESP", "No selected item in range", 3) return end
        CZ.notify("Item ESP", ("Walking to %s (%dm %s)"):format(it.name, math.floor(it.dist), M.Nearest and M.Nearest.dir or ""), 4)
        local inst = it.inst
        local ok = CZ.walkTo(it.pos, 1, nil, function(r)
            local p = inst.Parent and itemPos(inst)
            return not p or (p - r.Position).Magnitude <= 5
        end, 900)
        local prompt = inst.Parent and inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
        if ok and prompt then SafePrompt.hold(prompt) end
        CZ.notify("Item ESP", ok and ("Reached " .. it.name) or "Could not reach it", 3)
    end
    return M
end)()

local Boats = (function()
    local M = { Pilot=false, Mode="Marked Point", Cruise=false, MaxWalk=800, BoardWW=true, WaterWalkUser=false, Avoid=true, Arrive=18, HopOff=false, Mark=nil, Follow=nil,
        EspOn=false, EspFree=false, EspRange=3000, EspMax=30, EspMerch=true, AnimalEsp=false,
        AlertOn=false, AlertRange=300, HpAlert=false, HpPct=40, WaterWalk=false,
        Status="idle", RideStatus="idle", boardGen=0, Dead=false, empty=setmetatable({}, { __mode="k" }),
        Maid=Maid.new(), PilotMaid=Maid.new(), EspMaid=Maid.new(), WWMaid=Maid.new(), CruiseMaid=Maid.new() }
    local NICE = { Raft="Raft", Boat="Sailboat", Warboat="Warboat" }
    M.TYPES = { "Any", "Raft", "Sailboat", "Warboat" }
    M.MODES = { "Marked Point", "Nearest Merchant", "My Town Hall", "Follow Player", "Ram Nearest Enemy Boat" }
    local COL = { free=Color3.fromRGB(120,230,140), mine=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), team=Color3.fromRGB(110,170,255),
        enemy=Color3.fromRGB(255,90,90), barbarian=Color3.fromRGB(255,150,40), merchant=Color3.fromRGB(255,215,70),
        taken=Color3.fromRGB(200,200,200), animal=Color3.fromRGB(235,190,140), saddled=Color3.fromRGB(255,240,120) }
    local function notify(t, d) pcall(function() window:Notify({ Title=t, Description=d, Duration=3 }) end) end
    local function nice(b) return NICE[b.Name] or b.Name end
    local function isBoat(m) return m ~= nil and m.Parent ~= nil and m:IsA("Model") and CollectionService:HasTag(m, "Boat") end
    local function pivotPos(m) local ok, cf = pcall(m.GetPivot, m) return ok and cf.Position or nil end
    local function hasCZ() return CZ and CZ.ok end
    function M.all()
        local out = {}
        for _, b in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("Boat")) do if b:IsA("Model") and b.Parent then out[#out+1] = b end end
        return out
    end
    local function seatsOf(b)
        local drv, rows = nil, {}
        for _, c in ipairs(b:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("VehicleSeat") then drv = drv or c elseif c:IsA("Seat") then rows[#rows+1] = c end
        end
        return drv, rows
    end
    function M.merchants()
        local out = {}
        local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
        local f = map and map:FindFirstChild("Merchants")
        if f then
            for _, m in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
                local npc = m:FindFirstChild("NPC") or m
                local p = pivotPos(npc)
                if p then out[#out+1] = { model=m, pos=p } end
            end
        end
        return out
    end
    local function nearMerchant(pos, r)
        for _, m in ipairs(M.merchants()) do if (m.pos - pos).Magnitude <= (r or 45) then return true end end
        return false
    end
    function M.info(b)
        local drv, rows = seatsOf(b)
        local total, used, plrs, npc = 0, 0, {}, 0
        local function chk(s)
            if not s then return end
            total = total + 1
            local o = s.Occupant
            if o then
                used = used + 1
                local p = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(o.Parent)
                if p then plrs[#plrs+1] = p else npc = npc + 1 end
            end
        end
        chk(drv)
        for _, s in ipairs(rows) do chk(s) end
        local hp, mhp = b:FindFirstChild("Health"), b:FindFirstChild("MaxHealth")
        local streamed = drv == nil and #rows == 0
        local pos = pivotPos(b)
        local kind = "free"
        for _, p in ipairs(plrs) do if p == LocalPlayer then kind = "mine" end end
        if kind ~= "mine" then
            if #plrs > 0 then
                kind = "team"
                for _, p in ipairs(plrs) do if LocalPlayer.Team == nil or p.Team ~= LocalPlayer.Team then kind = "enemy" end end
            elseif npc > 0 then
                kind = (pos and nearMerchant(pos)) and "merchant" or "barbarian"
            elseif not NICE[b.Name] then
                kind = "merchant"
            elseif used > 0 then
                kind = "taken"
            end
        end
        return { boat=b, drv=drv, rows=rows, seats=total, used=used, players=plrs, npc=npc, kind=kind, pos=pos,
            hp=hp and hp.Value, mhp=mhp and mhp.Value, streamed=streamed, driverFree=(drv ~= nil and drv.Occupant == nil) }
    end
    function M.current()
        local h = Char.Hum()
        local s = h and h.SeatPart
        if s and isBoat(s.Parent) then return s.Parent, s end
    end
    local function promptOf(seat)
        local a = seat:FindFirstChild("Attachment")
        return (a and a:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt")) or seat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
    end
    local function firePrompt(pr)
        if not pr then return false end
        return SafePrompt.hold(pr)
    end
    function M.pickBoat(role, typ, skipB)
        local hrp = Char.HRP()
        if not hrp then return nil end
        local best, bd
        for _, b in ipairs(M.all()) do
            if (typ == "Any" or nice(b) == typ) and not (skipB and skipB[b]) then
                local i = M.info(b)
                local d = i.pos and (i.pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
                local room = (i.streamed and d > 220 and not M.empty[b]) or (role == "Driver" and i.driverFree) or (role ~= "Driver" and i.used < i.seats)
                if room and not i.streamed and role == "Driver" and M.afloat and not M.afloat(b) then room = false end
                if room and i.pos and (i.kind == "free" or i.kind == "team" or i.kind == "taken") then
                    if not bd or d < bd then best, bd = b, d end
                end
            end
        end
        return best, bd
    end
    function M.Leave()
        local h = Char.Hum()
        if not (h and h.SeatPart) then return false end
        h.Sit = false
        pcall(function() h:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
        return true
    end
    function M.CancelBoard() M.boardGen = M.boardGen + 1 if hasCZ() then pcall(CZ.cancelWalk) end end
    function M.Board(role, typ)
        local temp = M.BoardWW and not M.WaterWalk
        if temp then M.SetWaterWalk(true) end
        local r1, r2 = M.BoardInner(role, typ)
        if temp and not M.WaterWalkUser then M.SetWaterWalk(false) end
        return r1, r2
    end
    function M.BoardInner(role, typ)
        M.boardGen = M.boardGen + 1
        local gen = M.boardGen
        local function live() return gen == M.boardGen and not M.Dead end
        local tried = {}
        for _ = 1, 4 do
            local b, d = M.pickBoat(role, typ, tried)
            if not b then M.Status = "no free " .. (typ == "Any" and "boat" or typ) .. " found" return false, M.Status end
            if d > M.MaxWalk then M.Status = ("nearest free %s is %d studs away (max walk %d)"):format(nice(b), d, M.MaxWalk) return false, M.Status end
            tried[b] = true
            M.Status = ("going to %s (%d studs)"):format(nice(b), d)
            pcall(function() LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(b:GetPivot().Position, 5) end)
            local function seatTarget()
                local drv, rows = seatsOf(b)
                if role ~= "Rower" and drv and not drv.Occupant then return drv end
                if role ~= "Driver" then for _, s in ipairs(rows) do if not s.Occupant then return s end end end
            end
            local h = Char.Hum()
            if h and h.SeatPart then M.Leave() task.wait(0.4) end
            local t0 = os.clock()
            local done = false
            while live() and os.clock() - t0 < math.max(75, d / 6) and b.Parent do
                local hrp, hum = Char.HRP(), Char.Hum()
                if not (hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0) then M.Status = "no character" return false, M.Status end
                local s = seatTarget()
                local d0, r0 = seatsOf(b)
                local streamedNow = d0 == nil and #r0 == 0
                if not s and not streamedNow then break end
                local goal = s and s.Position or pivotPos(b)
                if not goal then break end
                local dist = (goal - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                if streamedNow and dist < 200 then
                    pcall(function() LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(goal, 3) end)
                    task.wait(1)
                    d0, r0 = seatsOf(b)
                    if d0 == nil and #r0 == 0 then M.empty[b] = true break end
                end
                local spr = s and promptOf(s)
                if s and spr and SafePrompt.inRange(spr, 1) then
                    firePrompt(spr)
                    local t1 = os.clock()
                    while os.clock() - t1 < 2 and hum.SeatPart ~= s do task.wait(0.1) end
                    if hum.SeatPart == s then
                        M.Status = ("seated: %s %s"):format(nice(b), s:IsA("VehicleSeat") and "(driver)" or "(rower)")
                        done = true
                        break
                    end
                elseif dist > 40 and hasCZ() and CZ.walkTo then
                    local target = s
                    CZ.walkTo(goal, 5, live, function(r)
                        local tp = target and target.Parent and target.Position or goal
                        return (tp - r.Position).Magnitude <= 4
                    end, 45)
                else
                    hum:MoveTo(goal)
                    task.wait(0.35)
                end
            end
            if done then return true end
            if not live() then M.Status = "boarding cancelled" return false, M.Status end
        end
        M.Status = "could not get a seat"
        return false, M.Status
    end

    local RP = RaycastParams.new()
    RP.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    RP.IgnoreWater = true
    local skip, skipSet = {}, {}
    local function groundY(p, ex)
        local list = { ex, LocalPlayer.Character }
        for i = #skip, 1, -1 do
            if skip[i].Parent then list[#list+1] = skip[i] else skipSet[skip[i]] = nil table.remove(skip, i) end
        end
        for _ = 1, 6 do
            RP.FilterDescendantsInstances = list
            local r = Workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(p.X, p.Y + 120, p.Z), Vector3.new(0, -260, 0), RP)
            if not r then return nil end
            local inst = r.Instance
            if inst:IsA("Terrain") or inst.CanCollide then return r.Position.Y end
            list[#list+1] = inst
            if not skipSet[inst] and #skip < 150 then skipSet[inst] = true skip[#skip+1] = inst end
        end
    end
    local function rotY(v, deg)
        local r = math.rad(deg)
        local c, s = math.cos(r), math.sin(r)
        return Vector3.new(v.X * c - v.Z * s, 0, v.X * s + v.Z * c)
    end
    local OFFS = { 0, 20, -20, 40, -40, 65, -65, 90, -90, 125, -125, 160, -160 }
    local SAMPLES = { 8, 16, 26, 38, 52 }
    local function hullBottom(b)
        local h = b:FindFirstChild("Hull") or b:FindFirstChild("Base")
        if h and h:IsA("BasePart") then return h.Position.Y - h.Size.Y / 2, h end
        local cf, size = b:GetBoundingBox()
        return cf.Position.Y - size.Y / 2, nil
    end
    local function laneClear(b, origin, dir, bottom, half, reach, len)
        local side = Vector3.new(-dir.Z, 0, dir.X)
        for _, d in ipairs(SAMPLES) do
            if d > reach then break end
            local c = origin + dir * (d + len)
            for _, o in ipairs({ 0, half, -half }) do
                local y = groundY(c + side * o, b)
                if y and y > bottom - 0.4 then return false end
            end
        end
        return true
    end
    function M.afloat(b)
        local bottom, hull = hullBottom(b)
        if not hull then return true end
        local cf = hull.CFrame
        local len = math.max(hull.Size.X, hull.Size.Z) / 2
        local ax = hull.Size.X >= hull.Size.Z and cf.RightVector or cf.LookVector
        for _, o in ipairs({ 0, len * 0.8, -len * 0.8 }) do
            local y = groundY(hull.Position + ax * o, b)
            if y and y > bottom - 0.4 then return false end
        end
        return true
    end
    function M.chooseDir(b, origin, want, dist)
        if not M.Avoid then return want, 0 end
        local bottom, hull = hullBottom(b)
        local half = hull and math.min(hull.Size.Z, hull.Size.X) / 2 or 4
        local len = hull and math.max(hull.Size.Z, hull.Size.X) / 2 or 6
        local reach = math.max(10, math.min(52, dist - len))
        for _, o in ipairs(OFFS) do
            local dir = rotY(want, o)
            if laneClear(b, origin, dir, bottom, half, reach, len) then return dir, o end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function steer(drv, dir, thr)
        local f = drv.CFrame.LookVector
        f = Vector3.new(f.X, 0, f.Z)
        if f.Magnitude < 0.01 then return 0 end
        f = f.Unit
        local cross = f.X * dir.Z - f.Z * dir.X
        local ang = math.deg(math.atan2(cross, f:Dot(dir)))
        drv.SteerFloat = math.clamp(ang / 25, -1, 1)
        drv.Steer = (ang > 5 and 1) or (ang < -5 and -1) or 0
        if math.abs(ang) > 75 and thr > 0 then thr = math.min(thr, 0.35) end
        drv.ThrottleFloat = thr
        drv.Throttle = (thr > 0.05 and 1) or (thr < -0.05 and -1) or 0
        return ang
    end
    local function neutral(drv)
        if drv and drv.Parent then pcall(function() drv.Throttle = 0 drv.ThrottleFloat = 0 drv.Steer = 0 drv.SteerFloat = 0 end) end
    end
    function M.enemyBoats(maxD)
        local hrp = Char.HRP()
        local out = {}
        if not hrp then return out end
        for _, b in ipairs(M.all()) do
            local i = M.info(b)
            if i.kind == "enemy" and i.pos then
                local d = (i.pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                if not maxD or d <= maxD then i.dist = d out[#out+1] = i end
            end
        end
        table.sort(out, function(a, c) return a.dist < c.dist end)
        return out
    end
    function M.targetPos(b)
        local hrp = Char.HRP()
        if not hrp then return nil end
        local mode = M.Mode
        if mode == "Marked Point" then
            if not M.Mark then return nil, "mark a point first" end
            return M.Mark, nil, true
        elseif mode == "Nearest Merchant" then
            local best, bd
            for _, m in ipairs(M.merchants()) do
                local d = (m.pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                if not bd or d < bd then best, bd = m.pos, d end
            end
            if not best then return nil, "no merchant on the map right now" end
            return best, nil, true
        elseif mode == "My Town Hall" then
            if not hasCZ() then return nil, "farm engine off" end
            local _, hp = CZ.myTownHall()
            if not hp then return nil, "your team has no town hall" end
            return hp, nil, true
        elseif mode == "Follow Player" then
            local p = M.Follow and Players:FindFirstChild(M.Follow)
            local r = p and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not r then return nil, "pick a player (they need to be spawned)" end
            return r.Position, r.AssemblyLinearVelocity, false
        else
            local e = M.enemyBoats()[1]
            if not e then return nil, "no enemy boat with players in it" end
            local h = e.boat:FindFirstChild("Hull") or e.boat:FindFirstChild("Base")
            local v = h and h:IsA("BasePart") and h.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
            return e.pos, v, false, e
        end
    end
    function M.SetPilot(on)
        M.Pilot = on
        M.PilotMaid:Clean()
        if not on then M.Status = "autopilot off" return end
        local plan = { dir=nil, t=0, stuckT=nil, revUntil=0, lastPos=nil, lastMoveT=os.clock() }
        local lastDrv
        local function step()
            if not M.Pilot then return end
            local b, s = M.current()
            if not (b and s and s:IsA("VehicleSeat")) then
                if lastDrv then neutral(lastDrv) lastDrv = nil end
                plan.lastMoveT = os.clock()
                M.Status = "autopilot: sit in a boat's driver seat"
                return
            end
            lastDrv = s
            local hull = b:FindFirstChild("Hull") or b:FindFirstChild("Base") or s
            local here = hull.Position
            local tp, tv, stops, enemy = M.targetPos(b)
            if not tp then neutral(s) plan.lastMoveT = os.clock() M.Status = "autopilot: " .. tostring(tv) return end
            local flatTo = Vector3.new(tp.X - here.X, 0, tp.Z - here.Z)
            local dist = flatTo.Magnitude
            local speed = Vector3.new(hull.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, hull.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z).Magnitude
            if tv and dist > 5 then
                local lead = math.clamp(dist / math.max(speed, 8), 0, 4)
                local aim = tp + Vector3.new(tv.X, 0, tv.Z) * lead
                flatTo = Vector3.new(aim.X - here.X, 0, aim.Z - here.Z)
            end
            local arrive = stops and M.Arrive or (enemy and 0 or math.max(M.Arrive, 30))
            if dist <= arrive then
                neutral(s)
                plan.lastMoveT = os.clock()
                if stops then
                    M.Status = ("arrived (%d studs away)"):format(dist)
                    M.Pilot = false
                    if M.OnPilotOff then task.defer(M.OnPilotOff) end
                    if M.HopOff then task.delay(0.6, M.Leave) end
                else
                    M.Status = ("holding near target (%d studs)"):format(dist)
                end
                return
            end
            if flatTo.Magnitude < 0.1 then return end
            local want = flatTo.Unit
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - plan.t > 0.2 or not plan.dir then
                plan.t = now
                local dir, off = M.chooseDir(b, here, want, dist)
                plan.dir, plan.off = dir, off
                if plan.lastPos and (here - plan.lastPos).Magnitude > 0.6 then
                    plan.lastMoveT = now
                    if now > plan.revUntil + 2 then plan.revCount = 0 end
                end
                plan.lastPos = here
            end
            if now < plan.revUntil then
                s.Throttle = -1 s.ThrottleFloat = -1
                s.Steer = plan.revSteer or 1 s.SteerFloat = plan.revSteer or 1
                M.Status = "autopilot: backing off an obstacle"
                return
            end
            if not plan.dir then
                if dist < 90 and stops then
                    neutral(s)
                    M.Status = "arrived at the shore"
                    M.Pilot = false
                    if M.OnPilotOff then task.defer(M.OnPilotOff) end
                    if M.HopOff then task.delay(0.6, M.Leave) end
                    return
                end
                plan.revUntil = now + 1.6
                plan.revSteer = (math.random() < 0.5) and 1 or -1
                return
            end
            if now - plan.lastMoveT > 3.5 then
                plan.lastMoveT = now
                plan.revCount = (plan.revCount or 0) + 1
                if plan.revCount >= 4 then
                    neutral(s)
                    M.Status = "boat is stuck (grounded or wedged), autopilot stopped"
                    M.Pilot = false
                    if M.OnPilotOff then task.defer(M.OnPilotOff) end
                    return
                end
                plan.revUntil = now + 1.6
                plan.revSteer = (plan.off or 0) >= 0 and -1 or 1
                return
            end
            local thr = 1
            if stops and dist < arrive + speed * 0.9 then thr = 0 end
            steer(s, plan.dir, thr)
            M.Status = ("autopilot: %s \194\183 %d studs \194\183 %.0f st/s%s"):format(enemy and ("ramming " .. nice(enemy.boat)) or M.Mode,
                dist, speed, (plan.off and plan.off ~= 0) and (" \194\183 avoiding (" .. plan.off .. "\194\176)") or "")
        end
        RunService:BindToRenderStep("LZBoatPilot", Enum.RenderPriority.Input.Value + 5, step)
        M.PilotMaid:Give(function() pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("LZBoatPilot") end) end)
        M.PilotMaid:Give(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(step))
        M.PilotMaid:Give(function() neutral(lastDrv) end)
    end
    function M.SetCruise(on)
        M.Cruise = on
        M.CruiseMaid:Clean()
        if not on then return end
        local function step()
            if M.Pilot then return end
            local _, s = M.current()
            if not (s and s:IsA("VehicleSeat")) then return end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Down) then return end
            if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
            s.Throttle = 1 s.ThrottleFloat = 1
        end
        RunService:BindToRenderStep("LZBoatCruise", Enum.RenderPriority.Input.Value + 6, step)
        M.CruiseMaid:Give(function() pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("LZBoatCruise") end) end)
        M.CruiseMaid:Give(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(step))
    end
    function M.MarkMouse()
        local p = Mouse.Hit and Mouse.Hit.Position
        if not p then return false end
        M.Mark = p
        notify("Boats", ("Autopilot point marked (%d studs away)"):format(Char.HRP() and (p - Char.HRP().Position).Magnitude or 0))
        return true
    end
    function M.MarkAhead(dist)
        local hrp = Char.HRP()
        if not hrp then return false end
        local b, s = M.current()
        local f = (s and s.CFrame.LookVector) or Camera.CFrame.LookVector
        f = Vector3.new(f.X, 0, f.Z)
        if f.Magnitude < 0.01 then return false end
        M.Mark = hrp.Position + f.Unit * (dist or 300)
        notify("Boats", ("Autopilot point marked %d studs ahead"):format(dist or 300))
        return true
    end

    local function animals()
        local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
        local res = map and map:FindFirstChild("Resources")
        local f = res and res:FindFirstChild("Animals")
        return f and f:GetChildren() or {}
    end
    local function animalSeat(a)
        local s = a:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Seat", true) or a:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat", true)
        return s
    end
    function M.findTool(name)
        for _, c in ipairs({ LocalPlayer.Character, LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack") }) do
            if c then for _, t in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do if t:IsA("Tool") and t.Name == name then return t end end end
        end
    end
    local function nearestAnimal(pred)
        local hrp = Char.HRP()
        if not hrp then return nil end
        local best, bd
        for _, a in ipairs(animals()) do
            local r = a:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local h = a:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if r and h and h.Health > 0 and pred(a) then
                local d = (r.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                if not bd or d < bd then best, bd = a, d end
            end
        end
        return best, bd
    end
    local function walkToAnimal(a, live)
        for _ = 1, 6 do
            local r, hrp = a:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), Char.HRP()
            if not (r and hrp and live()) then return false end
            if (r.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= 7 then return true end
            if hasCZ() and CZ.walkTo then
                CZ.walkTo(r.Position, 4, live, function(me) return r.Parent and (r.Position - me.Position).Magnitude <= 6.5 end, 25)
            else
                local hum = Char.Hum()
                if hum then hum:MoveTo(r.Position) end
                task.wait(1)
            end
        end
        local r, hrp = a:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), Char.HRP()
        return r and hrp and (r.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= 7.5
    end
    function M.SaddleNearest()
        M.boardGen = M.boardGen + 1
        local gen = M.boardGen
        local function live() return gen == M.boardGen and not M.Dead end
        local tool = M.findTool("Saddle")
        if not tool then M.RideStatus = "you need a Saddle in your inventory" return false, M.RideStatus end
        local a, d = nearestAnimal(function(x) local c = x:FindFirstChild("Child") return not animalSeat(x) and not (c and c.Value) end)
        if not a then M.RideStatus = "no grown animal without a saddle nearby" return false, M.RideStatus end
        M.RideStatus = ("walking to %s (%d studs)"):format(a.Name, d)
        if not walkToAnimal(a, live) then M.RideStatus = "could not reach the " .. a.Name return false, M.RideStatus end
        local hum = Char.Hum()
        if hum and tool.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end) task.wait(0.35) end
        local r = a:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local att = r and r:FindFirstChild("SaddleAttachment")
        local pr = att and att:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt")
        if not pr then M.RideStatus = "this animal has no saddle prompt" return false, M.RideStatus end
        pr.Enabled = true
        firePrompt(pr)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - t0 < 3 and not animalSeat(a) and tool.Parent do task.wait(0.15) end
        local ok = animalSeat(a) ~= nil or tool.Parent == nil
        M.RideStatus = ok and ("saddled the " .. a.Name) or "saddle prompt fired, the game did not saddle it"
        return ok, M.RideStatus
    end
    local SKIP_ACT = { Milk=true, Feed=true, Saddle=true, Shear=true, Dismount=true, Carry=true, Butcher=true, Skin=true }
    function M.RideNearest()
        M.boardGen = M.boardGen + 1
        local gen = M.boardGen
        local function live() return gen == M.boardGen and not M.Dead end
        local a, d = nearestAnimal(function(x) local s = animalSeat(x) return s and not s.Occupant end)
        if not a then M.RideStatus = "no saddled animal with a free seat nearby" return false, M.RideStatus end
        M.RideStatus = ("walking to the saddled %s (%d studs)"):format(a.Name, d)
        if not walkToAnimal(a, live) then M.RideStatus = "could not reach the " .. a.Name return false, M.RideStatus end
        local seat = animalSeat(a)
        local fired = 0
        for _, pr in ipairs(a:GetDescendants()) do
            if pr:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and pr.Enabled and not SKIP_ACT[pr.ActionText] and not SKIP_ACT[pr.Name:gsub("Prompt$", "")] then
                firePrompt(pr) fired = fired + 1
            end
        end
        local hum = Char.Hum()
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - t0 < 2.5 and hum and hum.SeatPart ~= seat do task.wait(0.1) end
        local ok = hum and hum.SeatPart == seat
        M.RideStatus = ok and ("riding the " .. a.Name) or (fired == 0 and "no ride prompt found on that animal" or "ride prompt fired, not seated")
        return ok, M.RideStatus
    end
    function M.Dismount()
        local c = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hum = Char.Hum()
        local s = hum and hum.SeatPart
        if c and c:GetAttribute("RidingAnimal") and s then
            local a = s.Parent and s.Parent.Parent
            local r = a and a:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local att = r and r:FindFirstChild("Attachment")
            local pr = att and att:FindFirstChild("DismountPrompt")
            if pr then pr.Enabled = true firePrompt(pr) task.wait(0.6) end
            if hum.SeatPart then M.Leave() end
            M.RideStatus = "dismounted"
            return true
        end
        return M.Leave()
    end

    local pool, used = {}, 0
    local function label()
        used = used + 1
        local t = pool[used]
        if not t then
            t = Drawing.new("Text")
            t.Size = 14 t.Center = true t.Outline = true t.Font = 2
            pool[used] = t
        end
        return t
    end
    local function freePool() for _, t in ipairs(pool) do pcall(function() t:Remove() end) end table.clear(pool) used = 0 M.Shown = 0 end
    local function put(pos, text, col, rows)
        local sp, on = Workspace.CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(pos)
        if not on or sp.Z <= 0 then return false end
        local t = label()
        t.Text = text t.Color = col t.Position = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y - (rows or 0) * 15) t.Visible = true
        return true
    end
    local function onScreen(pos)
        local sp, on = Workspace.CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(pos)
        return on and sp.Z > 0
    end
    local function render()
        used = 0
        local hrp = Char.HRP()
        if hrp then
            local items = {}
            if M.EspOn then
                for _, b in ipairs(M.all()) do
                    local p = pivotPos(b)
                    if p then
                        local d = (p - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                        if d <= M.EspRange and onScreen(p + Vector3.new(0, 9, 0)) then items[#items+1] = { k="b", b=b, p=p, d=d } end
                    end
                end
                if M.EspMerch then
                    for _, m in ipairs(M.merchants()) do
                        local d = (m.pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                        if d <= math.max(M.EspRange, 6000) and onScreen(m.pos + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)) then items[#items+1] = { k="m", p=m.pos, d=d } end
                    end
                end
            end
            if M.AnimalEsp then
                for _, a in ipairs(animals()) do
                    local r = a:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if r then
                        local d = (r.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                        if d <= M.EspRange and onScreen(r.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)) then items[#items+1] = { k="a", a=a, p=r.Position, d=d } end
                    end
                end
            end
            table.sort(items, function(x, y) return x.d < y.d end)
            local shown = 0
            for _, it in ipairs(items) do
                if shown >= M.EspMax then break end
                if it.k == "b" then
                    local i = M.info(it.b)
                    if (i.streamed and (it.d < 220 or M.empty[it.b])) then
                    elseif not M.EspFree or i.kind == "free" then
                        shown = shown + 1
                        local who = i.kind
                        if i.kind == "enemy" or i.kind == "team" then
                            local p1 = i.players[1]
                            who = i.kind .. " " .. (p1 and p1.Team and p1.Team.Name or "?")
                        end
                        local seats = i.streamed and "?" or (i.used .. "/" .. i.seats)
                        put(it.p + Vector3.new(0, 9, 0), ("%s [%s] %dm"):format(nice(it.b), who, it.d), COL[i.kind] or COL.free, 1)
                        put(it.p + Vector3.new(0, 9, 0), ("HP %s/%s \194\183 seats %s"):format(i.hp and math.floor(i.hp) or "?", i.mhp and math.floor(i.mhp) or "?", seats), COL[i.kind] or COL.free, 0)
                    end
                elseif it.k == "m" then
                    shown = shown + 1
                    put(it.p + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0), ("Merchant %dm"):format(it.d), COL.merchant, 0)
                else
                    shown = shown + 1
                    local s = animalSeat(it.a)
                    local ch = it.a:FindFirstChild("Child")
                    local tag = s and (s.Occupant and "ridden" or "saddled") or ((ch and ch.Value) and "baby" or "no saddle")
                    put(it.p + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), ("%s [%s] %dm"):format(it.a.Name, tag, it.d), s and COL.saddled or COL.animal, 0)
                end
            end
        end
        for i = used + 1, #pool do if pool[i].Visible then pool[i].Visible = false end end
        M.Shown = used
    end
    local espAcc = 0
    function M.RefreshEsp()
        M.EspMaid:Clean()
        if not (M.EspOn or M.AnimalEsp) then freePool() return end
        M.EspMaid:Give(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            espAcc = espAcc + dt
            if espAcc < 0.05 then return end
            espAcc = 0
            local ok = pcall(render)
            if not ok then for _, t in ipairs(pool) do t.Visible = false end end
        end))
        M.EspMaid:Give(freePool)
    end
    function M.SetEsp(on) M.EspOn = on M.RefreshEsp() end
    function M.SetAnimalEsp(on) M.AnimalEsp = on M.RefreshEsp() end

    function M.SetWaterWalk(on)
        M.WaterWalk = on
        M.WWMaid:Clean()
        if not on then return end
        local plat = Instance.new("Part")
        plat.Name = "LZ_WaterWalk" plat.Anchored = true plat.CanCollide = true plat.CanTouch = false plat.CanQuery = false
        plat.Transparency = 1 plat.Size = Vector3.new(12, 1, 12) plat.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
        M.WWMaid:Give(plat)
        local wp = RaycastParams.new()
        wp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
        local waters, nextScan = {}, 0
        local function scan()
            waters = {}
            local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
            local terr = map and map:FindFirstChild("Terrain")
            if terr then for _, d in ipairs(terr:GetChildren()) do if d:IsA("BasePart") and (d.Name == "Saltwater" or d.Name == "Freshwater") then waters[#waters+1] = d end end end
            for _, d in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("Water")) do if d:IsA("BasePart") then waters[#waters+1] = d end end
            wp.FilterDescendantsInstances = waters
        end
        local top
        M.WWMaid:Give(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local now = os.clock()
            if now > nextScan then nextScan = now + 8 scan() end
            local hrp, hum = Char.HRP(), Char.Hum()
            if not (hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0) or hum.SeatPart or #waters == 0 then plat.Parent = nil top = nil return end
            local r = Workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 30, 0), Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), wp)
            if not r then plat.Parent = nil top = nil return end
            local surface = r.Position.Y
            local feet = hrp.Position.Y - (hum.HipHeight + hrp.Size.Y / 2)
            local g = groundY(hrp.Position, plat)
            if g and g >= surface - 0.6 then plat.Parent = nil top = nil return end
            if feet < surface - 6 then plat.Parent = nil top = nil return end
            if not top then top = math.min(surface, feet - 0.05) end
            top = math.min(surface, math.max(top, math.min(surface, feet - 0.05)) + 0.35)
            plat.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position.X, top - 0.5, hrp.Position.Z)
            plat.Parent = Workspace
        end))
    end

    local alerted, lastHp = {}, 0
    M.Maid:Give(task.spawn(function()
        while not M.Dead do
            pcall(function()
                local now = os.clock()
                if M.AlertOn then
                    for _, e in ipairs(M.enemyBoats(M.AlertRange)) do
                        if not alerted[e.boat] or now - alerted[e.boat] > 30 then
                            alerted[e.boat] = now
                            local p1 = e.players[1]
                            notify("Enemy Boat", ("%s with %d %s player(s) %d studs away"):format(nice(e.boat), #e.players, p1 and p1.Team and p1.Team.Name or "?", e.dist))
                        end
                    end
                end
                if M.HpAlert then
                    local b = M.current()
                    local hp, mhp = b and b:FindFirstChild("Health"), b and b:FindFirstChild("MaxHealth")
                    if hp and mhp and mhp.Value > 0 and hp.Value / mhp.Value * 100 < M.HpPct and now - lastHp > 15 then
                        lastHp = now
                        notify("Boat Damage", ("%s at %d/%d HP"):format(nice(b), hp.Value, mhp.Value))
                    end
                end
                for b in pairs(alerted) do if not b.Parent then alerted[b] = nil end end
            end)
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end))

    function M.InfoText()
        local b, s = M.current()
        local lines = {}
        if b then
            local i = M.info(b)
            local hull = b:FindFirstChild("Hull") or b:FindFirstChild("Base")
            local sp = hull and hull:IsA("BasePart") and Vector3.new(hull.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, hull.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z).Magnitude or 0
            local cfg = b:FindFirstChild("Config")
            local ds = cfg and cfg:FindFirstChild("DriverSpeed")
            local rs = cfg and cfg:FindFirstChild("RowerSpeed")
            lines[#lines+1] = ("%s \194\183 %s \194\183 HP %s/%s"):format(nice(b), s:IsA("VehicleSeat") and "driving" or "rowing", i.hp and math.floor(i.hp) or "?", i.mhp and math.floor(i.mhp) or "?")
            lines[#lines+1] = ("speed %.1f st/s \194\183 seats %d/%d%s"):format(sp, i.used, i.seats,
                (ds and rs) and (" \194\183 top %d + %.1f per rower"):format(ds.Value, rs.Value) or "")
        else
            local c = LocalPlayer.Character
            lines[#lines+1] = (c and c:GetAttribute("RidingAnimal")) and "riding an animal" or "not in a boat"
        end
        lines[#lines+1] = "boat: " .. M.Status
        lines[#lines+1] = "ride: " .. M.RideStatus
        return table.concat(lines, "\n")
    end
    function M.Destroy()
        M.Dead = true
        M.boardGen = M.boardGen + 1
        pcall(M.SetPilot, false)
        pcall(M.SetCruise, false)
        pcall(M.SetWaterWalk, false)
        M.EspOn, M.AnimalEsp = false, false
        pcall(M.RefreshEsp)
        M.Maid:Clean()
    end
    return M
end)()

local function unloadAll()
    local steps = {
        function() Speed.Set(false) end, function() NoSlow.Set(false) end, function() InfJump.Set(false) end,
        function() Fly.Stop() end, function() Noclip.Set(false) end, function() Ghost.Set(false) end,
        function() CFSpeed.Set(false) end, function() CFFly.Stop() end, function() Water.Set(false) end,
        function() Food.Set(false) end, function() Prompts.Set(false) end, function() KeepBackpack.Set(false) end,
        function() AntiGrab.Set(false) end, function() Aim.Set(false) end,
        function() AutoCombat.Kick=false AutoCombat.Shield=false AutoCombat.Set() end, function() AutoWeapon.Set(false) end,
        function() Panic.SetAuto(false) end, function() Panic.SetStay(false) end, function() GhostKill.Set(false) end,
        function() ESP.Set(false) end, function() TownESP.Set(false) end, function() Hitbox.Set(false) end,
        function() RockESP.Set(false) end, function() LootESP.Set(false) end, function() Sapling.SetMouse(false) end,
        function() Sapling.SetAuto(false) end, function() Sapling.SetRing(false) end, function() Merchant.SetAuto(false) end,
        function() CZ.destroy() end, function() MobileCombat.Destroy() end,
        function() ItemESP.Set(false) ItemESP.Dead=true end,
        function() Boats.Destroy() end,
        function() SafePrompt.destroy() end,
        function()
            Gov.conn:Disconnect()
            local wsv = Char.Val("WalkSpeed")
            if wsv and Gov.lastVal and Gov.baseVal then wsv.Value = Gov.baseVal end
            Gov.extra = 0 table.clear(Gov.targets)
        end,
        function() jumpReqConn:Disconnect() end,
        function() window:Destroy() end,
    }
    for _, f in ipairs(steps) do pcall(f) end
end

local function killZombieBlur()
    local gu = (debug and debug.getupvalues) or getupvalues
    if not (getconnections and gu) then return 0 end
    local n = 0
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    for _, sig in ipairs({ RunService.RenderStepped, cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CFrame"), cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"), cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("FieldOfView") }) do
        local okC, list = pcall(getconnections, sig)
        for _, c in ipairs(okC and list or {}) do
            local okU, ups = pcall(gu, c.Function)
            if okU and type(ups) == "table" then
                for _, u in pairs(ups) do
                    local r = type(u) == "table" and rawget(u, "_root")
                    if typeof(r) == "Instance" and r.Parent == nil then
                        pcall(function() c:Disable() end)
                        n = n + 1
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return n
end

window = Library.new("LedZ Civ v3.5", "LedZCivConfigs")
getgenv().__LedZUnload = unloadAll
do
    local g = window.screenGui
    if g then
        pcall(function() g.ResetOnSpawn = false end)
        local safe = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
        pcall(function() g.Parent = safe end)
        g.Enabled = true
        g:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
            if not g.Enabled and g.Parent then g.Enabled = true end
        end)
        g:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
            if g.Parent and g.Parent ~= safe and g.Parent:IsA("PlayerGui") then task.defer(function() pcall(function() g.Parent = safe end) end) end
        end)
    end
end
task.defer(function()
    pcall(function()
        for _, root in ipairs({ LocalPlayer.PlayerGui, (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui") }) do
            for _, g in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
                if g.Name == "Acrylic" and g ~= window.screenGui then g:Destroy() end
            end
        end
    end)
    task.wait(0.5)
    local n = killZombieBlur()
    if n > 0 then print(("[LedZ] stopped %d leftover blur loop(s) from an older copy"):format(n)) end
end)
window:SetToggleKey(Enum.KeyCode.RightControl)
window:Notify({ Title="LedZ Civ v3.5", Description="v0.8.0 Boats update support loaded", Duration=3, Icon="rbxassetid://10709775704" })

Maid.Slice()

local boatPara = BoatTab:CreateParagraph({ Title="Boat", Content="..." })
Maid.Slice()
BoatTab:CreateSection("Board (Raft / Sailboat / Warboat)")
local boardType, boardRole = "Any", "Driver"
BoatTab:CreateDropdown({ Name="Boat Type", Options=Boats.TYPES, Default="Any", Flag="BoatType",

local function playerNames()
    local t = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p ~= LocalPlayer then t[#t+1] = p.Name end end
    table.sort(t)
    if #t == 0 then t = { "(no players)" } end
    return t
end

local JobsTab = WorldSec:CreateTab("Jobs", "rbxassetid://10734898355")
Maid.Slice()
JobsTab:CreateSection("Teams")
JobsTab:CreateButton({ Name="Create My Own Civ", Callback=function() joinTeam(false) end })
local teamByName = {}
local function buildTeamNames()
    teamByName = {}
    local names = {}
    local ok,teams = pcall(function() return TeamsService:GetTeams() end)
    if ok then for _,t in ipairs(teams) do
        local label = string.format("%s [%d]", t.Name, #t:GetPlayers())
        teamByName[label]=t; names[#names+1]=label
    end end
    if #names==0 then names={"(no teams)"} end
    return names
end
local teamNames = buildTeamNames()
local teamDropdown = JobsTab:CreateDropdown({ Name="Join Team / Civ", Options=teamNames, Default=teamNames[1], Flag="TeamPick",
    Callback=function(sel) local t=teamByName[type(sel)=="table" and sel[1] or sel] if t then joinTeam(t) end end })
JobsTab:CreateButton({ Name="Refresh Teams", Callback=function() pcall(function() teamDropdown:Refresh(buildTeamNames()) end) end })
Maid.Slice()
JobsTab:CreateSection("Jobs (auto-hops to board; XP is server-side)")
local jobByName = {}
local function buildJobNames()
    jobByName = {}
    local names = {}
    local jf = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Jobs")
    if jf then
        local list = jf:GetChildren()
        table.sort(list,function(a,b) local av=(a:IsA("ValueBase") and a.Value) or 0 local bv=(b:IsA("ValueBase") and b.Value) or 0 return av<bv end)
        for _,job in ipairs(list) do
            local tier=(job:IsA("ValueBase") and job.Value) or "?"
            local exp=job:FindFirstChild("Exp"); exp=(exp and exp.Value) or "?"
            local label=string.format("%s (T%s, %s XP)",job.Name,tostring(tier),tostring(exp))
            jobByName[label]=job; names[#names+1]=label
        end
    end
    if #names==0 then names={"(no jobs found)"} end
    return names
end
local jobNames = buildJobNames()
local jobDropdown = JobsTab:CreateDropdown({ Name="Pick Job", Options=jobNames, Default=jobNames[1], Flag="JobPick",
    Callback=function(sel) local job=jobByName[type(sel)=="table" and sel[1] or sel] if job then requestJob(job) end end })
JobsTab:CreateButton({ Name="Refresh Job List", Callback=function()
    local names=buildJobNames()
    pcall(function() jobDropdown:Refresh(names) end)
end })
