    while window and not ItemESP.Dead do
        local n = ItemESP.Nearest
        local txt = not ItemESP.Enabled and "Item ESP off"
            or n and ("%s%s \194\183 %dm \194\183 go %s"):format(n.name, n.kind=="spawn" and " (spawn)" or "", math.floor(n.dist), n.dir)
            or "No selected item in range"
        if txt ~= lastTxt then lastTxt = txt pcall(function() itemPara:SetContent(txt) end) end
        task.wait(0.5)
    end
end)

local Perf = { NoBlur = true }
local function applyPerf()
    pcall(function()
        local ab = window._acrylicBlur
        if ab and not ab._lzOrig then
            ab._lzOrig = ab.SetEnabled
            ab.SetEnabled = function(self, en) return ab._lzOrig(self, en and not Perf.NoBlur) end
        end
        if ab then ab:SetEnabled(window._visible ~= false) end
    end)
    local dof = game:GetService("Lighting"):FindFirstChild("AcrylicBlur")
    if dof and dof:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") and Perf.NoBlur then dof.Enabled = false end
end
applyPerf()
Maid.Slice()
MiscTab:CreateSection("Performance")
MiscTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Disable Menu Blur (less lag)", Default=true, Flag="PerfNoBlur", Callback=function(v) Perf.NoBlur=v applyPerf() end })
MiscTab:CreateToggle({ Name="No Shadows (less lag)", Default=false, Flag="PerfNoShadows", Callback=function(v) game:GetService("Lighting").GlobalShadows = not v end })

        task.spawn(function()
            local rep = function(t) S.goalStatus = t end
            CZ.INV.drinkTrip(nil, rep)
            CZ.INV.eatTrip(nil, rep)
            S.goalStatus = "idle"
        end)
    end })
    Maid.Slice()
    GoalTab:CreateSection("Jobs (walks to a job board)")
    local jobSel = CZ.JOB_LIST[1]
    GoalTab:CreateDropdown({ Name="Job", Options=CZ.JOB_LIST, Default=jobSel, Flag="GoalJob",
        Callback=function(sel) jobSel=(type(sel)=="table" and sel[1]) or sel or jobSel end })
    GoalTab:CreateButton({ Name="Walk To Job Board And Switch", Callback=function()
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, why = CZ.ensureJob(jobSel)
            CZ.notify("Job", ok and ("Now "..jobSel) or tostring(why), 4)
        end)
    end })

    local PlantTab = FarmSec:CreateTab("Smart Plant", "rbxassetid://10734898355")
    Maid.Slice()
    PlantTab:CreateSection("Saplings")
    local sapPara = PlantTab:CreateParagraph({ Title="Saplings", Content="..." })
    local function plantAt(pos)
        if not pos then CZ.notify("Plant", "No ground there", 2) return end
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, info = CZ.plant(pos, "Sapling")
            CZ.notify("Plant", ok and "Planted" or ("Failed: "..tostring(info)), 3)
        end)
    end
    PlantTab:CreateKeybind({ Name="Plant At Mouse (PC)", Default=Enum.KeyCode.G, Flag="KbPlant", Callback=function() plantAt(CZ.mouseGround()) end })
    PlantTab:CreateKeybind({ Name="Mark Spot At Mouse (PC)", Default=Enum.KeyCode.H, Flag="KbMark", Callback=function()
        local pos=CZ.mouseGround() if pos then CZ.addMark(pos) end
    end })
    PlantTab:CreateButton({ Name="Plant In Front Of Me", Callback=function() plantAt(CZ.frontGround(6)) end })
    local tapMode = "off"
    PlantTab:CreateDropdown({ Name="Tap The Ground To (mobile)", Options={ "off", "plant sapling", "mark spot" }, Default="off",
        Callback=function(sel) tapMode=(type(sel)=="table" and sel[1]) or sel or "off" end })
    UserInputService.TouchTapInWorld:Connect(function(pos, processed)
        if processed or tapMode == "off" or not CZ.alive() then return end
        local g = CZ.screenGround(pos.X, pos.Y)
        if tapMode == "plant sapling" then plantAt(g) elseif g then CZ.addMark(g) end
    end)
    PlantTab:CreateButton({ Name="Mark Spot In Front Of Me", Callback=function() local pos=CZ.frontGround(6) if pos then CZ.addMark(pos) end end })
    PlantTab:CreateButton({ Name="Plant At All Marked Spots", Callback=function() task.spawn(CZ.plantAtMarks) end })
    PlantTab:CreateButton({ Name="Clear Marks", Callback=function() CZ.clearMarks() end })
    PlantTab:CreateButton({ Name="Plant Every Sapling In A Grid Around Me", Callback=function() task.spawn(CZ.plantGrid) end })
    Maid.Slice()
    PlantTab:CreateSection("Retry Settings")
    PlantTab:CreateSlider({ Name="Spacing (studs)", Min=4, Max=20, Default=8, Flag="PlantSpacing", Callback=function(v) S.spacing=v end })
    PlantTab:CreateSlider({ Name="Retry Step (studs)", Min=1, Max=8, Default=3, Flag="PlantStep", Callback=function(v) S.retryStep=v end })
    PlantTab:CreateSlider({ Name="Max Retry Radius (studs)", Min=3, Max=30, Default=15, Flag="PlantRadius", Callback=function(v) S.retryRadius=v end })
    PlantTab:CreateSlider({ Name="Max Tries Per Sapling", Min=1, Max=30, Default=12, Flag="PlantTries", Callback=function(v) S.maxTries=v end })
    PlantTab:CreateSlider({ Name="Cooldown Between Tries (x0.1s)", Min=5, Max=30, Default=5, Flag="PlantCd", Callback=function(v) S.plantCooldown=v/10 end })

    local TravelTab = FarmSec:CreateTab("Travel", "rbxassetid://10734898355")
    Maid.Slice()
    TravelTab:CreateSection("Smart Walk (pathfinding + climbing)")
    TravelTab:CreateDropdown({ Name="Place", Options=CZ.PLACES, Default=S.place, Flag="TravelPlace",
        Callback=function(sel) S.place=(type(sel)=="table" and sel[1]) or sel or S.place end })
    TravelTab:CreateButton({ Name="Walk To Nearest Place", Callback=function()
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, why = CZ.walkToPlace(S.place)
            CZ.notify("Walk", ok and ("Arrived at "..S.place) or tostring(why), 3)
        end)
    end })
    TravelTab:CreateButton({ Name="Stop Walking", Callback=function() CZ.cancelWalk() end })

    Maid.Slice()
    FightTab:CreateSection("Auto Loot Best Gear (death drops)")
    local lootPara = FightTab:CreateParagraph({ Title="Loot", Content="off" })
    FightTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Auto Grab Best Weapons / Armor / Shields", Default=false, Flag="AutoLoot", Callback=function(v) S.autoLoot=v end })
    FightTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Only Grab Upgrades Over What I Have", Default=true, Flag="LootUpgrades", Callback=function(v) S.lootUpgradesOnly=v end })
    FightTab:CreateSlider({ Name="Grab Range (studs)", Min=5, Max=60, Default=25, Flag="LootRange", Callback=function(v) S.lootRadius=v end })
    FightTab:CreateSlider({ Name="Extra Hold On Top Of The Real Hold (x0.1s)", Min=0, Max=6, Default=1, Flag="LootHold", Callback=function(v) S.lootHold=v/10 end })

    Maid.Slice()
    MoveTab:CreateSection("Calibrated CFrame Speed (auto, used by the farm)")
    local speedPara = MoveTab:CreateParagraph({ Title="Calibrated Speed", Content="..." })
    MoveTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Calibrated Speed (pauses while other speed/fly is on)", Default=true, Flag="CalSpeed", Callback=function(v) S.speed=v end })
    local speedSlider = MoveTab:CreateSlider({ Name="Extra Speed (studs/s, above ~10 gets flagged)", Min=0, Max=20, Default=S.speedExtra, Callback=function(v) S.speedExtra=v end })
    MoveTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Auto-Calibrate On Load", Default=true, Flag="CalAuto", Callback=function(v) S.autoCalibrate=v end })
    MoveTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Lower Speed On Server Snapback", Default=true, Flag="CalAdaptive", Callback=function(v) S.adaptive=v end })
    MoveTab:CreateButton({ Name="Calibrate Now (stand in the open)", Callback=function() task.spawn(CZ.calibrate) end })
    CZ.onSpeed = function(v) pcall(function() speedSlider:SetValue(v) end) end
    if S.speedExtra ~= 10 then CZ.onSpeed(S.speedExtra) end

    Maid.Slice()
    VisTab:CreateSection("Resource ESP")
    VisTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Resource ESP", Default=false, Flag="ResESPOn", Callback=function(v) S.espOn=v end })
    VisTab:CreateDropdown({ Name="Show Resources", Options=CZ.FARMABLE, Default={ "Copper", "Tin", "Iron", "Gold", "Marble" }, MultiSelect=true, Flag="ResESP",
        Callback=function(list) table.clear(S.esp) for _,o in ipairs(list) do S.esp[o]=true end end })
    VisTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Planted Saplings", Default=false, Flag="ResESPSap", Callback=function(v) S.espSaplings=v end })
    VisTab:CreateSlider({ Name="Resource ESP Range", Min=100, Max=2000, Default=600, Flag="ResESPRange", Callback=function(v) S.espRange=v end })
    VisTab:CreateSlider({ Name="Max Labels", Min=5, Max=80, Default=40, Flag="ResESPMax", Callback=function(v) S.espMax=v end })
    Maid.Slice()
    VisTab:CreateSection("World")
    VisTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Fullbright", Default=false, Flag="Fullbright", Callback=function(v) S.fullbright=v if not v then CZ.restoreLighting("fullbright") end end })
    VisTab:CreateToggle({ Name="No Fog", Default=false, Flag="NoFog", Callback=function(v) S.noFog=v if not v then CZ.restoreLighting("fog") end end })
    VisTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Hide Hunger Grayscale", Default=false, Flag="NoHungerFx", Callback=function(v) CZ.setConfig("HungerGrayScaleEffect", not v) end })
    VisTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Hide Thirst Blur", Default=false, Flag="NoThirstFx", Callback=function(v) CZ.setConfig("ThirstBlurEffect", not v) end })
    VisTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Custom FOV", Default=false, Flag="FovOn", Callback=function(v) S.fovOn=v if not v then Camera.FieldOfView=70 end end })
    VisTab:CreateSlider({ Name="FOV", Min=60, Max=120, Default=90, Flag="Fov", Callback=function(v) S.fov=v end })

    MiscTab:CreateToggle({ Name="Anti-AFK", Default=true, Flag="AntiAfk", Callback=function(v) S.antiAfk=v end })

    getgenv().__LedZ = { farm = CZ, mobile = MobileCombat, items = ItemESP, unload = unloadAll, boats = Boats, prompt = SafePrompt,
        mods = { Speed = Speed, CFSpeed = CFSpeed, Ghost = Ghost, Fly = Fly, CFFly = CFFly } }

    task.spawn(function()
        local last = {}
        local function put(para, text)
            if last[para] ~= text then
                last[para] = text
                pcall(function() para:SetContent(text) end)
            end
        end
        local tick = 0
        while CZ.alive() do
            tick = tick + 1
            pcall(function()
                put(farmPara, S.status)
                local h = CZ.INV.hist or {}
                put(goalPara, S.goalStatus .. (#h > 1 and ("\nrecent:\n" .. table.concat(h, "\n", math.max(1, #h - 4), #h - 1)) or ""))
                put(buildPara, B.statusText())
                if tick % 2 == 0 then
                    local hum = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    put(speedPara, ("+%d studs/s%s%s\nnow: %s \194\183 walk speed %.1f"):format(S.speedExtra, CZ.isCalibrating() and " (calibrating...)" or "",
                        S.calibrated and " \194\183 calibrated" or "", tostring(S.boostWhy or "-"), hum and hum.WalkSpeed or 0))
                end
                if tick % 3 == 0 then
                    put(hallPara, CZ.hallStatus())
                    local best = CZ.LootGrab.myBest()
                    put(lootPara, ("%s\nyour best: weapon %s \194\183 chest %s \194\183 helmet %s \194\183 shield %s"):format(CZ.LootGrab.status,
                        best.weapon or "-", best.chest or "-", best.helmet or "-", best.shield or "-"))
                    put(sapPara, ("%d in inventory \194\183 %d marked spots"):format(CZ.countItem("Sapling"), CZ.markCount()))
                end
            end)
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end)
end
end
LedZBuildUI()
